### [23] [Cost-Effective Automated Judging of Natural-Language Mathematical Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00004)
*Benjamin Grayzel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究是否能用廉价的开源小模型替代昂贵的顶级闭源模型作为数学证明分级评判者。在200实例的验证样本中，三个廉价评判者（GPT-OSS 120B、DeepSeek-V4 Flash、Gemma-4 31B）与人类评判的一致性在统计上与Claude Opus 4.7和Gemini 3.1 Pro无显著差异，但成本降低高达100倍。在完整的1000实例基准上，要求三模型全部通过的共识规则（all-three-pass）达到了最高的通过一致性和精确度，且运行波动最小。核心发现是廉价评判者在成本低1-2个数量级的情况下可与前沿模型竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言数学证明的分级是评估数学推理系统的经常性成本，前沿LLM评判者（如Claude Opus、Gemini Pro）价格昂贵。研究者希望探索是否可以用便宜的开放权重模型替代昂贵的评判者，在不牺牲分级质量的前提下大幅降低成本。

Method: 使用三个廉价开放权重模型（GPT-OSS 120B、DeepSeek-V4 Flash、Gemma-4 31B）作为评判者，在IMO-GradingBench的200实例验证样本上评测，与人类通过/失败判定的一致性作为指标，并与Claude Opus 4.7和Gemini 3.1 Pro对比。随后扩展到完整的1000实例基准，探索不同的共识规则（包括多数投票和全员一致）对分级效果的影响，并进行四次重复实验评估稳定性。

Result: 三个廉价评判者与人类判定的一致性在统计上与两个前沿模型无显著差异，成本降低最高100倍。多数投票匹配前沿模型但未超越其最强成员。全员一致规则（all-three-pass）在完整基准上达到最高通过一致性和精确度，且在四次重复实验中运行波动最小。

Conclusion: 廉价开放权重模型可以作为可靠的数学证明分级评判者，在成本降低1-2个数量级的条件下达到与前沿模型相当的性能。推荐部署all-three-pass（三模型全员一致通过）作为默认规则，但需注意该规则是事后识别的，需独立复制验证其稳健性。

Abstract: Grading natural-language mathematical proofs is a recurring cost in evaluating math-reasoning systems, and frontier LLM judges are expensive. We ask whether cheap open-weight models can serve as reliable judges given a candidate proof, a ground-truth proof, and a human-grading rubric. On a 200-instance validation sample of IMO-GradingBench, three cheap judges (GPT-OSS 120B, DeepSeek-V4 Flash, Gemma-4 31B) agree with human pass/fail decisions at rates statistically indistinguishable from Claude Opus 4.7 and Gemini 3.1 Pro, at up to $100\times$ lower cost. We had expected a majority vote of the three to be the best budget option; it matched the frontier but did not improve on its strongest member. Extending to the full 1000-instance benchmark and exploring consensus rules, we found that requiring unanimous agreement (all-three-pass) reaches the highest pass-agreement and precision and, on four replicate runs, the smallest run-to-run spread. The headline finding is that cheap judges are competitive with the frontier at one to two orders of magnitude lower cost; as a deployable default we recommend all-three-pass, with the caveat that this rule was identified post-hoc and warrants independent replication.

</details>


### [24] [RubricReviewer: From Direct Critique to Objective and Comprehensive Rubric-Driven Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00005)
*Shuyu Guo,Wenxiang Hu,Yuyue Zhao,Yougang Lyu,Xiaohui Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RubricReviewer，一个完全以评分标准（rubric）为驱动的LLM审稿框架，通过将评分标准生成作为显式中间步骤，并结合训练无关的Scout智能体（收集外部证据）与训练对齐的Aligner模型（消费证据），解决现有LLM审稿人存在的两大结构性局限：评分标准隐式化以及训练/非训练范式各自只捕获部分审稿能力的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主要学术场所的同行评审面临前所未有的投稿压力，促使使用LLM作为评审助手。但现有LLM评审者存在两个结构性局限：一是将稿件直接映射到评审，评分标准隐式化，导致评分标准的推导与评判纠缠在一起；二是现有范式各自只捕获优秀评审的一半能力——训练无关智能体收集广泛证据但产生无针对性评论，而基于训练的评审者继承了人类判别判断同时带有噪音和不均匀覆盖。

Method: RubricReviewer框架包含两个核心创新：(1) 将评分标准生成作为显式中间步骤，使评审生成和最终评估都基于论文自适应的评分标准；(2) 结合训练无关的Scout智能体（收集外部证据）与人类对齐的Aligner训练模型（消费这些证据），融合两种监督来源的优势。

Result: 在真实世界投稿上的实验表明，RubricReviewer产生的评审比先前系统更加全面且更具区分度，并且对对抗性提示注入攻击表现出最强的鲁棒性。消融研究进一步确认了每个组件的必要性。

Conclusion: 通过显式的评分标准生成步骤以及训练/非训练双范式融合，RubricReviewer有效克服了现有LLM评审者的结构性局限，在评审质量、判别能力和鲁棒性方面均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Peer review at major venues is under unprecedented submission pressure, motivating the use of large language models (LLMs) as review assistants. Existing LLM-based reviewers, however, face two structural limitations. First, they map manuscripts directly to reviews, leaving the underlying rubric implicit and entangling its derivation with the judgement. Second, the prevailing paradigms each capture only half of a good review: training-free agents gather broad evidence but produce undirected critiques, while training-based reviewers inherit human discriminative judgement together with its noise and uneven coverage. We introduce RubricReviewer, a fully rubric-driven framework that addresses both limitations. It makes rubric generation an explicit intermediate step, so that both review generation and the final assessment are conditioned on paper-adaptive rubrics. It further combines a training-free agent (Scout) that gathers external evidence with a human-aligned trained model (Aligner) that consumes this evidence, fusing the strengths of both supervision sources. Experiments on real-world submissions show that RubricReviewer produces reviews that are markedly more comprehensive and more discriminative than prior systems, and exhibits the strongest robustness against adversarial prompt-injection attacks. Ablation studies further confirm the necessity of each component.

</details>


### [25] [MemoryForge: Synthesize Lifelong Memory for Human-Like LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00007)
*Bohan Tang,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MemoryForge 框架，用基于记忆的条件化（memory-based conditioning）替代传统的基于提示词描述的条件化方法，为冻结的大型语言模型合成自传体式终身记忆，使其在角色扮演和用户模拟等智能体应用中表现出更类人的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于提示词的方法通过注入静态文本档案来赋予LLM人设，但这种方式缺乏现实生活记忆，往往导致智能体表现出泛化、不真实的行为。作者受认知心理学启发，提出用自传体记忆替代抽象档案，以弥补这一不足。

Method: 提出 MemoryForge 框架，包含三个关键组件：1）上下文生成器，用于社会历史背景锚定；2）生活组织器，用于实现向目标身份的发展连贯性；3）多分辨率模拟器，在广域时间摘要与高保真情景经验之间取得平衡。该框架从简短的目标准入信息中合成定制化的终身记忆。

Result: 在 PersonaGym（角色扮演）和 SimulatorArena（用户模拟）上的实验表明，由 MemoryForge 合成的记忆库能使冻结的LLM在多个指标和多种LLM骨干网络上表现出比强描述性条件化基线更类人的行为。

Conclusion: 基于记忆的条件化范式优于传统的基于描述的条件化方法，为提升LLM智能体的人性化表现提供了一条新路径，具有广阔的应用前景。

Abstract: Equipping Large Language Models (LLMs) with human-like personas is crucial for agentic applications, such as role-play and user simulation. Traditional prompt-based methods rely on descriptive conditioning by injecting static textual profiles, which often makes agents show generic behaviors due to a lack of realistic life memory. To fill this gap, we introduce memory-based conditioning, a paradigm inspired by the cognitive psychology, which replaces abstract profiles with an autobiographical memory base, enabling frozen LLMs to dynamically retrieve situation-relevant memory to guide their behaviors. We formalize its enabling task as customized lifelong memory synthesis and propose MemoryForge, a novel framework to synthesize such lifelong memory from brief target personas. MemoryForge has three key components: a context generator for socio-historical grounding, a life organizer for developmental coherence toward the target identity, and a multi-resolution simulator that balances broad temporal summaries with high-fidelity episodic experiences. Experiments on PersonaGym for role-play and SimulatorArena for user-simulation, show that the synthesized memory base by MemoryForge enables frozen LLMs to exhibit more human-like behaviors than strong descriptive conditioning baselines across multiple metrics and LLM backbones.

</details>


### [26] [AgentMemBench: A Systematic Benchmark for Evaluating Long-Term Memory Management Strategies in Conversational AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00009)
*Ahmed Cherif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentMemBench，一个统一的、可复现的基准，在相同条件下评估五种记忆管理策略（ICW、EKV、GEM、CBS、WAM），横跨三个公开数据集和491个标注问题。结果显示EKV在所有质量指标上领先（宏观Recall@5为0.792），尤其在长程回忆任务中表现决定性优势，但代价是更高的内存占用（约5,100 vs 300 tokens）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话AI智能体面临长期记忆的瓶颈，有限上下文窗口无法支持跨数千轮对话的连贯回忆。现有研究缺乏在相同条件下对多种记忆管理策略进行统一比较的基准。

Method: 论文构建了统一可复现的基准AgentMemBench，在相同条件下评估五种记忆管理策略：上下文窗口化（ICW）、外部键值存储（EKV）、图基情景记忆（GEM）、压缩摘要（CBS）和网络增强记忆（WAM）。使用三个公共数据集，采用Recall@k、MRR、nDCG@k、Answer F1、LLM-judge Faithfulness、Memory Footprint和Latency七个指标，在491个标注问题轮次上评估，生成和评判均使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct（4-bit）和贪婪解码确保确定性。

Result: (1) EKV在所有质量指标上占优（Recall@5 0.792, MRR 0.677, F1 0.156, Faithfulness 0.354）；(2) 长程回忆是决定性因素：在LoCoMo中，ICW、WAM、GEM和CBS几乎无法检索（Recall@5≤0.005），而EKV达到0.573；(3) CBS在检索方面排名第二（0.556）；(4) WAM在语料内回忆上因架构设计等同于ICW；(5) EKV的回忆优势伴随约5,100 token的高内存占用，相对ICW/WAM的约300 token，存在显式的精度-效率权衡。还评估了MemGPT/Letta和HippoRAG两个已有系统。

Conclusion: 核心发现是只有密集检索（EKV）能够在长时域上扩展，而近期窗口、摘要和实体图在长时域上都会崩溃。EKV的召回优势需要付出显著的内存成本，存在明确的准确率-效率权衡。所有代码、环境和结果已发布以保证可复现性。

Abstract: Long-term memory remains a critical bottleneck for conversational AI agents, whose finite context windows cannot support coherent recall across thousands of turns. We present AgentMemBench, a unified, reproducible benchmark evaluating five memory management strategies under identical conditions: in-context windowing (ICW), external key-value store (EKV), graph-based episodic memory (GEM), compression-based summarisation (CBS), and web-augmented memory (WAM). All are assessed across three public datasets covering long-term multi-session dialogue (LoCoMo), task-oriented document grounding (MultiDoc2Dial), and persona-grounded multi-session chat (MSC), using Recall@k, MRR, nDCG@k, Answer F1, an LLM-judge Faithfulness score, Memory Footprint, and Latency over 491 annotated question turns. Generation and judging both use Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct (4-bit), with greedy decoding for determinism. Our results show that (1) EKV dominates on every quality axis (macro Recall@5 0.792, MRR 0.677, F1 0.156, Faithfulness 0.354); (2) long-range recall is decisive: on LoCoMo, where the gold turn lies many sessions back, ICW, WAM, GEM, and CBS retrieve almost nothing (Recall@5 <= 0.005) while EKV alone reaches 0.573, showing that recency windows, summaries, and entity graphs collapse at long horizons and only dense retrieval scales; (3) CBS is the runner-up on retrieval (0.556); (4) WAM equals ICW on in-corpus recall by construction, since external results carry no in-corpus provenance; and (5) EKV's recall advantage carries a footprint cost (~5,100 vs ~300 tokens for ICW/WAM), an explicit accuracy-efficiency trade-off. We additionally evaluate two published memory systems (MemGPT/Letta, HippoRAG) against the same harness, and release all code, environment, and result artefacts for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [27] [DLLM-TTS: Block Discrete Diffusion Language Model for Text-to-Speech Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00011)
*Wasim Madha,Nityanand Mathur,Hamees Sayed,Apoorv Singh,Sameer Khurana,Akshat Mandloi,Sudarshan Kamath*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DLLM-TTS是一个将文本转语音（TTS）建模为条件块离散扩散的框架，基于X-Codec2神经音频编解码器令牌。该模型将序列分解为块，在块内应用掩码扩散，同时顺序处理块，同时学习局部声学连贯性和全局文本语音对齐。推理时块内并行令牌预测实现高效生成，实时因子（RTF）为0.15。0.6B参数模型在20K小时数据上训练，在Seed-TTS-eval基准上取得了有竞争力的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本转语音系统面临权衡：自回归编解码器语言模型能产生高度可理解的语音，但需要大规模模型和训练数据且需顺序解码；非自回归方法虽提高了速度，但牺牲了语言准确性。作者旨在解决这一速度与质量之间的两难问题。

Method: 将TTS公式化为X-Codec2神经音频编解码器令牌上的条件块离散扩散。模型将序列分解为块，在每个块内应用掩码扩散，同时按顺序处理各块，从而同时捕捉局部声学连贯性和全局文本-语音对齐。推理过程中，块内采用并行令牌预测以提升效率。

Result: 在Seed-TTS-eval基准上，0.6B参数模型仅用20K小时训练数据即达到有竞争力的性能，推理实时因子（RTF）为0.15，证明了块离散扩散语言模型在并行生成的同时具备实用性和数据效率。

Conclusion: 块离散扩散语言模型能够在保持高效并行生成能力的同时，实现实用且数据高效的语音合成，有效弥合了自回归方法的高质量和非自回归方法的高速度之间的差距。

Abstract: Current text-to-speech systems face a trade-off: autoregres- sive codec language models produce highly intelligible speech but require large-scale models and training data and decode tokens sequentially, while non-autoregressive approaches im- prove speed at the cost of linguistic accuracy. We present DLLM-TTS, a framework that formulates TTS as conditional block discrete diffusion over X-Codec2 neural audio codec to- kens. The model decomposes sequences into blocks and applies masked diffusion within each block while processing blocks se- quentially, learning both local acoustic coherence and global text-speech alignment. During inference, parallel token pre- diction within blocks enables efficient generation with a real- time factor (RTF) of 0.15. A 0.6B-parameter model trained on 20K hours achieves competitive performance on the Seed- TTS-eval benchmark, demonstrating that block discrete diffu- sion language models enable practical and data-efficient speech synthesis with parallel generation.

</details>


### [28] [Obshazard-bench: Benchmarking Multimodal Foundation Models for Real-Time Disaster Intelligence from Raw Earth Observation Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00012)
*Fengxiang Wang,Qiuyang Yu,Yueying Li,Mingshuo Chen,Chengchi Fei,Kaiyi Xu,Lixin Gu,Wangxu Wei,Junchao Gong,Lipeng Ma,Jiong Wang,Fenghua Ling,Wenlong Zhang,Xue Yang,Wenjing Yang,Ben Fei,Long Lan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Obshazard-bench，一个面向多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的实时观测驱动型灾害智能评估基准，直接整合原始高频卫星探测流、地面站观测、历史灾害记录和社会经济指标，覆盖8大类28小类灾害、60多个国家和120多个极端事件案例，并定义了与运营灾害工作流对齐的三阶段评估框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感基准大多依赖静态、事后且经过专家处理的产品（如网格化再分析数据），难以与灾害快速演变和严格时间约束下的实际应急场景对齐，导致MLLMs在真实灾害应急响应中的能力评估不足。

Method: 构建Obshazard-bench基准，绕过延迟的专家处理和物理反演管线，直接整合多源卫星原始高频率探测流与地面观测、历史灾害记录和社会经济指标；定义三阶段评估体系：预测性危机预判（灾前）、主动演变推理（灾中）和多维影响量化（灾后）；包含数千个生命周期导向的VQA样本。

Result: 对代表性通用型和地球聚焦型基础模型的实验表明，这些模型在将原始多通道物理观测转化为时间上有依据且与决策相关的灾害推理方面存在显著局限。

Conclusion: Obshazard-bench揭示了当前MLLMs在真实灾害应急场景中的能力短板，为构建具备实时灾害智能的AI系统提供了新的评估标准和发展方向。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to interpret Earth observation data, yet their capability to support real-world disaster emergency response remains insufficiently evaluated. Existing remote sensing benchmarks largely rely on static, post-hoc, and expert-processed products, such as gridded reanalysis data, which are difficult to align with operational disaster scenarios where hazards evolve rapidly and decisions must be made under strict time constraints. To bridge this gap, we introduce Obshazard-bench, a real-time, observation-driven benchmark for evaluating disaster intelligence in MLLMs. Unlike image-centric or post-event benchmarks, Obshazard-bench directly integrates raw, high-frequency satellite sounding streams from diverse satellite sensors with concurrent ground-station observations, historical disaster records, and socio-economic indicators, bypassing delayed expert-processing and physical-inversion pipelines. The benchmark covers 8 major disaster categories and 28 sub-categories across more than 60 countries, incorporating over 120 historically documented extreme-event cases and thousands of lifecycle-oriented VQA samples. Moreover, Obshazard-bench further defines a three-stage evaluation taxonomy aligned with the operational disaster workflow: Predictive Crisis Anticipation for pre-disaster risk detection and early forecasting, Active Evolution Reasoning for in-situ disaster tracking and termination prediction, and Multi-faceted Impact Quantification for post-disaster magnitude deduction, humanitarian burden estimation, and socio-economic impact assessment. Experiments on representative general-purpose and Earth-focused foundation models reveal substantial limitations in transforming raw multi-channel physical observations into temporally grounded and decision-relevant disaster reasoning.

</details>


### [29] [What Transfers from Text to Vision? Capability Scaling Laws and Transfer Dynamics for VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00013)
*Ziran Li,Qiang Wang,Zhengyu Chen,Shanglin Lei,Borun Chen,Jingang Wang,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跨模型家族的"能力驱动的多模态缩放定律"，通过从LLM文本基准测试中提取的低维能力评分，直接预测VLM在训练前的基准准确率，将骨干模型选择从经验性搜索转变为定量决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 选择合适的LLM骨干是构建VLM时最重要的决策，但目前缺乏原则性框架：基于计算量的缩放定律无法跨模型家族泛化，也没有框架能在训练开始前直接预测VLM性能。

Method: 从LLM文本基准测试中通过PCA提取低维能力评分S，将VLM性能建模为S的函数，包含每个骨干的迁移率和量化数据缩放效率的吸收率。在34个LLM（7个模型家族）上训练了150多个VLM，并进行严格控制的实验。

Result: 该定律能从8B参数模型外推到72B规模的骨干，高保真预测完整VLM训练轨迹，并泛化到完全未见的模型家族。同时揭示了一些具有启发性的发现：某些文本基准与多模态性能负相关（暴露基准博弈行为）；基础LLM优于指令调优版本作为VLM骨干；不同模型家族在迁移-吸收空间中占据不同位置。

Conclusion: 该框架将骨干模型选择从昂贵经验性搜索转变为有原则的定量决策，为VLM构建提供了可操作的指导。

Abstract: Choosing the right large language model (LLM) backbone is the most consequential decision when building a vision-language model (VLM), yet it remains fundamentally unprincipled: compute-based scaling laws fail to generalize across model families, and no framework exists for directly predicting VLM performance before training begins. We propose the Capability-Driven Multimodal Scaling Law, the first cross-family framework that predicts VLM benchmark accuracy from directly observable textual capability. Given a low-dimensional capability score $S$ extracted from LLM textual benchmarks via PCA, we model VLM performance as a function of $S$, with a per-backbone transfer rate and an absorption rate that quantifies data-scaling efficiency. To fit and validate the framework, we train over 150 VLMs on 34 LLMs spanning 7 model families under a strictly controlled recipe. Evaluations on more than 200 textual and 50 multimodal benchmarks show that the law accurately extrapolates transfer rate from models up to 8B parameters to 72B-scale backbones, predicts full VLM training trajectories with high fidelity, and generalizes to entirely held-out model families. Beyond the scaling law, our analysis surfaces actionable insights: certain textual benchmarks negatively correlate with multimodal performance, exposing latent benchmark-gaming behavior; base LLMs outperform instruction-tuned counterparts as VLM backbones due to higher absorption rates and lower data-scaling decay; and different model families occupy distinct positions in the transfer--absorption space. The framework turns backbone selection from costly empirical sweeps into a principled, quantitative decision. Code and data are available at https://github.com/wangq-dev/CDMScaling.

</details>


### [30] [Role Steering of Language Models for Social Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00023)
*Isaac Song,Mohammed Rehan Parwani,Glenn Matlin,Emile Anand,Akhil Theerthala,Arjun Chatterjee,Maria Kostylew,Yonadav G. Shavit,Sebastien Krier,Mark Riedl*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于激活引导（activation-steering）的角色条件行为筛选工作流，用于在将语言模型智能体部署到社会模拟之前验证其角色扮演行为。通过在OLMo-3-7B-Instruct上对275个角色进行测试，发现角色特定方向比传统的assistant-axis控制获得更高的角色档案对齐分数（63.2 vs 41.1），且能保持词汇多样性。关键发现是大多数角色随引导强度增加而改善，但有38个角色在所有维度上退化，因此应针对每个角色单独选择引导系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会模拟中基于语言模型的智能体需要在部署前验证其角色条件行为。现有方法缺乏一种系统化的筛选工作流来预先评估角色扮演质量，且统一的引导强度设置可能导致部分角色退化。

Method: 提出四步激活引导筛选工作流：（1）定义角色档案；（2）提取角色特定方向；（3）扫描四个引导系数；（4）评估角色档案对齐度并决定通过或标记。使用OLMo-3-7B-Instruct，基于275个角色和228个角色无关问题，GPT-4.1-mini提供角色参考和评判。

Result: 角色特定方向的平均总体得分为63.2，显著高于assistant-axis控制方向的41.1；角色特定方向保持高词汇多样性而控制方向在大系数下急剧下降；大多数角色随引导强度增加而改善，但38个角色在所有六个维度上均下降。

Conclusion: 激活引导的角色特定方向优于先前的persona-vector工作；模拟构建者应根据每个角色的具体情况选择引导系数，而非对所有角色统一使用高强度设置；角色级筛选是该工作流的主要实用输出。

Abstract: Social simulations built from language-model agents need role-conditioned behavior that can be checked before agents are placed into a simulated population. We introduce an activation-steering screening workflow for role-conditioned agents: define a role profile, extract a role-specific direction, sweep four steering coefficients, evaluate role-profile alignment, and pass or flag each candidate configuration. On OLMo-3-7B-Instruct, we apply the workflow to a mixed 275-role inventory with 228 role-agnostic questions, GPT-4.1-mini prompted role references, and GPT-4.1-mini judges. Role-specific directions receive higher judged role-profile alignment than an assistant-axis directional control from prior persona-vector work, with mean overall scores of 63.2 versus 41.1 across the tested grid. They also preserve high lexical diversity, while the control drops sharply at larger coefficients. The role-level screen is the main practical output: most roles improve as steering increases, but 38 roles decline across all six measured dimensions, showing why simulation builders should choose coefficients per role rather than deploy a uniform high-strength setting. We make our code and evaluation artifacts available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/anonymous-research-code-5F03/.

</details>


### [31] [Cross-Task Dissociation in Frontier Vision-Language Model Theory of Mind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00261)
*Kejia Zhang,Youran Sun,Chugang Yi,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了九种前沿视觉语言模型（VLMs）在两个心理学基准任务（Keysar导演任务和Frith-Happé动画三角形任务）上的心智理论（ToM）表现，发现模型中不存在跨任务一致的人类参考特征，其ToM画像在不同范式间呈现碎片化特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究前沿视觉语言模型是否表现出连贯的心智理论（ToM）画像，即在不同任务中是否与同一人类参考群体匹配，还是其表现会随任务范式不同而碎片化。

Method: 对九个前沿VLM模型在两个心理学基准任务上进行评估：1）Keysar导演任务（测视觉观点采择和以自我为中心干扰）；2）Frith-Happé动画三角形任务（用Castelli评分体系测纯运动意图归因）。

Result: 在导演任务中，无思维链时模型面板在78%的试验中像儿童一样犯以自我为中心的错误；在三角形任务中，模型的意图归因不足，其ToM画像更接近高功能自闭症成人（HF-ASD）均值而非典型发展成人（TD）均值。没有模型在两个任务上都最接近TD。

Conclusion: VLM的ToM能力在不同任务间不连贯，模型面板呈现碎片化的ToM画像。该研究提供的是群体层面的描述，而非对任何模型的诊断标签。

Abstract: Do frontier vision-language models present a coherent Theory-of-Mind (ToM) profile across tasks, matching the same human reference group, or does that profile fragment from one paradigm to the next? We evaluate a shared panel of nine frontier VLMs on two psychology-derived benchmarks: the Keysar Director Task (visual perspective-taking under egocentric interference) and the Frith-Happé animated triangles scored with the Castelli rubric (intention attribution from pure motion). On the Director Task, without chain-of-thought, the panel makes the egocentric error on 78\% of trials like children rather than adults; variation is substantial across models, and reasoning rescues several models. On the triangles, the panel under-attributes intention: its ToM profile sits more than three times closer to the high-functioning-autistic-adult (HF-ASD) mean than to the typical-development-adult (TD) mean, while Goal-Directed and Random stay near TD. No model is nearest TD on both tasks; the model that looks adult-like on the Director Task falls on the HF-ASD side on the triangles, and the most TD-like model on the triangles is child-like on the Director Task. We report group-level descriptions, not diagnostic labels for any model.

</details>


### [32] [Exploring More to Solve More: Boosting Diversity in Text Diffusion Models via Entropy-Based Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00024)
*Jingwei Zhang,Haoyu Lei,Zijin Feng,Jiacheng Sun,Farzan Farnia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的语义感知核熵（SAKE）引导方法，用于文本扩散模型，通过计算核Gram矩阵上的二阶Rényi熵来动态调整采样分布，在保真度和多样性之间取得更优的Pareto平衡，并在代码和数学生成等推理密集型任务上提升了多样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本扩散模型采样策略和引导方法仅调整token似然，未能捕捉更广泛的语义景观，导致保真度与多样性之间的平衡不理想。

Method: 提出SAKE引导方法：计算捕捉跨token语义交互和相对位置信息的核Gram矩阵上的二阶Rényi熵，在嵌入空间中线性化该目标，得到可解的引导信号，从而动态调整采样分布——在冗余时展平分布以鼓励探索，在多样时锐化分布以提升保真度。

Result: 实验表明SAKE在保真度与多样性之间实现了更优的Pareto前沿，并在代码生成和数学生成等推理密集型任务上，相比温度缩放和离散引导基线，显著提升了多样本（multi-sample）性能。

Conclusion: SAKE作为一种训练免费的引导方法，通过语义感知的熵调控有效改善了文本扩散模型的生成质量，为离散序列生成中的可控引导提供了新思路。

Abstract: Although diffusion models have revolutionized continuous domains like image synthesis through high quality generations and controllable guidance mechanisms, bringing this controllability to the discrete, sequential nature of text remains an open challenge. Meanwhile, current sampling strategies and guidance methods adjust token likelihoods without capturing the broader semantic landscape, leading to a suboptimal balance between fidelity and diversity. In this work, we introduce a novel training-free Semantic-Aware Kernel Entropy (SAKE) guidance method. Our method computes the order-2 Rényi entropy over a kernel Gram matrix that captures both cross-token semantic interactions and relative token positions. By linearizing this objective in the embedding space, we derive a tractable guidance signal that dynamically adjusts the sampling distribution, flattening it to encourage exploration during redundancy and sharpening it for fidelity when diverse. Empirical experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves a superior Pareto frontier between fidelity and diversity, and improves multi-sample performance on reasoning-intensive tasks, such as code and mathematics generation, compared to temperature scaling and discrete guidance baselines.

</details>


### [33] [SLMs as Multi-Agent Routers: A Progressive SFT and Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00030)
*Gayathri V Kondapalli,Alexander Ng,Hirsh Pithadia,Rahul Monish,Harvey Yorke,Amir Kayhani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过监督微调结合强化学习训练一个小语言模型，实现智能体选择和结构化参数生成，利用基于检索相关性和主题对齐的分层奖励函数，解决了仅基于意图路由的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法基于查询的推断主题或意图来选择检索智能体，但这种基于意图的选择存在根本局限：无法利用检索内容中的信号，也无法检测到主题对齐的智能体产出低相关结果的情况。

Method: 训练一个小语言模型，先用监督微调再通过强化学习，联合执行智能体选择和下游工具调用的结构化参数生成。使用分层奖励函数，该函数基于检索相关性和查询-智能体主题对齐，让模型从检索性能中学习任务相关的智能体适用性。

Result: 在有针对性的智能体-查询不匹配子集上，模型NDCG@10达0.918，对比两个仅基于意图路由的LLM基线（Amazon Nova Lite和Claude Haiku 4.5）的0.539和0.490。整体平均NDCG@10为0.771（比Nova Lite高0.177，比Haiku高0.219），平均选择延迟120.1ms，比Nova Lite降低82.4%。

Conclusion: 利用检索反馈信号的强化学习方法能显著提升智能体选择的准确性，尤其在处理表面的主题重叠但实际检索效果不佳的查询重定向场景中表现突出，同时大幅降低了延迟。

Abstract: Specialised retrieval agents typically surface higher quality results than general-purpose search, but selecting the optimal agent for a given query remains an open problem. Current approaches route queries based on inferred topic or intent, however intent-based selection is fundamentally limited: it does not incorporate signal from retrieved content, and cannot detect when a topically aligned agent produces low-relevance results. We address this by training a small language model via supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning to jointly perform agent selection and structured parameter generation for downstream tool calls, using a hierarchical reward function grounded in retrieval relevance along with query-agent topic alignment. This enables the model to learn task-dependent agent suitability from retrieval performance: which agents reliably yield high-relevance results for which query distributions, and when to redirect queries away from specialised agents despite surface-level topical overlap. On a targeted subset of such agent-query mismatches, the trained model achieves an NDCG@10 of 0.918 compared to 0.539 and 0.490 for two LLM baselines (Amazon Nova Lite and Claude Haiku 4.5) that route on intent alone. Overall, it achieves a mean NDCG@10 of 0.771 (+0.177 over Nova Lite, +0.219 over Haiku) with a mean selection latency of 120.1ms, an 82.4% reduction over Nova Lite.

</details>


### [34] [XL-DocBench: Benchmarking Evidence-Grounded Extra-Long Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00036)
*Hongchen Wei,Yuanzhe Wang,Bei Liu,Yifan Yang,Qi Dai,Ruichun Ma,Kai Qiu,Yunsheng Li,Dongdong Chen,Chong Luo,Zhenzhong Chen,Baining Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了XL-DocBench，一个完全人工验证的超长文档理解基准，包含1,519个来自六个专业领域的问题，上下文最长可达2,303页，其中72.6%的问题需要多页证据，旨在填补现有基准在长上下文、多页证据和结构化推理方面的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的专业文档任务（如年报、法规、临床指南等）通常需要跨数百或数千页进行问答，有些还需要跨文档比较。然而现有基准大多只评测短上下文或单页QA，无法满足合规、临床、金融和工程等领域对可溯源证据和低错误成本的高要求。

Method: 采用树引导的合成流水线构建基准，配合人工过滤器，由194名人类专家进行全面验证。每个问题包含十二种推理标签之一、专家标注的证据页、类型化验证规则和答案格式，包括218个None答案案例。基准包含多页证据（72.6%）、表格图表（36.6%）和跨文档问题（10.9%）。

Result: 实验结果表明，当前系统在长上下文、多页证据和结构化推理场景下仍然表现困难，难以处理专业文档的复杂理解任务。该基准能够将系统失败归因于检索、证据使用或规则遵循等具体环节。

Conclusion: XL-DocBench填补了先前单页、短多页或纯文本长上下文基准的空白，提供了将超长专业上下文与页级证据和类型化规则相结合的评测框架，为未来工作提供了细粒度的失败归因能力。

Abstract: Real-world document tasks often ask professionals to answer questions from annual reports, regulations, clinical guidelines, and technical manuals that span hundreds or thousands of pages. Some questions also require comparing related reports. Reliable long-document understanding is therefore a prerequisite for using LLMs in compliance, clinical, financial, and engineering workflows, where decisions must be traceable to specific evidence pages and the cost of an unsupported answer is high -- yet most existing benchmarks still measure short-context or single-page QA. We introduce XL-DocBench, a fully human-verified benchmark for extra-long document understanding, with 1,519 retained questions from six professional domains and contexts up to 2,303 pages. XL-DocBench goes beyond page-level lookup. 1,103 examples (72.6\%) use multiple evidence pages. The final set also includes 556 questions (36.6\%) that use tables, charts, or figures, and 165 questions (10.9\%) that require evidence from multiple documents. Each question has one of twelve reasoning labels, expert-annotated evidence pages, a typed verification rule, and an answer format, including 218 None-answer cases. We build the benchmark with a tree-guided synthesis pipeline followed by artifact filters and full verification by 194 human experts. By coupling extra-long professional contexts with page-level evidence and typed rules, XL-DocBench fills a gap left by prior single-page, short multi-page, or text-only long-context benchmarks, and lets future work attribute system failures to retrieval, evidence use, or rule following rather than to a single leaderboard score. The results show that current systems still struggle with long contexts, multi-page evidence, and structured reasoning over professional documents.

</details>


### [35] [Trustworthiness Costs of Domain Adaptation in Small Language Models:A Cross-Architecture Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00042)
*Ramesh B. Paramkusham*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了小型语言模型(SLM)领域适配的信任成本，涵盖3种架构、3个领域、2种训练数据条件和4种微调策略共216个实验配置。研究发现：基线QLoRA领域适配对事实校准影响极小；对抗扰动数据能提升适配质量且不损害信任度；三种安全保持策略均未能有效降低对抗攻击风险，其中Dark ER和TA-LoRA反而显著增加了HarmBench ASR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型领域适配在资源受限的高风险场景（医疗、法律、金融）中应用日益广泛，但领域适配对模型信任度（事实校准与对抗鲁棒性）的影响尚未得到系统理解，现有研究缺乏跨领域、跨架构的实证分析。

Method: 采用系统的交叉实验设计：3种SLM架构（TinyLlama 1B、Gemma-2 2B、Llama 3.2 1B）× 3个领域（医疗、法律、金融）× 2种数据条件（良性/对抗扰动）× 4种微调策略（基线LoRA、Safety-DPO、Dark Experience Replay、Task Arithmetic LoRA），使用TruthfulQA MC2评估事实校准、HarmBench ASR评估对抗鲁棒性，共216个配置×3个随机种子。

Result: 三个主要发现：(1) 基线QLoRA领域适配对TruthfulQA MC2影响极小（平均|Delta TQA| < 0.02）；(2) 对抗扰动训练数据一致改善领域适配质量（Delta loss约-0.040）且不恶化信任度指标；(3) Safety-DPO基本中性（Delta ASR < 0.001），但Dark ER和TA-LoRA在安全对齐模型中分别使平均HarmBench ASR增加+0.171和+0.155，个别配置超过+0.45。

Conclusion: 研究结果挑战了"重放式"和"算术合并式"策略能将对齐能力迁移到领域适配SLM的假设。领域适配的信任成本因策略而异：基线适配和对抗数据增强相对安全，但声称的安全保持方法（如Dark ER、TA-LoRA）反而可能引入严重的对抗攻击脆弱性，需谨慎使用。

Abstract: Domain adaptation of small language models (SLMs) has emerged as a practical strategy for deploying capable NLP systems in resource-constrained, high-stakes environments including healthcare, legal services, and financial analysis. While performance gains from parameter-efficient fine-tuning are well characterised, the corresponding impact on trustworthiness (factual calibration and adversarial robustness) remains poorly understood. This paper presents the first systematic cross-domain, cross-architecture empirical study quantifying the trustworthiness cost of domain adaptation across three SLM architectures (TinyLlama 1B, Gemma-2 2B, Llama 3.2 1B), three domains (healthcare, legal, finance), two training-data conditions (benign and adversarially perturbed), and four fine-tuning strategies (baseline LoRA, Safety-DPO, Dark Experience Replay, and Task Arithmetic LoRA, TA-LoRA). Trustworthiness is evaluated through TruthfulQA MC2 (factual calibration) and HarmBench ASR (adversarial robustness) across all 216 experimental configurations with three random seeds. Three principal findings emerge. First, baseline QLoRA domain adaptation produces minimal TruthfulQA MC2 change across all model-domain combinations (mean |Delta TQA| < 0.02). Second, adversarially perturbed training data consistently improves domain adaptation quality (Delta loss approximately -0.040) without worsening trustworthiness benchmarks. Third, none of the three safety-preserving strategies reduced adversarial harm susceptibility: Safety-DPO was effectively neutral (mean Delta ASR < 0.001), while Dark ER and TA-LoRA increased mean HarmBench ASR by +0.171 and +0.155 respectively in safety-aligned models (Gemma-2 2B, Llama 3.2 1B), with individual configurations exceeding +0.45. These results challenge the assumption that replay-based and arithmetic-merge strategies transfer alignment to domain-adapted SLMs.

</details>


### [36] [Predicting Startup Exit from Textual Descriptors - A Computational Linguistics Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00045)
*Alberto M. G. Saruggia,Sebastien Germano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究证明仅凭文本描述即可预测早期初创企业的成功（Exit），在7419家初创企业20年数据上构建850个文本特征，LightGBM取得最佳预测性能（F1=0.48），纯文本描述特征F1=0.30，并引入了可量化的"Hyping Score"指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高度信息不对称的早期投资环境中，传统依赖财务数据、人力资本等变量的预测方法存在局限，本文旨在探索纯文本描述是否足以独立预测初创企业退出成功。

Method: 使用风投策展的7419家初创企业20年数据集，排除背景/财务/人力资本变量，通过初创企业叙事映射构建850个文本特征，进行统计显著性检验和向量嵌入分析，随后在6个监督学习模型中进行实验对比。

Abstract: This study shows that textual descriptors alone can predict early-stage startup success, defined as Exit, without relying on contextual, financial, or human capital variables. Using venture capital-curated datasets covering 7,419 startups over 20 years, the research isolates text-based framing variables and engineers 850 features through startup narrative mapping. Data subsets and vector embeddings are evaluated for statistical significance, followed by supervised machine learning experiments across six models. LightGBM achieved the highest predictive performance (F1 = 0.48), while textual descriptors alone achieved F1 = 0.30, confirming the standalone predictive value of founder narratives. Feature analysis shows that optimized densities of hyping markers, including adjectives, jargon, and buzzwords, are associated with higher Exit probability, whereas excessive statement or name length reduces it. The study also introduces a quantifiable Hyping Score for venture capital applications, demonstrating that startup framing provides measurable signals for predicting Exit under conditions of high information asymmetry.

Abstract: The success of connectome mapping now shifts the challenge of understanding the nervous system to the interpretation of neural circuits. Here, we devise a new automated method, LLantia (LLM automated neural circuit inference and analysis), to systematically infer neural circuit function and the role of its component neural cell types. Our approach distills descriptions of cell type function from the literature and, in combination with the connectome, then infers the function for all other cell types, which serves as a basis for subsequent neural circuit function inference. Results are structured hierarchically, with different possible circuit functions organised under multiple possible behavioural and physiological contexts, and each circuit function composed of subcircuit descriptions alongside relevant cell types to facilitate both backtracking to known, published information and support further experimental research. We illustrate our method by inferring cell type function for all cell types of the adult fruit fly brain and for select broader circuits within, and validate our findings, including by cross-checking with literature published after the release date of our analysis.

</details>

*Yan Fang,Jialin Chen,Chun Gan,Hang Yu,Mingjun Nie,Yeyu Zhang,Fengxiang He,Ching Law*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-OSDA，一种面向LLM原生广告的动态每次点击付费拍卖机制，将最优停止理论、赢家分配和封套定价整合到动态拍卖框架中。理论证明在Bellman oracle下分配单调性和真实性成立，实践上用StopNet近似Bellman动作值并在模拟数据集上相比最强固定时机基线提升11%净收入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM广告拍卖机制只在单次响应内运作，只决定赢家但不决定广告插入的时机。然而在每会话仅有一次原生插入机会的设定下，停止时间取决于竞价，导致时机与分配耦合，静态真实性论证不再适用，需要动态拍卖机制来解决这一非平凡扩展问题。

Method: 提出LLM-OSDA动态拍卖框架：(1) 将最优停止（Bellman方程）与赢家分配及封套定价整合；(2) 使用竞价无关的LLM层估计上下文点击质量并渲染广告，竞价仅进入拍卖机制；(3) 在精确Bellman oracle下证明期望折扣点击分配的单调性及封套支付使真实竞价成为弱占优策略；(4) 实际部署中用StopNet近似Bellman动作值，分析其与最优策略仅在停止边界附近存在差异，并将激励损失界限绑定到近似误差。

Result: 在模拟对话广告语料上的实验表明，LLM-OSDA相比最强固定时机基线在净收入上提升11%，同时保持相当的\"用户留存\"。理论结果包括分配单调性和激励兼容性界限，StopNet的近似误差与激励损失之间存在可量化关联。

Conclusion: LLM-OSDA有效解决了LLM原生广告中\"何时插入广告\"的动态决策问题，通过将最优停止理论融入拍卖机制，在保持激励兼容性的同时显著提升收入，为LLM广告拍卖提供了动态化的理论框架与实际可行的实现方案。

Abstract: LLM-native advertising embeds sponsored content directly into model-generated responses, shifting the unit of sale from a fixed slot to a moment within an evolving conversation. Existing LLM ad-auction mechanisms primarily operate within a single response, settling the winner but not the timing. The extension is nontrivial: with one native insertion opportunity per session, the stopping time depends on bids, coupling timing with allocation, so static truthfulness arguments no longer apply. We propose the LLM-based Optimal Stopping Dynamic Auction (LLM-OSDA), a dynamic cost-per-click auction that integrates Bellman optimal stopping, winner allocation, and envelope pricing. A bid-independent LLM layer estimates contextual click quality and seamlessly renders the winning ad, while bids enter only the committed auction mechanism. Under an exact Bellman oracle, the expected discounted-click allocation is monotone in each advertiser's bid, and the corresponding envelope payment makes truthful bidding weakly dominant in expectation. For practical deployment, a learned StopNet approximates the Bellman action values. We show that its decisions differ from the optimal policy only near the stopping boundary and bound the resulting incentive loss in terms of its approximation error. Experiments on a simulated conversational advertising corpus show that LLM-OSDA improves net revenue by 11 percent over the strongest fixed-timing baseline while maintaining comparable user retention. Code is at https://github.com/2025Fang2025/llm-osda.

</details>

*DiffusionGemma Team,Adrien Ali Taïga,James Assiene,Daniele Calandriello,Rahma Chaabouni,João Gante,Tamara von Glehn,Nate Keating,Chris Knutsen,Martin Kukla,Tianlin Liu,Ivan Lobov,Ofir Nabati,João Gabriel Oliveira,Nicolas Perez-Nieves,Nastasia Prutianova,Bobak Shahriari,Jean Tarbouriech,Pavel Tyletski,Çağlar Ünlü,Cindy Wu,Glenn Cameron,Jerome Connor,Sertan Girgin,Maarten Grootendorst,Alon Levkovitch,Eliya Nachmani,Omar Sanseviero,Piotr Stanczyk,Quentin Berthet,Andrew Campbell,Clément Crepy,Valentin De Bortoli,Arnaud Doucet,Romuald Elie,Alexandre Galashov,Klaus Greff,Alexis Jacq,David Ruhe,Yu-Han Wu,Sebastian Flennerhag,Brendan O'Donoghue,George Scrivener,Shantanu Thakoor*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DiffusionGemma是一个实验性的开源权重语言模型，通过离散扩散机制并行生成256个token块，而非逐token解码。它通过对Gemma 4 MoE模型（3.8B激活/25.2B总参数）进行微调获得，使用不到原始模型10%的训练token预算，在单块H100 GPU上实现约1500 tokens/s的生成速度，建立了生成速度与模型能力之间的新帕累托前沿，同时保留思考模式、多模态和长上下文支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自回归大语言模型的逐token顺序解码存在严重的速度瓶颈，作者希望利用离散扩散模型并行生成文本，以从根本上解决自回归解码的序列依赖问题，在保持模型能力的同时大幅提升推理速度。

Method: 采用两阶段计算高效训练流程：(1) 第一阶段使用监督微调（SFT）教导模型进行双向去噪；(2) 第二阶段结合强化学习（RL）与采样器蒸馏，联合优化生成质量和推理效率。模型通过对Gemma 4 MoE模型微调获得，而非从零训练。

Result: 模型每次前向传播约生成20个token，在单块NVIDIA H100 GPU上达到约1500 tokens/s的生成速度，远超即使采用最先进投机解码的AR模型。在完整评估套件上建立了生成速度与模型能力权衡的新帕累托前沿，且扩散微调后仍能以轻微性能损失进行AR生成。

Conclusion: DiffusionGemma验证了扩散式解码作为AR解码可行替代方案的潜力，为混合扩散-AR解码路径提供了可能性，同时证明计算高效的扩散微调能够在不牺牲过多能力的前提下实现显著的推理加速。

Abstract: We introduce DiffusionGemma, an experimental open-weight language model that uses discrete diffusion to generate text at exceptionally high speed. Rather than decoding one token at a time, DiffusionGemma iteratively refines blocks of 256 tokens in parallel, avoiding the sequential decoding bottleneck of conventional autoregressive (AR) large language models. Instead of training from scratch, we obtain DiffusionGemma by fine-tuning the mixture-of-experts Gemma 4 model with 3.8B activated and 25.2B total parameters. Our compute-efficient two-stage training pipeline uses fewer than 10% of the starting AR model's total training token budget. The first stage uses supervised fine-tuning to teach bidirectional denoising, while the second stage combines reinforcement learning with sampler distillation to jointly improve generation quality and inference efficiency. DiffusionGemma establishes a new Pareto frontier for the trade-off between generation speed and model capability. Averaged across our full evaluation suite, it generates around 20 tokens per forward pass and achieves roughly 1,500 output tokens per second on a single NVIDIA H100 GPU, which is substantially faster than AR models even with state-of-the-art speculative decoding. DiffusionGemma also retains the starting model's support for thinking mode, multimodal inputs, and long contexts. Despite diffusion fine-tuning, it remains capable of AR generation with only minor performance degradation, suggesting a path toward hybrid diffusion-AR decoding.

</details>


### [40] [A Constitution-Grid Instrument for Data-Efficient RL Alignment (C-Guard)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00180)
*Lily Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出C-Guard和C-LIM方法，用于解决RL对齐中安全性与有用性冲突的训练数据效率问题。通过constitution-grid生成RL训练数据，并用per-cell可学习性评分决定每个数据单元的处理策略（剪枝、增密、修正、扩展），从而在提高拒绝性能（过拒率从22.4%降至12.8%）的同时避免对对抗攻击的欠拒恶化，并消除无效数据区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL对齐中安全目标与有用性目标天然冲突（捕捉真实危害 vs 不拒绝良性提示），在冲突目标上做数据高效训练非常困难。现有的过拒与欠拒之间存在难以平衡的权衡，且训练预算可能浪费在无效数据上。

Method: 提出C-Guard：constitution-grid工具，用于生成RL训练数据；提出C-LIM：per-cell可学习性评分，指导每个cell的四种操作（prune剪枝、densify增密、amend修正、expand扩展），在训练前识别dead-weight数据区域，优化数据分布与效率。

Result: 过拒率从22.4%改善至12.8%；对抗攻击的欠拒从0.27轻微恶化至0.33；识别出187行无价值的untargeted数据行，并通过方法将同一数据区域的学习影响力从0.733提升至0.80。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度的数据单元感知与治理，可以在安全对齐中实现更高效的数据利用，在降低过拒的同时控制欠拒风险，并显著提升无效数据区域的训练影响力。代码与宪法开源。

Abstract: Conflicting objectives are general in RL alignment, and training on them data-efficiently is hard. Training a safety guard with RL means optimizing two objectives that conflict: catch real harm, and do not refuse benign prompts. Our finding is that over-refusal improves 22.4% to 12.8%, while under-refusal on adversarial attacks silently worsens 0.27 to 0.33. We present C-Guard, a constitution-grid instrument that generates the RL training data, and C-LIM, a per-cell learnability score that decides each cell's move: prune, densify, amend, expand. C-LIM flags the dead-weight data region before any training budget is spent: 187 untargeted rows had bought zero gain, and our method lifts the same region's learning impact 0.733 to 0.80. Code and the constitution are open-sourced.

</details>


### [41] [Averaging Bias: Human Faithfulness Annotations are not Locally Faithful](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00205)
*Huajian Zhang,Yiyang Feng,Jiawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了文本摘要忠实度评估中人类标注的"平均偏差"现象，发现人类标注者倾向于在大多数句子忠实时就判定摘要忠实，而非严格执行"所有句子都必须忠实"的合取规则。通过五个LLM逐句评判和四个基准数据集的实验，证明全局人类标注与逐句判断的平均值相关性更高，且人工复核确认了大量被标为忠实的摘要实际包含局部事实错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本摘要忠实度评估的严格合取规则要求每个句子都必须被源文档支持才算忠实，但现有基准数据集中每个摘要仅有一个全局人类标注。作者质疑这些全局标注是否真正实现了合取规则，怀疑标注者可能在大多数句子忠实时就予以认可，这可能导致基准数据含有系统性偏差。

Method: 使用五个大型语言模型（LLM）作为逐句评审器，在四个广泛使用的忠实度基准数据集上，对比全局人类标注与两套指标的相关性：一是逐句LLM评判的合取规则实现，二是逐句LLM评判的平均值。同时辅以人工复核验证结论。

Result: 实验发现，全局人类标注与逐句LLM判断的平均值相关性更高，而非与严格合取规则的相关性更高。人工复核确认，相当比例被人类标注为忠实的摘要中确实包含真实的事实性错误，证实了"平均偏差"的存在。

Conclusion: 人类标注者在广泛使用的忠实度基准中表现出可测量的"平均偏差"，这意味着现有基准的标注可靠性存在隐患，呼吁设计更严谨的结构化人类标注流程以获取可信的标注数据。

Abstract: Evaluation of faithfulness of text summarization treats a model generated summary as faithful only if every of its sentences is supported by the source document: a strict conjunctive rule under which a single unsupported sentence makes the whole summary unfaithful. Yet most faithfulness benchmarks collect only one global human annotation label per summary. We ask whether such global human labels actually implement the conjunctive rule. We hypothesize that annotators may accept a summary as faithful when most sentences are faithful, not only when all are faithful. To test our hypothesis, we use five large language model (LLM) judges as per-sentence raters across four widely used faithfulness benchmarks. We find that global human labels correlate better with the average of per-sentence LLM judgments than with the implementation of the strict conjunctive rule. A manual review confirms that a substantial fraction of summaries labeled faithful by humans contain genuine local factual errors. We call this tendency Averaging Bias. Our results reveal that human labels on widely used faithfulness benchmarks contain measurable Averaging Bias, calling for carefully structured designs for trustworthy human annotations

</details>


### [42] [Bridging the English-Arabic Medical Knowledge Gap: Targeted Low-Rank Adaptation via Causal Layer Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00207)
*Chaimae Abouzahir,Musa Khan,Hala Ali-Hassan,Congbo Ma,Khaled Saleh,Yousra Sadqi,Jihad Mallat,Walid Al-Eisawi,Nizar Habash,Farah E. Shamout*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过机械性诊断（调谐透镜探测和因果激活修补）发现阿拉伯语医学知识实际上存在于LLM的中间层表示中，但无法在输出层显现。基于此，作者提出靶向低秩适应（TLoRA），仅对跨语言表示分叉的层窗口进行适配，在多种医学任务上优于全网络LoRA及零样本/少样本基线，并引入了阿语医学对话基准AraClinicDialog。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在英语医学任务上表现优异，但在阿拉伯语上性能大幅下降，此前普遍归因于训练数据不足。作者希望系统性地验证这一假设，通过机械性分析揭示性能下降的真实机制，从而指导更有效的适配策略。

Method: 1) 使用调谐透镜探测（tuned lens probing）和因果激活修补（causal activation patching）进行机械性诊断，定位跨语言表示分叉的层窗口。2) 提出靶向低秩适应（TLoRA），仅对识别出的分叉层窗口进行低秩适配，而非全网络微调。3) 构建AraClinicDialog基准，包含标准阿拉伯语及四种方言变体。

Result: TLoRA在多项选择题医学问答上优于全网络LoRA、零样本和少样本基线；在短答案生成和多轮临床对话任务上与基线表现相当，且无需任务特定微调。AraClinicDialog基准的引入为阿拉伯语医学对话评估提供了新的资源。

Conclusion: 机械性诊断可以成为低资源语言医学LLM靶向适配的实用指南。阿拉伯语医学性能下降并非知识缺失，而是表示-输出之间的失联，针对性适配分离层窗口即可有效弥补这一差距。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) perform strongly in English medical tasks but degrade substantially in Arabic, a gap widely attributed to limited training data. We systematically investigate this assumption via tuned lens probing and causal activation patching, and find that Arabic medical knowledge is present in intermediate model representations but fails to surface at the output. This mechanistic insight motivates a targeted adaptation strategy: rather than fine-tuning the full network, we propose Targeted Low-Rank Adaptation (TLoRA), restricted to the layer window where cross-lingual representations diverge, upstream of the output layers where the failure manifests. We evaluate TLoRA on multiple-choice medical QA, where our approach outperforms full-network LoRA, zero-shot, and few-shot baselines. We further evaluate it on short-answer generation and multi-turn clinical dialogue, where it performs competitively without the need for task-specific finetuning. We additionally introduce AraClinicDialog, a clinician-constructed Arabic medical dialogue benchmark in MSA with validated variants across four Arabic dialects. Together, these contributions demonstrate that mechanistic diagnosis can serve as a practical guide for targeted adaptation in underrepresented-language medical LLMs.

</details>


### [43] [A Few Neurons Reveal When LLMs Misuse Tools: Sparse Detection and Selective Steering for Reliable Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00218)
*Yutong Ke,Ming Yin,Chongwen Zhao,Kaizhu Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISMS框架，利用失败特定的MLP神经元实现轻量级的工具调用失败检测与选择性干预，在六个模型上显著降低过度调用率并提升工具调用准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic LLMs在工具使用时存在三类关键失败：参数无效（validity）、不必要调用（over-calling）和遗漏调用（missing）。现有方法缺乏轻量且可干预的失败检测机制，且无条件干预会带来附带损害。

Method: PRISMS是一个闭环框架，通过发现失败特定的MLP神经元子集，拟合L1正则化稀疏探测器（仅使用1-128个神经元），并在检测到失败风险时实施选择性激活干预（steering），实现双向控制工具调用行为。

Result: 在Qwen3、Llama、Gemma六个模型中，over-calling和missing在提示边界处检测ROC-AUC达0.90-1.00，validity在工具调用片段检测达0.86-0.90。与稠密基线相比使用23-627倍更少特征。干预后over-calling率从0.131降至0.026（降80%），工具所需准确率从0.689提升至0.831（提升14.2个百分点）。

Conclusion: PRISMS证明失败特定的稀疏MLP神经元可同时支持检测与干预，为跨模型家族提供轻量级的工具调用失败监控与选择性修正方案。

Abstract: Agentic LLMs exhibit three consequential tool-use failures: invalid arguments (validity), unnecessary calls (over-calling), and omitted calls when tools are needed (missing). We find that a small, failure-specific set of MLP neurons could distinguish such failures with linearly separable decision boundaries. Building on this observation, we introduce PRISMS (Probing Representations In Support of Monitoring and Steering), a closed-loop framework that shares a failure-specific neuron basis between sparse detection and activation steering. PRISMS selects contribution-critical MLP neurons and fits an L1-regularized detector on their activations. Across six models from the Qwen3, Llama, and Gemma families, over-calling and missing are detected at the pre-generation prompt boundary with ROC-AUC 0.90-1.00, while validity is detected from the generated tool-call span with ROC-AUC 0.86-0.90. These results are achieved with highly sparse readouts: only 1-2 MLP neurons for missing, 2-16 for over-calling, and approximately 128 for validity. These sparse detectors match or outperform dense residual-stream baselines using 23-627 times fewer features. The shared neuron basis also supports bidirectional control over tool-calling behavior, suppressing unnecessary calls and eliciting omitted ones. PRISMS therefore gates intervention on predicted failure risk to mitigate the collateral effects of unconditional steering. Across all six models, PRISMS reduces pooled over-calling rate by 80% (from 0.131 to 0.026) while increasing tool-required accuracy by 14.2 percentage points (from 0.689 to 0.831). PRISMS thus provides lightweight failure detection and selective intervention across model families.

</details>


### [44] [Sixteen models, fewer than two voices: measuring ensemble dispersion where no answer is uniquely correct](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00285)
*Mario Vega-Barbas,Lidia Mora-Valenciano,Iván Pau,Fernando Seoane,Farhad Abtahi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究分析了16个来自十个语言模型家族的模型在心理治疗案例文本生成中的语义多样性，发现模型集成比单一模型产生更多样化的表述。作者定义了"异议贡献"指标来识别集成中最具分歧的声音，并发现模型身份是可检测的结构性因素，但传统分类（规模、家族）只能部分解释这种变异，分歧主要受临床内容而非解释开放性的驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然集成方法基于"多个模型提供多种视角"的假设，但现有研究仅衡量输出的多样性或不确定性，缺乏对多样性来源的归因分析。传统验证方式依赖正确性标准，而本文所面对的心理治疗案例生成任务不存在客观正确性标准，因此需要新的度量方法来理解集成分散性

Method: 使用Vendi Score（冯诺依曼熵的指数）测量多样性；定义每个模型的"异议贡献"——该模型与集成中其他模型的平均相似度的补数；通过预注册假设检验模型身份对异议方差的作用；使用十五个分层小插图构建7,082个表述进行分析

Result: 十六个模型的平均语义多样性为1.69个不同表述（基准为1.43）；模型身份是异议的可检测结构因素，但规模差异在不同模型对间方向相反，家族仅在五条双成员线上分组；最分歧的声音随集成构成变化，表明异常模型描述的是集成而非模型本身；分歧不跟随解释开放性分层，而是由临床内容组织

Conclusion: 集成产生的分散性是一个需要测量而非假设的属性。传统对模型规模和家族的分类只能部分解释异议结构，模型的发散性具有情境依赖性。在缺乏正确性标准的任务中，需要从模型特征导向转向集成构成导向的分析框架。

Abstract: Sixteen language models drawn from ten families produced, on average, the semantic diversity of 1.69 distinct formulations of a psychotherapeutic case, against a single-model baseline of 1.43 from one model's own runs. Ensembles place more than one reading before a decision-maker on the premise that several models supply several perspectives. Dispersion over their outputs is measured both as diversity and as uncertainty, and both traditions validate it against a correctness criterion that this task does not admit. Measuring diversity is a solved problem: the Vendi Score, the exponential of the von Neumann entropy of a similarity matrix, is an effective number of distinct elements. What a single aggregate does not say is where the diversity comes from. We define a per-model dissent contribution, the complement of a model's mean similarity to the other members of its ensemble: a magnitude from the same matrix, not a decomposition of the spectral index, whose maximum identifies the most divergent voice. Crossing model and case, we test as a preregistered hypothesis whether model identity accounts for a non-zero share of the variance in dissent, and characterise the structure that test detects. The panel formulated fifteen stratified vignettes, yielding 7,082 formulations for analysis. Model identity was a detectable structuring factor of the dissent that remained, but the usual categories recovered it only partly: scale differences pointed in opposite directions across pairs, family grouped models on only five two-member lines, and the most divergent voice changed with panel composition, so that the surfaced outlier describes the ensemble rather than the model. Dissent did not track the interpretive openness for which the case bank was stratified; it was organised by clinical content instead, leaving the dispersion an ensemble produces a property to measure rather than assume.

</details>

### [45] [Skillsets on the Chain: A Blockchain-based Zero-Trust Framework for Agentic AI Networking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00104)
*Yayu Gao,Yong Xiao,Hao Hu,Xubo Li,Zhiwei Liu,Yingyu Li,Guangming Shi,Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TrustAgentNet，一个双层区块链保护的零信任框架，用于解决Agentic AI网络（AgentNet）中第三方技能集（skillset）的可信性与安全性问题，通过全局技能链（CoS）和瞬态任务协作链（CoC）实现去信任化的多智能体协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AgentNet系统严重依赖第三方技能集实现和分布式多智能体协作，但在"信任即声明"（trust-by-declaration）假设下存在声明与能力不一致（claim-to-capability inconsistencies）以及安全漏洞等问题，亟需建立可信的验证框架。

Method: 提出TrustAgentNet双层零信任框架：（1）全局Chain of Skillsets（CoS）管理技能集元数据生命周期，结合专用智能体执行链下审计与轻量级链上加密共识；（2）动态建立瞬态、面向任务的Chain of Collaboration（CoC）实现无信任分布式多智能体协作；理论分析安全性、任务性能和资源开销的三方权衡。

Result: 在硬件原型上验证：相比无区块链的trust-by-default基线，零信任开销主要由链下推理主导，区块链层通过ledger-IPFS存储和链上链下集成设计仅产生少量账本成本；验证流水线在50个AI模型上实现100%准确率（40个诚实技能集全部验证通过，10个对抗性技能集全部拦截），并在171个ClawHub技能的1478个特征上以83.91%准确率和0.85 F1-score泛化到非AI领域；对抗实验证明TrustAgentNet支持技能集自主恢复能力。

Conclusion: TrustAgentNet通过双层区块链架构有效弥合了AgentNet中声明与能力之间的差距，实现了高准确率的技能集验证、较低的链上开销以及对抗环境下的自主恢复能力，为可信Agentic AI网络提供了可行的零信任方案。

Abstract: Agentic AI networking (AgentNet) systems rely heavily on third-party skillset implementations and distributed multi-agent collaboration, yet they face major claim-to-capability inconsistencies and security vulnerabilities under trust-by-declaration assumptions. To bridge this gap, this paper proposes TrustAgentNet, a dual-tier blockchain-secured zero-trust framework. Specifically, a global Chain of Skillsets (CoS) governs the lifecycle of skillset metadata with protocols empowered by specialized agents to enforce off-chain auditing while maintaining lightweight on-chain cryptographic consensus. Furthermore, transient, task-oriented Chains of Collaboration (CoC) are dynamically established to enable trustless distributed multi-agent collaboration. Theoretical analysis of the three-way trade-off among security level, task performance, and resource overhead is provided and empirically validated. Experimental results on a hardware prototype demonstrate that compared with no-blockchain trust-by-default baselines, the zero-trust overhead of TrustAgentNet is dominated by off-chain inference, while the blockchain layer incurs minor ledger costs via the ledger-IPFS storage and on/off-chain integration design. Crucially, the proposed verification pipeline achieves a flawless 100% accuracy across 50 AI models, correctly validating 40 honest skillsets and intercepting 10 adversarial ones, and generalizes to non-AI domains with an 83.91% accuracy and a 0.85 F1-score across 1478 features from 171 ClawHub skills. Adversarial experiments further show that TrustAgentNet enables autonomous skillset self-recovery against various malicious attacks.

</details>


### [46] [Deep Learning for Cyber Threat Detection and Mitigation in Healthcare-IoT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00118)
*Mirza Akhi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对医疗物联网（H-IoT）中的网络安全问题，生成了多个真实数据集（UL-ECE系列），并提出了轻量级深度学习模型TCN和Res-TCN用于检测DDoS、选择性转发和中间人攻击等威胁，最终通过量化转换为TFLite模型部署在树莓派4上实现边缘端的实时低延迟检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗物联网系统中网络安全直接关系到患者安全，然而现有基于深度学习的网络安全机制存在模型架构复杂、参数量大、难以在资源受限设备上部署的问题，且已有数据集缺乏质量评估，适用性有限。

Method: （1）利用Cooja和ns-3仿真器生成了三个数据集：两个二分类DDoS攻击数据集（基于MQTT和UDP协议）和一个多分类攻击数据集（SF、MITM、DDoS），第三个数据集融合了生理特征和网络特征；（2）提出时间卷积网络（TCN）和残差TCN（Res-TCN）轻量级模型；（3）采用基于监控频率的检测机制和动态阈值缓解策略；（4）模型经量化后转换为TensorFlow Lite（TFLite）格式部署于树莓派4进行实时检测。

Result: 所提出的轻量级模型在三个自建数据集上实现了有效的攻击检测与缓解，并在树莓派4边缘设备上以低延迟和低功耗方式完成实时DDoS检测，验证了模型在资源受限环境下的实用性。

Conclusion: 本文构建了一套完整的基于深度学习的H-IoT网络安全防御体系，涵盖真实数据集生成、轻量级模型设计和边缘部署三个环节，为医疗物联网系统的安全性提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Cybersecurity is a fundamental requirement for protecting wearable devices used in healthcare Internet of Things (H-IoT) systems. Security failures in these resource-constrained systems directly compromise patient safety. Physiological data and network traffic are frequent targets of cyberattacks in H-IoT environments. To address these risks, deep learning-based cybersecurity mechanisms for H-IoT often involve complex architectures with large parameter counts. Existing datasets are also rarely assessed for quality, limiting their applicability. However, this research addresses these challenges by developing multiple realistic datasets and proposing lightweight deep learning models, namely the Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) and Residual TCN (Res-TCN), for H-IoT. It includes two binary classification datasets for Distributed Denial of Service (DDoS) attacks and a multiclass dataset representing Selective Forwarding (SF), Man-in-the-Middle (MITM), and DDoS attacks. The datasets UL-ECE-MQTT-DDoS-H-IoT2025 and UL-ECE-UDP-DDoS-H-IoT2025 are generated in Cooja and ns-3 to capture transmission behaviours and protocol variations. The third dataset, UL-ECE-MultiAttack-H-IoT2025, integrates physiological and network features to represent multiple cyber threats in H-IoT. Building on this, the TCN model is designed to detect and mitigate DDoS attacks over the MQTT and UDP-based datasets. It incorporates a monitoring frequency-based detection mechanism and a dynamic threshold-based mitigation strategy. To enable edge deployment, the model is quantised and converted into TensorFlow Lite (TFLite) for real-time DDoS detection on Raspberry Pi 4, achieving low latency and power-efficient operation in H-IoT. This thesis establishes a deep learning-based cybersecurity defence mechanism encompassing realistic dataset generation, lightweight model design, and edge deployment for securing H-IoT systems.

</details>


### [47] [Enforcing Access Control in Distributed Version Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00132)
*Xin Xu,Quanwei Cai,Jingqiang Lin,Shiran Pan,Liangqin Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向分布式版本控制系统（DVCS）的分布式访问控制方案（Disac），利用基于属性的加密（ABE）和基于属性的签名（ABS）分别实现读写权限控制，使每个实体能独立执行访问控制并完全掌控自身数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的访问控制方案主要针对中心化版本控制系统（CVCS）设计，依赖服务器协调执行；而DVCS中每个实体都存储整个仓库、可自由共享，服务器不再是可信中心，因此CVCS的访问控制方案不适用于DVCS，需要一种分布式的访问控制机制。

Method: 提出Disac方案，采用基于属性的加密（ABE）实现读权限控制，采用基于属性的签名（ABS）实现写权限控制；访问控制由每个实体独立执行，同时保证各实体对自身数据的完全控制权。

Result: 对Git客户端的分析表明，Disac方案可以轻松集成到现有DVCS工具中。

Conclusion: Disac是一种适用于DVCS的分布式访问控制方案，能够在不依赖中心服务器的前提下实现有效的读写权限管控，且具有良好的可集成性。

Abstract: Version control systems (VCS), including central VCS (CVCS) and distributed VCS (DVCS), are widely adopted to manage the changes to various types of data. Unlike the CVCS where all the entities obtain the data from the server and the access control is enforced with the cooperation of the server, each entity in the DVCS stores the entire repository, obtains the repository shared by any entity and is free to share its own repository. Therefore, existing access control schemes for CVCS are not suitable for DVCS. In this paper, we present a distributed access control scheme (Disac) for DVCS. Disac makes each entity have full control over its data, while the access control is enforced at each entity independently. We adopt Attribute-based Encryption (ABE) and Attribute-based Signature (ABS) to achieve the read and write permission control. The analysis of the Git client demonstrates that Disac can be integrated easily.

</details>


### [48] [Stateful Cooperative Agents Safeguarding LLMs Against Evolving Multi-Turn Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00134)
*Siyuan Li,Zehao Liu,Haoyu Li,Xi Lin,Ning Liu,Jun Wu,Jianhua Li,Mohsen Guizani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CoopGuard，一个基于多智能体协作的主动防御框架，通过"破坏-误导-适应"三种互补策略对抗多轮对抗性攻击。在EMRA数据集上（5,200个样本，8种攻击类型），该框架将攻击成功率平均降低69%，欺骗参与率（DR）提升6倍以上，并使攻击者Token消耗增加198.83%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LLM的对抗性防御方法本质上是被动反应式的，难以应对攻击者在多轮交互中持续调整策略的复杂威胁。

Method: 提出一个主动防御框架，包含三个核心策略：1) 受控响应节奏以增加攻击成本；2) 战略性模糊输出以误导攻击者进入无效策略；3) 对交互日志进行取证分析以识别攻击模式并优化防御。这些策略由多智能体架构中的专门智能体协调执行，并由自适应机制根据威胁升级动态调整防御策略。同时构建了EMRA数据集模拟多轮攻击的演化策略。

Result: 在EMRA数据集上，相对于最先进的基线，该框架将攻击成功率平均降低69%；欺骗参与率（DR）达到最强基线的6倍以上；攻击者Token消耗平均增加198.83%。

Conclusion: 通过主动防御策略替代被动反应式防御，能够更有效地对抗多轮对抗性攻击，既抑制了有害输出，又通过保持欺骗性参与来消耗攻击者资源，为LLM安全防御提供了新的范式。

Abstract: As LLMs become increasingly integrated into complex applications, their vulnerability to adversarial attacks has raised significant concerns. However, existing defenses remain reactive in nature. This limitation makes it difficult for them to counter sophisticated threats, as adversaries continuously adjust their strategies across multi-turn interactions. In this paper, we present a proactive defense framework for securing LLMs against evolving multi-turn adversarial attacks that combines disruption, misdirection, and adaptation across successive interaction turns. In particular, it employs a cooperative multi-agent architecture in which specialized agents execute complementary defense strategies. These strategies include controlled response pacing to increase attack costs, strategically ambiguous outputs to mislead adversaries into ineffective strategies, and forensic analysis of interaction logs to identify attack patterns and refine defenses. These agents are coordinated by an adaptive mechanism that dynamically adjusts the defense strategy in response to escalating threats. To facilitate comprehensive evaluation, we present the EMRA dataset designed to simulate evolving strategies across multi-turn attacks, including 5,200 adversarial samples across eight attack types. Experimental results on EMRA across multiple LLM backbones show that the proposed framework reduces ASR by 69% on average relative to evaluated state-of-the-art baselines. Beyond suppressing harmful outputs, it sustains deceptive engagement, achieving an average DR more than six times that of the strongest baselines and increasing attacker-token consumption by 198.83% on average relative to evaluated baselines. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/SiyuanLi00/CoopGuard.

</details>


### [49] [Symbolic Attack Chain Generation from Atomic Red Team Techniques: An Empirical Study of Predicate Representation Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00143)
*Ramya Varunsegar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了在自动化攻击链生成中，谓词表示粒度（九类别AALM vs 五类别方案）对计划有效性、成本和保真度的影响。通过LLM翻译和Fast Downward推理的pipeline，在十六种技术语料库上的实验表明，两种方案81.3%的结果相同，计划有效性和成本对粒度不敏感，更高粒度主要增强计划内部结构分辨率而非攻击链本身的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化攻击链生成对现代网络安全至关重要，但手动构建无法随攻击者行为扩展。虽然基于PDDL的经典AI规划能自动化此过程，但依赖技术到符号谓词的准确翻译。现有系统如AURORA采用九类别攻击动作链接模型（AALM），但其特定粒度的必要性尚未得到验证。

Method: 采用LLM执行技术到谓词的翻译，Fast Downward引擎执行确定性推理的pipeline。将完整的九类别AALM与从Atomic Red Team（ART）执行证据中经验推导的五类别简化方案进行比较研究。

Result: 在十六种技术语料库上，两种方案在81.3%的情况下产生相同结果。计划有效性和成本在很大程度上对粒度不敏感。

Conclusion: 更高的粒度主要增强计划论证的内部结构分辨率，而非生成攻击链本身的可行性。这表明在攻击链规划中，过多追求谓词表示粒度可能并非必要。

Abstract: Automated attack chain generation is critical for modern cybersecurity, yet manual construction fails to scale as adversary behaviors expand. While classical AI planning using PDDL offers a formal method to automate this process, it relies on the accurate translation of techniques into symbolic predicates. Current state-of-the-art systems like AURORA employ a nine-category Attack Action Linking Model (AALM), but the necessity of this specific granularity remains unvalidated. This work investigates the impact of predicate representation granularity on plan validity, cost, and fidelity. Utilizing a pipeline where a Large Language Model (LLM) performs translation and the Fast Downward engine performs deterministic reasoning, the study compares the full nine-category AALM against a reduced five-category scheme derived empirically from Atomic Red Team (ART) execution evidence. Results from a sixteen-technique corpus demonstrate that plan validity and cost are largely insensitive to granularity, with 81.3% identical outcomes across both schemes. The findings suggest that higher granularity primarily enhances the internal structural resolution of a plan's justification rather than the viability of the generated attack chain itself.

</details>


### [50] [Exposed by Design: A Dynamic Security Assessment of Internet-Facing MCP Servers at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00150)
*Nicolás Padilla*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对互联网上暴露的MCP（模型上下文协议）服务器进行动态行为安全评估，通过11个数据源被动发现640个生产环境MCP服务器，并使用自研的Corvus框架动态审计了414个服务器，发现了68个可报告漏洞，包括SQL注入、SSRF、提示词模板注入和路径遍历等。研究发现91.8%的服务器缺乏OAuth认证，41.6%的服务器在三天内消失，表明缺乏安全审查的快速部署周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP协议自2024年11月推出以来迅速被广泛采用，公共互联网上已检测到超过21,000个服务器实例，但此前缺乏对互联网暴露的MCP服务器的动态行为安全评估，安全性状况不明。

Method: 结合被动发现（覆盖crt.sh、HuggingFace、GitHub、npm等11个数据源）和主动动态测试，使用自研的Corvus框架对MCP服务器进行安全审计。Corvus实现了34个测试模块，覆盖10类MCP特有漏洞。研究在2026年7月进行了四轮测量。

Result: 确认640个生产MCP服务器，动态审计414个，发现68个可报告漏洞（包括SQL注入、针对云元数据服务的SSRF、提示词模板注入和路径遍历）。91.8%的服务器缺乏OAuth认证，687个工具实例暴露无访问控制的shell执行能力，41.6%的服务器在三天内消失。

Conclusion: MCP服务器的安全状况堪忧，存在大量缺乏认证和访问控制的高危暴露。研究建立了负责任披露流程，并开源了Corvus框架供MCP安全评估使用，为该领域的安全研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) has seen rapid adoption since its November 2024 launch, with over 21,000 server instances detectable on the public internet. We present the first dynamic behavioral security assessment of internet-facing MCP servers, combining passive discovery across eleven data sources (crt.sh, HuggingFace, GitHub, npm, Smithery, PyPI, Censys, FOFA, Shodan, glama.ai, and pulsemcp.com) with active dynamic testing using Corvus, a purpose-built framework implementing 34 test modules covering 10 MCP-specific vulnerability classes. Across four measurement runs spanning July 2026, we confirm 640 production MCP servers and dynamically audit 414, uncovering 68 reportable vulnerabilities including SQL injection, SSRF targeting cloud metadata services, prompt template injection, and path traversal via cursor manipulation. We find that 91.8% of dynamically audited servers lack OAuth authentication, 687 tool instances across confirmed servers expose shell execution capabilities without access controls, and 41.6% of confirmed servers disappear within three days between consecutive measurement runs---indicating rapid deployment cycles without security review. We report on our responsible disclosure pipeline and release Corvus as an open-source framework for MCP security evaluation.

</details>


### [51] [From Monoliths to Swarms: A Study of Attack Surface Evolution in the Transition to Multi-Agent Web Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00202)
*Yashaswi Malla,Sandra Siby*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多智能体Web系统的攻击分类体系（taxonomy），并构建了测试平台WebMASLab来系统性地分析多智能体架构引入的新安全漏洞。研究发现，从单智能体（SAS）向多智能体（MAS）的转变并非仅是性能优化，还会引入全新的架构性攻击面，其中最典型的是作者提出的"Telephone Loop攻击"，该攻击利用跨智能体委派机制制造循环任务，能使三个前沿模型平均80%的攻击成功率，但在单智能体场景下完全无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的Web智能体正从单智能体系统（SAS）向多智能体系统（MAS）演进。虽然MAS通过角色分解和子智能体协作提升了任务性能，但这种角色分解引入了SAS中不存在的新型结构性攻击面。这一扩展的攻击面目前缺乏系统性的理解和分类，亟需深入研究。

Method: 首先提出一个针对基于Web的MAS攻击向量的分类体系（taxonomy），涵盖多智能体参与所引入或放大漏洞的类别。然后构建WebMASLab测试平台，在固定用户任务、工具面和浏览器环境的前提下，仅改变架构（单智能体vs多智能体）来隔离架构效果。评估三种对抗场景，跨三个条件（基线、提示加固、推理增强），并设计了创新的Telephone Loop攻击，利用跨智能体委派创建循环任务。

Result: Telephone Loop攻击在单智能体系统中无效，但在多智能体系统中能够使四个前沿模型中的三个（Claude Sonnet 4.5、GPT-5.2、GPT-5.4）平均达到80%的攻击成功率。只有Claude Sonnet 4.6能够以92%的检测率抵抗该攻击。其他模型基线检测率为0%，其中一个模型在提示加固后检测率仅提升至33%。此外，简单防御不具备泛化性：提示加固能将一个模型的攻击成功率从100%降至8%，但对其余模型只有微小的改善。

Conclusion: 从单智能体到多智能体Web系统的转变从根本上改变了安全格局。角色专业化不仅带来性能优化，也带来了新的架构性风险，这些风险需要进一步研究和针对性防御。当前显而易见的防御措施（如提示加固）无法通用地解决这些问题，必须开发面向MAS架构特性的新型防御策略。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based web agents are increasingly evolving from single-agent systems (SAS) to multi-agent systems (MAS). While MAS can lead to improved task performance by decomposing complex tasks across specialized sub-agents, such role decomposition introduces new structural attack surfaces that are absent in SAS. This expanded attack surface remains poorly understood and inadequately categorized.
  To address this, we propose a taxonomy to categorize attack vectors specific to web-based MAS, accounting for vulnerabilities introduced or amplified by the involvement of multiple agents. We further present a test-bed WebMASLab to analyze web agent security against a fully external, web-only adversary. To isolate the effect of architecture, we keep the user task, tool surface, and browser substrate fixed, and compare single- and multi-agent setups. We evaluate three adversarial scenarios, across three conditions (baseline, prompt-hardened, and reasoning-enabled), including a novel MAS-specific Telephone Loop attack that exploits cross-agent delegation to create cyclical task loops. The attack is inert against SAS but compromises MAS when powered by three of the four frontier models evaluated (Claude Sonnet 4.5, GPT-5.2, GPT-5.4), averaging 80% across them at baseline. Only the fourth model, Claude Sonnet 4.6, resists the attack with a 92% detection rate. For the rest, the detection is 0% at baseline, reaching 33% with prompt-hardening for one model. We also show that obvious defenses do not generalize; prompt-hardening collapses one model's ASR from 100% to 8% while providing only modest reduction to the others. Our findings demonstrate that the transition from single- to multi-agent web systems changes the security landscape. Role specialization may not only lead to performance optimization but also introduce new architectural risks that require further study and defenses.

</details>


### [52] [Reflection, Education, Consistency: Towards Best Ethics Practices At Security And Privacy Conferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00282)
*Florian Hantke,Rafael Mrowczynski,Til Dralle,Ben Stock*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过访谈安全与隐私社区资深和初级成员，探讨了顶级会议伦理政策的目标与效果，发现社区虽在向提升伦理意识和防止发表不道德工作方向演进，但缺乏伦理教育是主要障碍，并提出建立社区范围的伦理协调机制和开放伦理Wiki作为第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全与隐私社区中研究伦理议题引发广泛争议，领先会议引入了强制伦理章节等干预措施但反应不一，然而缺乏对伦理程序和干预措施的实证验证，也没有明确传达的目标，阻碍了社区的共识形成和知情决策。

Method: 对四大顶级会议过去的伦理政策进行审查，并对20位社区成员（包括资深和初级成员、现任/前任程序委员会及研究伦理委员会主席）进行深度半结构化访谈，探讨伦理程序的动机、目标、现有干预措施及新方案。

Result: 社区正向提升伦理意识和防止不道德工作发表的目标演进，但发现社区内部缺乏伦理教育是重大障碍；访谈还揭示了伦理政策复杂性、不同会议之间缺乏一致性以及政策逐年变化的问题；部分参与者警告过度监管可能导致反弹，促使人们仅追求形式合规（如使用LLM生成伦理章节）。

Conclusion: 建议采取协调的社区范围的伦理引导工作，并首次引入开放伦理Wiki以推进社区伦理共识和制度建设。

Abstract: Research ethics is a controversial and emotionally charged topic in the security and privacy community, sparking discussions at conferences and on social media. In recent years, some of the leading conferences have introduced interventions such as mandatory ethics sections, with mixed reactions within the community. Program committee chairs and steering committees increasingly emphasize ethics, yet there is limited empirical validation on ethics procedures and interventions, as well as no explicitly communicated goals.
  To support a shared understanding in our community and guide informed decisions at the conference level, we examined past ethics policies at the top-four conferences and conducted in-depth semi-structured interviews with senior and junior (n=20) community members, including some (former) chairs of program and research ethics committees. In these, we explored reasons for and goals of ethics procedures and discussed existing ethics interventions as well as possible new approaches proposed by our team.
  While the community appears to be evolving toward the identified goals, i.e., raising ethical awareness and preventing the publication of unethical work, we identified the lack of ethical education within the community itself as a major obstacle to further progress on this matter. Ethical education is often passed to the universities. Interviewees also mentioned challenges with the complexity of ethics policies and the lack of consistency across different conferences, as well as policies changing year-to-year. Several participants warned that over-regulation may lead to backlash, encouraging mere compliance, such as people turning to LLMs for ethics sections rather than undertaking in-depth consideration. In response, we suggest a coordinated, community-wide ethics steering effort and take a first step by introducing an open ethics wiki.

</details>


### [53] [Trimming: Decoupling Multiplicative Depth from Modulus Chains in RNS-CKKS via Rational Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00375)
*John Chiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Trimming机制，通过引入由较小的NTT友好模数因子组成的辅助修剪模数链，在RNS-CKKS中实现分数级别的模数转换，从而将乘法深度与模数链结构解耦，突破了传统整数级模数链对乘法深度的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Grafting技术已经将缩放因子与密文模数解耦，实现了更灵活的精度管理，但乘法深度仍然受限于模数链的结构。传统RNS-CKKS中，每进行一次乘法就需要丢弃整个模数因子，导致深度调整粒度粗糙，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 提出Trimming机制，核心思想是引入一个由较小的NTT友好模数因子组成的辅助修剪模数链，实现部分模数转换而非直接丢弃整个模数因子。通过用细粒度的模数因子细化取代传统的离散层级缩减，提供超越传统整数基模数链表示的分数层级抽象。

Result: 该方法保持了与现有RNS-CKKS算术的兼容性，同时实现了更灵活的深度管理和自适应模数转换。论文指出将通过具体实现和实验评估来验证该框架在实际同态加密应用中的性能和计算开销。

Conclusion: Trimming针对RNS-CKKS中的深度瓶颈问题，与Grafting解决精度瓶颈互补，共同推动构建完全解耦的RNS-CKKS架构。实验验证仍在进行中，需要进一步评估实际应用中的性能表现。

Abstract: Recent work on Grafting decouples scale factors from ciphertext moduli, enabling more flexible precision management in RNS-CKKS. However, the multiplicative depth remains fundamentally constrained by the modulus chain structure.
  In this paper, we propose \emph{Trimming}, a novel fine-grained level management mechanism that decouples multiplicative depth from modulus chains in RNS-CKKS via rational levels. The key idea is to introduce an auxiliary trimming modulus chain composed of smaller NTT-friendly modulus factors, which enables partial modulus transitions instead of directly discarding an entire modulus factor. By replacing conventional discrete level reductions with fine-grained modulus factor refinement, Trimming provides a rational-level abstraction beyond the traditional integer-based modulus chain representation.
  Our approach preserves the compatibility with existing RNS-CKKS arithmetic while enabling more flexible depth management and adaptive modulus transitions. Similar to Grafting, which addresses the precision bottleneck, Trimming targets the depth bottleneck in RNS-CKKS and contributes toward a fully decoupled RNS-CKKS architecture.
  The proposed framework will be further validated through concrete implementation and experimental evaluation to investigate its practical performance and computational overhead in real-world homomorphic encryption applications.

</details>


### [54] [TI-StegoAlign: Channel-Guided Post-Training for Generative Text Steganography under Tokenization Inconsistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00382)
*Jiuan Zhou,Yuhao Xue,Yu Cheng,Yuan Xie,Zhaoxia Yin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了TI-StegoAlign，一个基于通道引导的后训练框架，用于解决生成式文本隐写术中发送端与接收端token边界不一致导致恢复率被高估的问题，实现了100%的接收端比特准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式文本隐写术方法在发送端token上评估恢复率，而接收端只能看到表面文本，去token化与重新token化会改变token边界并使编码状态失同步，导致评估高估。现有解决方案依赖推理时过滤或验证，未能使生成策略适应接收端通道。

Method: 提出TI-StegoAlign框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) Bit-Consistent Supervised Objective (BCSO) 在发送端嵌入位置扩大局部编码边际；(2) Channel-Conditioned Preference Optimization (CCPO) 使用接收端真实的恢复质量、文本质量和反隐写分析反馈对齐完整隐写文本。仅更新LoRA参数，通信过程中无需token化特定修正。

Result: 实现100%的接收端比特准确率。与最强基线相比，归一化困惑度偏差降低21.6%，反隐写分析性能相对提升6.3%。

Conclusion: TI-StegoAlign通过通道引导的后训练框架有效解决了发送端与接收端通道失配问题，在不改变推理流程的前提下显著提升了生成式文本隐写术的接收端恢复可靠性和安全性。

Abstract: Generative text steganography enables LLM agents to exchange secret information through task-relevant messages. Yet most methods evaluate recovery on sender-side tokens, whereas the receiver observes only surface text. Detokenization and receiver-side retokenization can alter token boundaries, desynchronize coding states, and cause such evaluation to overestimate receiver-side recovery. Existing remedies rely on inference-time filtering or verification, correcting individual outputs without adapting the generation policy to the receiver-side channel. To address these limitations, we propose TI-StegoAlign, a channel-guided post-training framework. The Bit-Consistent Supervised Objective (BCSO) enlarges local coding margins at realized sender-side embedding positions. Channel-Conditioned Preference Optimization (CCPO) then aligns complete stegotexts using receiver-realistic recovery, text quality, and anti-steganalysis feedback. TI-StegoAlign updates only LoRA parameters and requires no tokenization-specific correction during communication. Experimental results show 100% receiver bit accuracy. Compared with the strongest baselines, TI-StegoAlign achieves a 21.6% reduction in normalized perplexity deviation and a 6.3% relative improvement in anti-steganalysis performance.

</details>


### [55] [Adversarial Attacks in Multi-Agent LLM Pipelines: Unveiling Structural Vulnerabilities in Agentic AI Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00718)
*Faisal Haque Bappy,Tahrim Hossain,Tarannum Shaila Zaman,Raiful Hasan,Kamrul Hasan,Tariqul Islam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出多智能体LLM流水线因其跨智能体信息传递缺乏边界验证，存在结构性安全漏洞，并通过GAIA和SWE-Bench基准的注释生产轨迹证明该漏洞在正常部署中即可出现，且攻击成功率与流水线架构而非模型能力相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM流水线中，中间输出在智能体之间传递时缺乏边界验证机制，一旦某智能体接受对抗性内容，该内容会作为可信输入在整个流水线中传播。这种单智能体设置中不存在的安全缺口导致流水线并非对抗鲁棒的，亟需系统分析和防御。

Method: 利用GAIA和SWE-Bench基准的注释生产轨迹展示漏洞在良性部署中自然出现并规避现有评估框架；在受控多智能体环境中将多种失败模式（内容注入、智能体冒充、计划偏差、记忆投毒）操作化，并在GPT-5-mini、Claude Sonnet 4.5和Kimi K2.5上以相同流水线配置进行评估。

Result: 攻击成功率与流水线结构一致相关而非模型能力，表明对抗性漏洞本质上是架构属性；边界验证作为安全原语缺失是根源，现代流水线嵌入了不可对抗鲁棒的隐式信任假设。

Conclusion: 多智能体流水线的对抗性脆弱性本质上是架构层面的问题，应推动流水线级防御方案，而非仅依赖模型能力提升。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM pipelines orchestrate multiple specialized language model agents into structured workflows where intermediate outputs are passed across agents to solve complex tasks. This design introduces a security gap absent in single-agent settings: once an agent accepts adversarial content, it is propagated as trusted input throughout the pipeline. We argue that this vulnerability stems from the absence of boundary verification, a security primitive that enforces explicit validation of data as it crosses inter-agent boundaries, including content, identity, execution intent, and state integrity. Without such verification, modern pipelines embed implicit trust assumptions that are not adversarially robust, giving rise to structurally distinct attack surfaces (e.g., content injection, agent impersonation, plan deviation, and memory poisoning). Leveraging annotated production traces from the GAIA and SWE-Bench benchmark, we show that these vulnerabilities arise in benign deployments and largely evade existing evaluation frameworks. We further operationalize these failure modes within a controlled multi-agent setting and evaluate them across GPT-5-mini, Claude Sonnet 4.5, and Kimi K2.5 under identical pipeline configurations. The results reveal that attack success aligns with pipeline structure rather than model capability, indicating that adversarial vulnerability is fundamentally an architectural property and motivating a shift toward pipeline-level defenses.

</details>


### [56] [Auditable Release Control for Pedagogical Leakage in LLM Tutors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00515)
*Nizam Kadir*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"教学泄漏"（pedagogical leakage）概念，指大语言模型辅导系统在未经授权的情况下泄露答案或决定性推理过程。作者设计了一个授权感知的完整中介边界系统，通过选择器、策略门控和渲染器三层架构，配合五种披露契约，实现对泄漏的严格管控。实验表明严格中介可将泄漏标记从181降至0（降低30.22个百分点），同时识别出安全-效用权衡前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型辅导系统虽然可能正确且有用，但会在未经授权的情况下提前泄露答案或关键推理步骤，这种状态和动作依赖的失败缺乏正式定义和系统性的防护机制。

Method: 提出授权感知的完整中介边界架构：选择器发出五种披露契约之一，策略门控控制特权模式，渲染器提出语言建议；单一发布函数应用可检查的检查项、可选的累积验证和动作特定的回退机制；可重放痕迹区分选择、生成、验证和执行四类失败；使用匹配组件归因方法分析安全-效用前沿。

Result: 在599个固定Gemini 3.5提议上，严格中介将三模型面板多数泄漏标记从181降至0（配对差异-30.22，95%CI[-35.00,-25.72]），但替换了581个响应并降低了有用性。检查器触发的回退产生11个多数标记；添加语义验证器产生14个。全局A1脚手架产生0个多数和54个任意评审标记，在自动安全和效用上优于拟合Q。外部时间戳复制实验（40个未见问题簇、480个攻击序列）将多数标记从42降至8（-7.08，95%CI[-13.13,-2.29]），但仍有7个失败，1个新引入，平均有用性下降0.192。

Conclusion: 研究建立了可审计的发布边界和基于声明契约的失败归因机制，但明确表示这不等于实现了通用的语义安全或学习效益提升。系统能有效管控教学泄漏，但在有用性和安全性之间存在明确的权衡。

Abstract: Large language model tutors can be correct and helpful yet disclose an answer or decisive reasoning before that disclosure is authorized. We formalize this state- and action-dependent failure as pedagogical leakage and introduce an authorization-aware complete-mediation boundary. A selector emits one of five disclosure contracts, trusted policy gates privileged modes, and a renderer proposes language.
