A single release function applies inspectable checks, optional cumulative verification, and action-specific fallback; replayable traces separate selection, generation, verification, and enforcement failures.
  Matched component attribution exposes a safety-utility frontier. On 599 fixed Gemini 3.5 proposals, strict mediation reduces blinded three-model panel-majority leakage flags from 181 to 0 (paired problem-cluster difference -30.22 points, 95% CI [-35.00,-25.72]), while replacing 581 responses and lowering helpfulness. Checker-triggered fallback alone yields 11 majority flags; adding the semantic verifier yields 14 and no reliable marginal gain. A global A1 scaffold yields 0 majority and 54 any-judge flags, outperforming fitted Q on automatic safety and utility. In an externally timestamped replication over 40 unseen problem clusters and 480 attack sequences, high-assurance release reduces majority flags from 42 to 8 (-7.08 points, 95% CI [-13.13,-2.29]); seven failures persist, one is introduced, and mean helpfulness falls by .192. These results establish an auditable release boundary and failure attribution under declared contracts, not universal semantic safety or learning gains.

</details>


### [57] [Neuro-Symbolic Participation Governance for Verifiable AI Agents in Open Digital Twin Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00937)
*Juan Li,Wei Cai,Yan Bai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号去中心化治理框架，用于数字孪生环境中可验证自主AI代理的系统。通过多层语义配置文件桥接概率神经推理与确定性机构治理，并利用区块链智能合约验证凭据，确保跨机构安全协作，原型实验表明能有效阻止未授权交互且开销可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的自主AI代理多智能体系统在去中心化跨机构环境中缺乏身份、能力和策略合规性的稳健验证机制，难以支撑高风险的数字孪生决策支持场景。

Method: 提出神经符号去中心化治理框架：1) 通过多层语义配置文件表示代理，将概率神经推理与确定性机构治理相结合；2) 基于形式化领域本体对能力进行落地，实现机器可解释、策略感知和上下文敏感的参与；3) 由组织权威机构签发凭据，通过区块链智能合约验证，实现可审计参与且不泄露敏感数据。

Result: 在包含诊所、数字孪生和可穿戴设备提供者代理的决策支持原型中验证，框架有效防止了未授权交互并强制执行机构策略，同时保持了可管理的系统开销。

Conclusion: 神经符号去中心化治理为跨机构边界的安全人机协作提供了一条可扩展且可信的路径，支持可信赖的人机协作和有意义的监督。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents, increasingly empowered by large language models, are becoming important components of human-machine systems for high-stakes decision support in digital twin ecosystems. However, existing multi-agent systems often lack robust verification for identity, capability, and policy compliance, especially in decentralized environments spanning multiple institutions. This paper proposes a neuro-symbolic decentralized governance framework for verifiable agents in collaborative digital twin environments. By representing agents through multi-layer semantic profiles, the framework bridges probabilistic neural reasoning with deterministic institutional governance, thereby supporting trustworthy human-AI collaboration and meaningful human oversight. Capabilities are grounded in formal domain ontologies to enable machine-interpretable, policy-aware, and context-sensitive participation. These credentials, issued by organizational authorities, are validated via blockchain-based smart contracts, ensuring auditable participation without exposing sensitive data. We demonstrate the framework using a decision-support prototype with clinic, digital twin, and wearable provider agents effectively prevents unauthorized interaction and enforces institutional policies with manageable overhead. Our findings suggest that neuro-symbolic decentralized governance provides a scalable and trustworthy pathway for safe human-machine collaboration across institutional boundaries.

</details>


### [58] [Exploring Usability and Legal Practice: Insights from German Judicial Users of Digital Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00541)
*Tobias Hoppmann,Leona Lassak,M. Angela Sasse,Zinaida Benenson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过调查德国北莱茵-威斯特法伦州101名司法从业者（检察官、法官和数字取证专家），将数字取证置于刑事司法程序的社会技术系统中进行考察，发现工作流程整合、跨领域沟通和利益相关者期望对齐存在显著改进需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注数字取证的工具和方法，忽视了其更广泛的程序性和组织性背景。随着数据量增长和技术演进，数字取证在刑事程序中的有效整合仍面临挑战，需要从人机交互和以人为中心的安全视角重新审视。

Method: 采用社会技术系统视角，对德国北莱茵-威斯特法伦州司法系统中的101名从业者（包括检察官、法官和数字取证专家）进行问卷调查。

Result: 调查结果显示从业者对数字取证与工作流程的更好整合有强烈需求，要求改进跨领域沟通，并使利益相关者的期望更紧密对齐。同时发现数字取证可用性存在巨大改进潜力，包括加强跨学科合作、更便捷快速地访问证据数据和结果，以及改善从业者培训和教育。

Conclusion: 数字取证应被视为刑事程序中的社会技术系统组成部分，其有效整合依赖于技术工具与人类组织流程的协同改进，而不仅仅是工具本身的优化。

Abstract: Digital forensics has become an integral part of modern criminal proceedings, yet its effective integration remains challenging because of increasing data volumes, evolving technologies, and complex interactions between technical and legal stakeholders. Although prior work has focused primarily on digital forensic tools and methods, its broader procedural and organizational context has received limited attention. Building on emerging perspectives inspired by usability research and human-centered security, we conceptualize digital forensics as part of a socio-technical system within criminal proceedings. We consequently investigate this perspective through a survey of 101 practitioners from the judiciary of the German federal state of North Rhine-Westphalia, including public prosecutors, judges, and digital forensic experts. The results indicate a strong demand for improved integration of digital forensics into workflows, enhanced cross-domain communication, and a closer alignment of stakeholder expectations. They also uncover great potential for the improvement of digital forensics usability, e.g., through stronger interdisciplinary cooperation, easier and faster access to evidential data and results, or improvement of stakeholder training and education.

</details>


### [59] [Robust Watermarks Meet Backdoored Models: Evading Diffusion Semantic Watermarks via Stealthy Backdoor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00543)
*Jinyuan Liu,Tianshuo Cong,Pei Li,Tianrui Wang,Xinlei He,Anyu Wang,Xiaoyun Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GhostVAE，通过在VAE编码器中植入隐蔽后门，使潜在扩散模型（LDM）生成的图像能够可靠地逃避语义水印检测。该方法在良性图像上保持94.4%的检出率，同时在触发条件下实现94.6%的攻击成功率，并能在17种防御方法下保持隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义水印被认为是LDM生成图像的重要保护手段，但其检测流程依赖神经网络，这引入了一个被忽视但关键的投毒/后门攻击面。作者旨在系统性地研究这一安全漏洞，揭示语义水印系统在神经网络组件层面的安全隐患。

Method: GhostVAE采用两阶段策略：首先通过功率谱正则化构造通用触发器以提升触发器鲁棒性，然后使用参数对齐目标训练被植入后门的VAE编码器，使后门在输入空间、参数空间和隐空间中均保持隐蔽性。

Result: 在三种先进语义水印方案和三种主流LDM上的评估中，GhostVAE在良性图像上平均真阳性率94.4%，触发激活时平均攻击成功率94.6%；对17种代表性防御方法的综合分析表明其在输入空间、参数空间和隐空间均保持隐蔽。

Conclusion: 该工作从根本上削弱了语义水印系统的可信度，强调安全部署语义水印需要端到端的安全考量，尤其要关注神经网络组件的安全防护。

Abstract: Although semantic watermarking is considered a promising safeguard for images generated by Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs), the reliance of the watermark detection pipeline on neural networks introduces a critical yet underexplored backdoor attack surface. To systematically study this vulnerability, we propose GhostVAE to plant a stealthy backdoor into the encoder of Variational Autoencoder (VAE), enabling reliable evasion of watermark detection. GhostVAE operates in two stages: it first constructs a universal trigger via power spectrum regularization to improve the trigger robustness, and then trains a backdoored VAE encoder with a parameter-aligned objective. Through extensive evaluations across three state-of-the-art semantic watermarking schemes and three widely adopted LDMs, we show that GhostVAE preserves watermark detection performance on benign images (achieving an average true positive rate of 94.4%), while simultaneously enabling highly effective evasion under trigger activation (achieving an average attack success rate of 94.6%). Moreover, we comprehensively analyze seventeen representative defenses and demonstrate that GhostVAE remains stealthy across the input space, parameter space, and latent space. Our work fundamentally undermines the trustworthiness of semantic watermarking systems and highlights that secure deployment of semantic watermarks requires end-to-end security considerations, particularly for neural network components.

</details>


### [60] [A False Average: Chain-of-Thought Monitors Collapse Where They Are the Only Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00583)
*Shikhar Shiromani,Leo Richter*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现，当攻击者能够控制推理过程时，只需重写推理文本使其看起来像正常工程行为（但保留所有命令和输出不变），就能将监测器对"仅靠推理暴露攻击"子集的捕捉率从约95%降至11%以下，且无需梯度。这种攻击可跨监测器和代理模型迁移，并可在真实代理上复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思维（CoT）监测本意是捕捉那些在行动上表现正常、仅在推理中暴露痕迹的奖励黑客行为。作者想检验当攻击者完全控制推理过程时，这种监测手段是否仍可靠。

Method: 采用无梯度的单次攻击方法：仅重写智能体的推理文本使其读起来像善意的工程行为，同时逐字复制所有命令和输出，保持利用行为不变。测试了跨监测器家族、跨代理模型的迁移性，用真实代理复现，并尝试了仅基于轨迹的防御措施和基于可解释性的探针分析。

Result: 攻击将监测器在相关子集上的捕捉率从约95%降至11%以下；聚合准确率因被其他容易从行动中识别的攻击主导而呈现虚假的偏高假象；攻击跨监测器和代理模型转移；仅轨迹的防御只能部分恢复检测能力；虽然探针能分离被漏掉的攻击，但因果控制表明这只是检测器而非监测器内部知晓的证据。

Conclusion: CoT监测在面对能控制推理的对手时本质上不可靠，特别是当攻击仅由推理暴露时，监测近乎完全失效。仅靠轨迹信息的防御无法充分解决该问题，需要轨迹之外的信息才能实质性提升检测能力。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is meant to catch the reward hacks that look clean in the actions and betray themselves only in the reasoning. We show that this is exactly where an adversary who controls the reasoning can defeat it. Rewriting only an agent's reasoning to read as good-faith engineering, while copying every command and output verbatim so the exploit is unchanged, drops a held-out monitor's catch rate on that subset from about 95% to under 11% in one gradient-free shot. A monitor's aggregate accuracy is a false average: dominated by hacks the actions give away, it hides the near-total collapse this rewrite produces on the subset where CoT monitoring is the only signal. The attack transfers across monitor families and agent models, reproduces with live agents, though against a calibrated monitor evasion concentrates in the strongest agent. Trace-only defenses recover it only partially, even one primed on the attack, because the rewrite stays truthful about what happened and lies only about intent; only information from outside the trace helps substantially. A probe on an open-weight surrogate monitor's activations separates the hacks its verdict misses, but a causal control shows this is a detector, not evidence the monitor secretly knows.

</details>


### [61] [Domain Decoupling Attack: Exploiting the Validation Gap Between Protective DNS and Shared Edge Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00643)
*Weizhe Wang,Minhong Dong,Jinhao Li,Yao Zhang,Hao Liu,Qiang Hu,Tao Luo,Guangquan Xu,Bin Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出域解耦攻击（DDA），利用DNS授权验证缺陷，使攻击者通过对允许域名的解析获得共享边缘IP的访问权限，然后在TLS SNI和HTTP Host中一致地呈现隐藏域名，绕过CDN和非CDN共享托管环境中的访问控制。大规模测量显示总体暴露率高达95.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CDN逃避技术依赖SNI-Host不一致、域名所有权验证不足或特定供应商路由重写等，在现代CDN环境中适用范围有限。作者识别出DNS授权中的验证盲区：允许域名的权限被错误地应用于共享IP，可被复用访问其他租户，需要系统性地揭示这一安全风险。

Method: 提出域解耦攻击（DDA）：先解析允许域名获得共享边缘IP的权限，随后对同一地址发起连接，同时在TLS SNI和HTTP Host中一致地呈现隐藏域名。跨六大洲对1,069,048个域名进行大规模测量，并结合实验室实验验证DNS绑定访问控制的结构性局限。

Result: 共完成18,025,068次成功探测，整体暴露率95.8%，其中CDN域名为99.26%，非CDN域名为92.75%，非CDN跨租户IP为97.7%。实验室实验揭示了DNS绑定访问控制对共享地址的结构性缺陷。

Conclusion: 研究结果阐明了DNS派生IP授权的安全风险，揭示了DNS绑定访问控制在共享地址场景下的根本局限，为CDN和非CDN共享托管环境中访问控制机制的评估与改进提供了依据。

Abstract: Network attackers often conceal malicious communication within legitimate Internet traffic. Existing CDN-based evasion techniques rely on SNI--Host inconsistency, insufficient domain ownership verification, or provider-specific routing rewrites, which limit their applicability in modern CDN environments. We identify a validation gap in DNS-based authorization, where permission derived from an allowed domain applies to a shared IP and can be reused to reach another tenant in both CDN and non-CDN shared-hosting environments. This paper presents the Domain Decoupling Attack (DDA), which resolves an allowed domain to obtain permission for a shared edge IP and subsequently connects to the same address while presenting the hidden domain consistently in both TLS SNI and HTTP Host. Measurements of 1,069,048 domains across six continents produce 18,025,068 successful probes and identify exposure rates of 95.8% overall, 99.26% for CDN domains, 92.75% for non-CDN domains, and 97.7% for non-CDN cross-tenant IPs, while laboratory experiments reveal a structural limitation of DNS-bound access control on shared addresses. These results clarify the security risks of DNS-derived IP authorization and support the evaluation and improvement of access-control mechanisms in CDN and non-CDN shared-hosting environments.

</details>


### [62] [Improving the Security of Containerized Workloads using Transparency and Traceability Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00660)
*Nikos Fotiou,Lefteris Georgiadis,Ignacio Lacalle,George C. Polyzos,Vasilios A. Siris*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可验证容器镜像分发架构，通过透明服务生成一次性签名密钥并通过只能追加的透明注册表记录签名事件，在部署准入时利用政策即代码进行验证，确保只有符合合规要求的工件才能被部署，并已在GitHub Actions和GitLab Runners上实现概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容器化工作负载通常通过CI/CD流水线构建并跨异构基础设施（云端和边缘）执行，单一的签名步骤或凭据泄露就可能导致恶意工件的大规模分发，现有的完整性、透明性和部署时检查机制存在不足，尤其面临密钥管理挑战。

Method: 提出一个可验证容器镜像分发架构：透明服务生成与认证身份绑定的一次性签名密钥，将签名事件记录在只能追加的透明注册表中，并返回可加密验证的包含证明；这些证明和身份属性被附加到镜像元数据中，在准入时由政策即代码（policy-as-code）进行评估。

Result: 实现了与GitHub Actions和GitLab Runners集成的概念验证系统，并在现实威胁模型下评估了该流水线对常见供应链攻击的缓解效果。

Conclusion: 该架构通过一次性密钥绑定身份、透明注册表记录签名事件、以及准入时政策验证，有效增强了容器镜像分发的安全性和供应链攻击防御能力。

Abstract: Containerized workloads are commonly built via CI/CD pipelines, stored in registries, and executed across heterogeneous infrastructures, including cloud and edge environments. A single compromised build step or credential can turn routine automation into large-scale distribution of malicious artifacts, motivating integrity, transparency, and enforceable deployment-time checks. In this paper, we present an architecture for verifiable container image distribution that addresses key-management challenges and enables policy-enforced admission-time verification. A transparency service generates one-time signing keys bound to authenticated identities, records signing events in an append-only transparency registry, and returns cryptographically verifiable proofs of inclusion. These proofs and identity attributes are attached to image metadata and evaluated by policy-as-code at admission time, so only compliant artifacts are deployed. We implement a proof-of-concept integrated with GitHub Actions and GitLab Runners and evaluate how the resulting pipeline mitigates common supply-chain attacks under a realistic threat model.

</details>


### [63] [From Chasing Ghosts to Missed Attacks: Perspectives and Perceptions of SOC Practitioners on LLM Integration, Risks, and Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00672)
*Jonas Thurner,Nadine Jost,Stefan Albert Horstmann,Fabian Ising,Lea Groeber,Alena Naiakshina,Sebastian Schinzel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 通过对25名有LLM使用经验的SOC从业者进行半结构化访谈，识别出15个LLM用例并归类为六大功能类别，发现LLM擅长自动化重复性低级任务（如报告自动化），但在高影响任务（如事件分析）上仍不可行，主要受限于技术深度、上下文感知和组织特定知识的不足。从业者虽对模型局限有认知，但面临竞争压力，仍强烈愿意采用LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心（SOC）需要处理大量安全事件，分析人员需在时间压力下准确检测和评估攻击。尽管LLM在安全运营方面显示出潜在优势，但其在真实SOC工作流程中的实际适用性仍未被充分理解。

Method: 采用质性研究方法，对25名有LLM使用经验的SOC从业者进行半结构化访谈，并结合交互式场景来预判挑战并识别LLM工具负责任融入SOC工作流程的机会。

Result: 识别出15个LLM用例，归入六大功能类别。LLM在自动化重复性低级任务（如报告自动化）方面受重视，但如事件分析等高影响任务被认为尚不可行，存在技术深度、上下文感知和组织特定知识方面的不足。局限更多源于SOC自身的准备度和导致过度依赖的人为因素，而非模型本身。尽管存在担忧，从业者因竞争压力表现出强烈的采用意愿。

Conclusion: 本文提供了基于实证的、从业者驱动的LLM在SOC角色和组织中使用的分析，并提出了以人为中心、操作安全的LLM辅助安全运营的具体设计和集成要求。

Abstract: Security Operations Centers (SOCs) process large volumes of security events, requiring analysts to accurately detect and assess ongoing cyberattacks under time pressure. Recent advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) suggest potential benefits for security operations, yet their practical suitability for real-world SOC workflows remains poorly understood. To address this gap, we conducted 25 semi-structured interviews with SOC practitioners who had prior experience with LLMs, complemented by interactive scenarios to anticipate challenges and identify opportunities for the responsible integration of LLM-based tools into SOC workflows. We identified 15 LLM use cases grouped into six functional categories. While LLMs are valued for automating repetitive, low-level tasks such as report automation, practitioners rate high-impact tasks such as incident analysis as not yet feasible, reporting limitations in technical depth, context awareness, and organization-specific knowledge. They locate these limitations less in the models than in the readiness of their SOCs and human factors driving over-reliance. Despite concerns, practitioners express a strong willingness to adopt LLMs, describing competitive pressure that leaves few alternatives. This work contributes an empirical, practitioner-driven analysis of LLM use across SOC roles and organizations and derives concrete design and integration requirements for human-centered, operationally safe LLM-assisted security operations.

</details>


### [64] [Supporting Cybersecurity Risk Management for Medical Devices via the SECUMAN Ontology and Shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00698)
*Martin Diller,Anne Esslinger,Piotr Gorczyca,Evi Hartig,Lia Kacholdt,Hannes Strass*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了SECUMAN本体和形状约束，用于表示和分析医疗设备的网络安全风险管理文档，支持自动化验证和审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联网医疗设备的网络安全风险日益重要且可能直接影响患者安全，但现有风险管理文件通常以半结构化的自然语言文本维护，导致一致性检查、认证审查和重用困难。

Method: 构建基于OWL的SECUMAN本体，用于建模安全风险背景、评估、控制措施和残余风险评价；利用SHACL约束检查文档的结构完整性和模型符合性；与VDE Spec 90025及RISKMAN本体对齐，并扩展至威胁场景、保护目标、攻击者画像、暴露等级、资产和安全设计论证等网络安全相关概念。

Result: SECUMAN支持自动化首轮验证、可追溯性以及网络安全与安全风险管理文档的整合。 

Conclusion: SECUMAN提供了一种正式的、机器可读的框架，能够提升医疗设备网络安全风险管理文档的一致性、审查效率和可重用性，弥补了现有安全导向方法在网络安全方面的不足。

Abstract: We propose the SECUMAN ontology and shapes for representing and analysing cybersecurity risk-management documentation for medical devices. Cybersecurity risks are increasingly relevant for connected medical devices and may have direct consequences for patient safety. Current risk-management files are often maintained as semi-structured natural language text, which makes consistency checking, certification review, and reuse difficult. SECUMAN provides a formal OWL-based vocabulary for modelling security-risk context, assessment, control measures, and residual-risk evaluation, and uses SHACL constraints to check structural completeness and conformity with the intended documentation model. The ontology is aligned with VDE Spec 90025 and the related RISKMAN ontology and shapes, while extending their safety-oriented approach to concepts relevant to cybersecurity risk documentation such as threat scenarios, protection goals, attacker profiles, exposure levels, assets, and secure design arguments. SECUMAN is intended to support automated first-pass validation, traceability, and integration of cybersecurity and safety risk-management documentation.

</details>


### [65] [Safety Invariants for Agents Orchestrating Irreversible State Transitions: A Four-Dimensional Formalism Evaluated on Public Ledgers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00783)
*Zhaoming Yin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了自主代理在外部系统上产生不可逆副作用的安全性问题，以公共账本上的价值转移为形式化场景，提出"执行保真度"定理及其七项安全不变量，并通过对抗性实验验证了该安全栈的有效性，同时揭示单模型评估的不可证伪性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理框架（如ReAct、Reflexion、MCP）在基准测试上优化任务成功率的优化，却很少关注不可逆副作用的安全性。然而自主代理越来越多地被要求对传输资金、写入持久存储、驱动硬件等外部系统产生不可逆影响，安全保证的缺失构成了重大风险。

Method: 将跨公共账本的价值转移形式化为以（钱包、链、地址、协议）为索引的四维空间中的状态转移，基于此形式化定义并证明了"执行保真度"保证。该保证在特定的故障模型下确保会话的账本实现效果要么为空、要么精确且恰好一次地执行渲染给用户的转移。从保真度条件推导出七项安全不变量，而非从经验枚举，并在N=60的对抗性测试套件中进行了实证评估。

Result: 在对抗性套件上，该安全栈相比朴素ReAct基线在两个写激进的后端模型上通过率提升约74个百分点，但在写谨慎的模型上仅提升约3个百分点，表明单一模型评估的安全栈几乎不可证伪。系统已实际部署，108次跨8条链的生产环境写操作支撑了故障分类法。

Conclusion: 该工作通过形式化保证和系统化不变量显著提升了代理执行不可逆操作的安全性，并指出模型间差异巨大的评估结果意味着代理安全评估需要覆盖多样化的模型。虽然具体评估环境是公共账本，但所提出的形式化和不变量适用于任何作用于不可逆外部状态的概率性代理。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are increasingly asked to produce irreversible effects on external systems - transferring funds, writing to durable storage, actuating hardware. Existing agent frameworks (ReAct, Reflexion, MCP) optimize task success on benchmarks and give little attention to the safety of irreversible side-effects. We formalize one such setting, movement of value across public ledgers, as state transitions in a four-dimensional space indexed by (wallet, chain, address, protocol), and use that formalism to state and prove a guarantee we call execution fidelity: under a fault model admitting planner mis-mapping, ambiguous outcomes, retries, at-least-once delivery, and delegated non-human callers, a session's realized effect on the ledger is either nothing at all or exactly the transition that was rendered to the user, exactly once. The theorem deliberately does not claim that the rendered transition matches the user's intent - no runtime layer can decide that - but it confines that unbounded question to a single predicate over a finite object, which is what makes a preview a sufficient control rather than a formality. Seven safety invariants, derived from the fidelity condition rather than enumerated from experience, discharge the guarantee. Empirically, on a controlled N=60 adversarial suite the stack lifts pass rate by ~74 percentage points over a naive-ReAct baseline on two write-aggressive backing models, but by only ~3 points on a write-cautious one - evidence that single-model evaluations of agent safety stacks are close to unfalsifiable. The system is deployed; 108 production write operations across 8 chains back the failure taxonomy. Although the evaluation setting is public ledgers, the formalism and invariants apply to any probabilistic agent acting on irreversible external state.

</details>


### [66] [Hardware-rooted attestation for AI-agent evidence: composing IETF RATS with action evidence packages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00801)
*Anton Sokolov*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出将硬件信任根（RATS架构/Veraison）与软件层行动证据包（AEP）绑定，形成复合证明，以解决AI代理行为验证中"模型版本是否真实部署"的问题，并在模拟TPM上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AEP只能证明AI代理声称做了什么，但无法回答"操作者声称部署的特定模型版本是否真的在未修改硬件上产生了该输出"这一关键问题，缺少硬件信任根。

Method: 提出复合证明方案：在RATS架构下评估硬件证据，并与软件AEP绑定。通过输出绑定协议，将AEP结果的哈希和验证者随机数折入由证明密钥签名的quote中，模型工件测量值存入平台寄存器。使用swtpm模拟器进行实验验证。

Result: 在模拟TPM上验证了三种平台结果的判定：良好且新鲜的quote判定为Attested，模型测量值被替换时判定为Contested，陈旧quote重放时判定为Expired，同时能拒绝绑定有效quote的伪造AEP结果。

Conclusion: 该方案在模拟硬件上证明了可行性，但仍只是仿真平台的验证，尚不能提供硬件根保证，需在真实TPM硬件上进一步验证。

Abstract: An action evidence package (AEP) is a signed, append-only record of what an AI agent did, who or what authorised the action, and what the outcome was. It is a software-layer artefact: it tells a verifier the story of an action as the agent's own runtime reports it. This note argues that software attestation of this kind is necessary but not sufficient. When a verifier's question shifts from "what does the agent claim it did?" to "did the specific model version the operator claims to have deployed actually produce this output, on unmodified hardware?", the AEP alone cannot answer. The missing element is a hardware root of trust: an attestation that the measured boot and runtime state of the platform match an endorsed reference. The IETF Remote Attestation Procedures (RATS) architecture (RFC 9334) and Veraison, an open-source RATS Verifier implementation (Confidential Computing Consortium / Linux Foundation), supply exactly this. We propose a composite attestation: hardware Evidence appraised under RATS, bound to a software AEP. We map a small verifier vocabulary (Authorised / Unauthorised / Indeterminate / Attested / Contested / Expired) onto RATS appraisal outcomes, and demonstrate feasibility with a small executed experiment: on a software Trusted Platform Module (TPM; the swtpm emulator), an output-binding protocol folds the hash of an AEP outcome and a fresh appraiser nonce into an attestation-key-signed quote, with a model-artefact measurement carried in a platform register. A minimal RATS-Verifier stand-in resolves the three platform outcomes as designed -- Attested for a good, fresh quote; Contested when the model measurement is swapped; Expired when a stale quote is replayed -- and rejects a forged AEP outcome bound to a valid quote. The result is a feasibility demonstration on emulated hardware, not a hardware-rooted guarantee.

</details>


### [67] [XR-PRISM: Data-Driven Privacy and Risk Impact Scoring Metric for Extended Reality in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00826)
*Nafisa Anjum,M. Rasel Mahmud*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文综述了2017至2024年间65项关于扩展现实（XR）安全与隐私的同行评审研究，构建了一个四层威胁分类体系（设备、网络、用户、云层），并提出了XR-PRISM评分指标——一个六因素加权的隐私与风险影响评分模型，用于量化、比较和优先排序医疗XR部署中的安全与隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展现实技术在医疗领域广泛应用，但其广泛的感知能力和复杂的数据管道带来了独特的安全、隐私和安全隐患。现有研究缺乏一个统一的定量框架来评估和优先处理这些风险，因此需要建立系统化的风险评估方法。

Method: 作者对2017-2024年间发表的65篇关于XR安全与隐私的同行评审研究进行了系统综述，综合出一个包含设备层、网络层、用户层和云层的四层威胁分类体系及对应的防御目录。在此基础上，提出了XR-PRISM指标，该指标整合了威胁可能性、系统脆弱性、攻击面、安全影响、隐私影响和控制有效性六个加权因素，生成一个可操作的综合风险评分。

Result: 分析发现超过70%的已识别对策缺乏标准化的风险评估，而只有不到15%的有记录的攻击需要高水平的专业执行技能。XR-PRISM为研究人员和实践者提供了一种透明、数据驱动的方法来比较、优先处理和缓解医疗XR部署中的安全与隐私风险。

Conclusion: XR-PRISM弥补了医疗XR领域缺乏统一定量风险评估框架的空白，通过可量化的评分方法帮助研究者和实践者更有效地识别和优先处理安全与隐私威胁，但也反映了当前研究中风险标准化评估的普遍不足。

Abstract: Extended Reality (XR) technologies are transforming healthcare through immersive training, remote consultation, and patient rehabilitation. However, their extensive sensing capabilities and complex data pipelines introduce distinct security, privacy, and safety risks. Existing research lacks a unified quantitative framework for assessing and prioritizing these risks. We review 65 peer-reviewed studies on XR security and privacy published from 2017 to 2024, synthesizing a four-layer threat taxonomy consisting of Device, Network, User, and Cloud layers, along with a corresponding catalog of defenses. Building on this analysis, we introduce XR-PRISM, a six-factor weighted Privacy and Risk Impact Scoring Metric that integrates threat likelihood, system vulnerability, attack surface, safety impact, privacy impact, and control effectiveness into a single actionable risk score. Our analysis shows that more than 70% of the identified countermeasures lack standardized risk evaluation, while fewer than 15% of the documented attacks require a high level of expertise to execute. XR-PRISM provides researchers and practitioners with a transparent, data-driven method for comparing, prioritizing, and mitigating security and privacy risks in healthcare XR deployments.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [68] [Width, Memory, and Delay: A Resource Accounting for the Limits of Flat Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00028)
*Oleksandr Kuznetsov,Emanuele Frontoni*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了多智能体系统中增加智能体数量能否克服性能极限的问题。作者提出三维资源模型（种群宽度N、每智能体内在模型记忆d、观测延迟τ上的预测），通过可控扰动抑制测试平台证明：平坦同构多智能体系统的性能上限不取决于架构层级，而取决于每智能体的内部模型内容；三种资源不可完全互换；残余性能下限由观测延迟和环境不可预测性决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对近期预印本声称"平坦同构多智能体系统面临不可约的种群无关'因果下限'，只能通过层级化组织克服"的过于强硬的结论，作者希望用精确可计算最优解的控制实验来反驳这一说法，并提出更定量的资源模型。

Method: 使用可控扰动抑制测试平台（具备精确可计算的最优解），建立宽度N、记忆d、延迟τ三种资源模型。通过对比平坦同构群体（带匹配内部模型）与双层级联层级结构，并进行等总状态预算比较、在线学习替代oracle知识、以及非线性与空间扩展植物下的鲁棒性检验。

Result: (i) 平坦同构群体若每智能体携带匹配的扰动内部模型，在同等每智能体记忆下可匹配或超越双层层级结构——时间深度可以是动态（循环记忆）而非架构性的；(ii) 三种资源不可互相完全互换，绘制了宽度×记忆交换率图及硬性不可交换边界；(iii) 残余性能下限由观测延迟和环境在该时域上的不可预测性决定。

Conclusion: 层级架构并非克服性能下限的必要条件；每智能体的内部模型内容才是关键。作者量化了在线学习替代oracle知识的代价，并通过初步鲁棒性检验验证了结论的稳定性，最终为实践者提炼了四条设计规则。

Abstract: A recurring question in the design of scalable multi-agent systems -- from robot swarms to collectives of large-language-model (LLM) agents -- is whether adding more agents can, on its own, overcome performance limits, or whether a qualitatively \emph{deeper} organization is required. A recent preprint argues that flat, homogeneous multi-agent systems face an irreducible, population-independent ``causal floor'' on achievable error, removable only by hierarchical (nested-loop) organization. Using a controlled disturbance-rejection testbed with an exactly computable optimum, we show this conclusion is too strong and replace it with a quantitative resource model built on three resources: population \emph{width} $N$, per-agent internal-model \emph{memory} $d$, and prediction across the observation \emph{delay} $τ$. We establish three claims. (i) The achievable floor is governed not by architectural hierarchy but by per-agent internal-model content: a flat, homogeneous swarm whose agents carry a matched internal model of the disturbance matches or beats a designed two-loop hierarchy at equal per-agent memory -- so temporal depth can be dynamical (recurrent memory), not architectural (nesting). (ii) The three resources are \emph{not mutually interchangeable}; we chart the exchange rates and the hard non-exchange boundaries on an explicit width$\times$memory map, including a strict equal-total-state-budget comparison. (iii) A residual floor is set by the observation delay and the environment's unpredictability over that horizon, which we verify against the optimal controller. We quantify the price of replacing oracle knowledge of the disturbance spectrum with online learning, provide a preliminary robustness check against a mild bounded nonlinearity and a spatially-extended plant, and distill four design rules for practitioners.

</details>


### [69] [MAPLE-Guard: Memory-Aware Link Enforcement Against Memory-Link Poisoning in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00426)
*Wenjun Xiong,Yijin Zhou,Jiaqian Wang,Shangding Gu,Bo Tang,Zhiyu Li,Feiyu Xiong,Ying Wen,Muning Wen*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出MAPLE-Guard，一种针对基于LLM的多智能体系统（MAS）中持久化记忆投毒攻击的记忆链路防御机制。通过在记忆生命周期的写入、检索、提升和跨智能体重用四个阶段设置门控，阻断被污染的私有/共享记忆在多智能体间传播，在LongMemEval和AppWorld基准上将攻击成功率从38.2%/34.7%分别降至0.9%/0.2%，并将防御成功率提升至74.3%/99.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体系统防御主要关注提示词、动作或通信边的安全检查，但忽略了记忆层的持久性投毒风险。一次写入的恶意记忆可被持续检索、提升为共享记忆并被其他智能体复用，且攻击内容在写入时看似良性、待检索后才产生危害，导致现有防御手段难以检测此类攻击。

Method: 提出Memory-Aware Propagation and Link Enforcement Guard（MAPLE-Guard），在记忆生命周期四个关键节点设置防御门控：写入时隔离风险记忆、检索时过滤不安全结果、防止毒化私有记忆进入共享记忆，并阻断跨智能体复用传播路径，构建从源头到复用的全链路记忆防护。

Result: 在LongMemEval上，攻击成功率从38.2%降至0.9%，多智能体防御成功率从54.0%提升至74.3%；在AppWorld上，攻击成功率从34.7%降至0.2%，多智能体防御成功率从42.5%提升至99.8%。与仅检查提示词和拓扑结构的基线防御相比，效果显著更优。

Conclusion: 记忆感知的链路防御能够有效填补提示级和拓扑级防御的盲区，证明在多智能体系统中，将防御边界扩展到记忆生命周期管理是抵御持久性记忆投毒攻击的关键路径。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) increasingly rely on persistent private and shared memories for long-horizon coordination. This memory layer improves continuity, but it also gives attackers a durable channel: a poisoned memory can be written once, continuously retrieved in later tasks, promoted into shared memory, and reused by other agents. A single poisoned write can therefore steer many later decisions and contaminate agents that never saw the original attack, all while no malicious message crosses a visible communication edge at the moment of harm. Further, because existing safeguards mainly inspect prompts, actions, or communication edges, they can miss attacks whose content appears benign at write time but becomes harmful after retrieval. We introduce Memory-Aware Propagation and Link Enforcement Guard, MAPLE-Guard, a memory-link guard for memory-enabled MAS. MAPLE-Guard monitors the memory lifecycle and places gates at write, retrieval, promotion, and cross-agent reuse, so risky memories can be quarantined, unsafe retrievals filtered, and poisoned private memories blocked before they enter shared memory. In the main evaluation, MAPLE-Guard lowers attack success rate (ASR) from 38.2% to 0.9% on LongMemEval and from 34.7% to 0.2% on AppWorld; it also raises multi-agent defense success rate (MDSR) from 54.0% to 74.3% and from 42.5% to 99.8% on the same benchmarks. These results suggest that memory-aware link enforcement covers a gap left by prompt-level and topology-level defenses. Code is available at the link: https://github.com/xiong-wenjun/MAPLE-Guard.

</details>


### [70] [BANDMAS: Causality-Inspired Semantic Packet Scheduling for Bandwidth-Efficient Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00458)
*Jiangwen Dong,Wanyu Lin*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: BANDMAS是一个多智能体协作框架，通过将智能体间通信建模为任务导向流量，并利用因果启发的回放估值来选择性传输数据包，从而在保持任务性能的同时大幅减少应用层流量和推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统通过消息交换进行决策，但转发所有消息会导致输入token数量激增，增加推理延迟和计算开销。已有的智能体剪枝或消息丢弃方法要么导致证据不足，要么仍含有冗余传输，无法有效解决通信效率与决策质量的矛盾。

Method: BANDMAS将智能体间通信建模为任务导向流量。具体地，将消息按照语义特征（如证据和请求）分解为多个数据包，仅当预测的回放贡献超过资源成本时才会传输这些数据包，并在带宽、延迟、截止时间和接收方上下文等约束下自适应调度通信包。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B流量下，BANDMAS在SciFact、HotpotQA和FanOutQA三个基准上减少了53.2%至77.3%的应用层字节数，并在所有三个工作负载上达到了受限方法中最高的平均任务指标。

Conclusion: BANDMAS通过数据包级粒度的选择性传输，在显著降低通信开销的同时保持甚至提升了任务性能，为大规模LLM多智能体系统的高效协作提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems make decisions based on the aggregated information via exchanging messages across specialized agents. Forwarding every generated message among agents increases application-layer traffic. Yet, it introduces tremendous input tokens for agent processing, potentially raising inference latency and computational overhead. Existing approaches attempt to address the above issues by pruning agents or discarding redundant messages. Nevertheless, such agent-level or message-level optimization results in insufficient evidence supporting for final decisions or still containing redundant message transmissions. To address these challenges, we propose BANDMAS, a multi-agent collaboration framework that models inter-agent communications as task-oriented traffic, which enables efficient transmission via causality-inspired replay valuation. Specifically, we decompose messages into several data packets by analyzing their semantic features such as evidence and requests. The system only transmits these packets if their predicted replay-derived contribution exceeds their resource cost. Consequently, BANDMAS is able to adaptively schedule communication packets while adhering to bandwidth, latency, deadline, and receiver context constraints. On frozen Qwen3-4B traffic across SciFact, HotpotQA, and FanOutQA, our framework reduces application-layer bytes by 53.2\% to 77.3\% at selected caps and attains the highest mean task metric among constrained methods on all three workloads.

</details>


### [71] [MDGAM-Based Cooperative Task Scheduling for Communication-Constrained Distributed Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00648)
*Licheng Wang,Mingtao Huang,Yuan Shen*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向通信受限分布式多机器人任务分配（MRTA）的神经调度框架，包含多解码器图注意力模型（MDGAM）策略网络和无评论家的组相对多智能体策略梯度（GRMAPG）训练算法，在多种问题规模和通信范围下均优于现有启发式与学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有启发式方法依赖手工设计的投标规则和重复共识机制，而多数学习方法假设全局观测、缺乏基于显式通信的协调，难以应对分布式系统中部分且动态观测下的复杂实际约束。

Method: 提出MDGAM-GRMAPG框架：MDGAM扩展图注意力机制以联合更新节点与边特征，采用多个解码器生成任务选择决策和通信消息；GRMAPG利用等价任务规划实例构造组相对优势以替代传统MARL算法中的评论家网络，降低训练难度并提升收敛性能。

Result: 在不同问题规模和通信范围下的实验表明，所提方法在任务完成性能上优于现有启发式和基于学习的方法；消融实验、复杂度分析和泛化测试进一步验证了所提创新的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了通信受限分布式多智能体系统中显式协调缺失的问题，为MRTA提供了一种兼具训练效率和收敛性能的新范式。

Abstract: Cooperative task scheduling in communication-constrained distributed multi-agent systems is challenging because each agent must make decisions from partial and dynamic observations while satisfying complex practical constraints. Existing heuristics rely on handcrafted bidding rules and repeated consensus, whereas many learning-based methods assume global observations and lack explicit communication-based coordination. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a neural scheduling framework for distributed multi-robot task allocation (MRTA), consisting of a multi-decoder graph attention model (MDGAM) policy model and a critic-free group relative multi-agent policy gradient (GRMAPG) training algorithm. MDGAM uses an extended graph attention mechanism to jointly update node and edge features, and employs multiple decoders to generate task-selection decisions and communication messages. GRMAPG constructs group-relative advantages from equivalent task-planning instances to replace the critic network used in conventional MARL algorithms, thereby reducing training difficulty and improving convergence performance. Experiments under different problem scales and communication ranges show that the proposed method improves task-completion performance over existing heuristic and learning-based methods, while ablation, complexity, and generalization tests further validate the proposed innovations.

</details>


### [72] [HIERA: Hierarchical Multi-Agent Relevance Assessment for Content Discovery Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00785)
*Pritom Saha Akash,Phanideep Gampa,Chao Shen,Ying Chen,Sheikh Muhammad Sarwar*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出HIERA，一种层级式多智能体相关性评估框架，包含四个专门化智能体（相关性法官、查询分析器、条目分析器、关系分析器），通过层级协调机制改善内容发现系统的相关性判断质量，在五个数据集上超越11个基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工标注相关性判断存在标注者间分歧和扩展成本问题，现有LLM自动评估方法依赖扁平化聚合策略（单步提示、投票集成、无协调的多智能体管道），缺乏有效的结构化协调机制。

Method: 构建层级式多智能体框架HIERA：Relevance Judge决定何时需要专家分析，Relation Analyzer协调查询分析、条目分析与外部知识，建立相关性关系并给出最终判断。通过消融实验验证层级协调结构本身对性能提升的贡献。

Result: 在五个数据集（EVS、MSRD、ESCI、WANDS、Home Depot）上超越11个基线：Home Depot提升10.2%，ESCI提升4.8%，EVS最高提升38%（p<0.05）。层级协调相比使用相同智能体的无协调协作提升12.7%。

Conclusion: 层级协调结构是性能提升的关键因素，而非智能体数量或外部知识的简单堆叠；HIERA证明了结构化多智能体协作在相关性评估任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Content discovery systems depend on relevance judgment for search quality evaluation, but human annotation faces inter-annotator disagreement and scaling costs. While Large Language Models show promise as automated assessors, current approaches rely on flat aggregation strategies: single-step prompting, voting ensembles, or uncoordinated multi-agent pipelines that aggregate independent outputs without integration. We propose HIERA, a hierarchical multi-agent relevance assessment framework with four specialized agents: a Relevance Judge, Query Analyzer, Item Analyzer, and Relation Analyzer. The Judge determines when specialist analysis is needed; the Relation Analyzer then coordinates query and item analyses with external knowledge to establish relevance relationships for final judgment. Ablation studies show that the same agents and external knowledge without hierarchical coordination degrade performance, confirming that the coordination structure itself accounts for the improvement. Evaluation across five datasets (EVS, MSRD, ESCI, WANDS, Home Depot) shows improvements over 11 baselines: 10.2\% on Home Depot, 4.8\% on ESCI, and up to 38\% on EVS ($p < 0.05$). Hierarchical coordination yields 12.7\% improvement over uncoordinated collaboration using identical agents.

</details>


### [73] [Stress-Relief Annealing: Polynomial-Time Simulation-Free Layout Optimization for Automated Warehouses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01024)
*Xiangjie Luo,Yulun Zhang,Miyuki Koshimura,Makoto Yokoo,Jiaoyang Li*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Stress-Relief Annealing (SRA)，一种多项式时间复杂度、无需仿真的仓库布局优化算法。SRA将任务需求转化为逐顶点的应力场来预测交通拥堵位置，其峰值可证明地限制吞吐量上限。实验表明SRA在单核CPU上仅需19分钟即可匹配或超越需要25,000次仿真和25小时（64核）的进化优化方法，且能将吞吐量提升约一倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有仓库布局优化方法（基于进化算法）虽然效果不错，但将整个仓库视为黑盒，依赖随机变异搜索高质量布局，需要大量仿真来评估候选方案，样本效率极低。作者希望设计一种无需仿真的高效布局优化算法。

Method: SRA算法将任务需求转化为一个逐顶点的"应力场"，用以预测仓库中交通将集中的位置；该应力场的峰值理论上限定了系统吞吐量。算法基于该应力场进行布局优化，时间复杂度为多项式级别，且整个过程无需任何仿真评估。

Result: （1）SRA相比人工设计的仓库，吞吐量和可扩展性均提升，可使支持的机器人数量翻倍；（2）SRA匹配或超越进化基线方法的吞吐量，但仅需单核CPU 19分钟，而基线需要25,000次仿真和64核机器上25小时；（3）改进效果在不同MAPF算法、非均匀任务需求以及两倍尺寸的仓库上均具有泛化性。

Conclusion: SRA证明了通过理论驱动的应力场分析，可以在不进行昂贵仿真的情况下高效优化仓库布局，显著提升了样本效率和时间效率，同时吞吐量表现不逊于甚至优于传统的进化优化方法。

Abstract: We study the problem of optimizing physical layouts for automated warehouses, where hundreds to thousands of robots are coordinated to transport packages. Previous works have shown that optimizing the warehouse layout (e.g., the physical location of the storage shelves) significantly improves throughput. However, state-of-the-art layout optimization approaches are based on evolutionary optimization methods, which treat the entire warehouse as a black box and rely on random mutation to search for high-quality layouts. While the optimization outcomes are promising, these methods require a massive number of simulations to evaluate candidate solutions, making them sample-inefficient. In this paper, we present Stress-Relief Annealing (SRA), a polynomial-time simulation-free layout optimization algorithm. SRA turns the task demand into a per-vertex \emph{stress field} that predicts where traffic will concentrate in the warehouse; the field's peak provably caps the throughput.
  Our experimental results show that (1) SRA improves both the throughput and the scalability of a human-designed warehouse, roughly doubling the number of robots it can sustain, (2) it matches or exceeds the throughput of the evolutionary baselines while taking only $19$ minutes on one CPU core, against their $25{,}000$ simulations and $25$ hours on a $64$-core machine, and (3) the gain generalizes across different Multi-Agent Path Finding algorithms, non-uniform task demands, and a warehouse with doubled dimensions.

</details>


### [74] [When Collaboration Becomes a Trigger: Collective Evidence-Threshold Backdoors in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01085)
*Jia-Hao Xiao,Lei Feng,Min-Ling Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM多智能体系统提出一种集体证据阈值后门攻击范式（BCBI）及相应防御方法（LATTE），攻击通过多个智能体间的累积证据而非单条消息触发后门行为，防御则通过学习良性通信动态并在异常更新传播前进行隔离来缓解威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统通过迭代通信和共享上下文扩展了LLM能力，但这种协作引入了新的安全漏洞——后门行为可以由多个智能体的累积证据达到隐藏阈值时触发，而非由单一消息决定。现有研究尚未充分关注这一集体层面的后门攻击风险，需要系统地揭示并提出防御方案。

Method: 攻击方面，提出集体证据阈值后门范式，并设计边界条件后门注入（BCBI），通过构造反事实边界对来区分阈值前后的良性行为与对抗目标，并学习与证据一致性的潜在进展。防御方面，提出潜在转换测试时评估（LATTE），一种仅需干净数据的潜在转换防御方法，学习良性通信动态，并在异常智能体更新传播前将其隔离。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，BCBI能够实现选择性激活且几乎没有过早激活；而LATTE在不了解攻击目标和触发器的情况下，能够限制后门传播，同时对正常系统运行的干扰最小。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了多智能体系统中集体层面后门攻击的可行性，并提出了一种实用的、无需攻击先验知识的干净数据防御方案，为LLM多智能体系统的安全性研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) extend LLM capabilities through iterative communication and shared contexts. However, this collaboration introduces a vulnerability: backdoor behavior can be activated when peer evidence reaches a hidden threshold, rather than being determined by any single message. We introduce a collective evidence-threshold backdoor paradigm for MAS and Boundary-Conditioned Backdoor Injection (BCBI), which constructs counterfactual boundary pairs to separate benign behavior before the threshold from the adversarial objective after it, and learns latent progression aligned with evidence. To mitigate this threat, we propose LAtent Transition Test-time Evaluation (LATTE), a clean-only latent-transition defense that learns benign communication dynamics and quarantines anomalous agent updates before their responses propagate. Across several benchmarks, BCBI yields selective activation with little premature activation; without knowing the attack target or trigger, LATTE limits propagation with minimal disruption.

</details>


### [75] [Asking Questions the Right Way: A Multi-Agent Conversational System for Prompt Formulation in Complex Task Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01366)
*B. Sankar,Pawni Yadav,Srinidhi Ranjini Girish,Amogh A. S*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出PAWNI——一个由八个代理组成的对话式提示词构建系统，通过引导式问答将非结构化查询转化为结构化提示词，并提出了包含18个提示词元素的三层框架。探索性研究（N=4）显示其可将提示词结构完整度从42%提升至91%，降低NASA-TLX工作负载（39.6降至21.7），并让所有参与者在单轮内获得满意输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型输出质量受限于用户提供的提示词，而多轮对话迭代往往导致上下文退化与认知收益递减。现有研究多聚焦于优化模型回答而非优化提问本身，因此需要一种在提问环节前置意图澄清的系统化方法。

Method: 构建PAWNI代理式对话界面，包含八个代理，通过引导式问答对话将非结构化查询转化为结构化提示词，并依托自进化知识库；提出三类（Essential、Enhancement、Elevation）共18个提示词元素的分层框架。采用探索性被试内设计（N=4），结合32通道EEG、NASA-TLX工作量量表和行为指标，在四个复杂任务上进行系统行为验证与测量协议验证。

Result: 参与者在PAWNI辅助下生成的提示词结构完整度从42%提升至91%；在所有质量维度上对LLM输出的评分更高；NASA-TLX工作量从39.6降至21.7；所有参与者在单轮内达到满意输出，而未辅助情况下需要1-12轮。由于样本量小，效应量不稳定，但方向一致性支持假设。

Conclusion: 在提示词构建前端进行意图澄清与优化，是提升人机协作效率的关键杠杆。尽管本研究的效应量因样本量限制而不稳定，但结果方向一致，支持了"优化问题而非回答"这一核心假设，为后续更大规模研究奠定了测量协议基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are integral to complex intellectual tasks, yet output quality remains constrained by user-provided prompts. Iterative multi-turn prompting often leads to context degradation and diminishing cognitive returns. We present PAWNI (Prompt Architecture Wizard using Neural Intelligence), an agentic conversational interface of eight agents that transforms unstructured queries into structured prompts through guided question-and-answer dialogue informed by a self-evolving knowledge base. Rather than optimizing the models response, PAWNI optimizes the question itself by front-loading intent clarification. We also propose a three-tier framework of 18 prompt elements across Essential, Enhancement, and Elevation categories.To evaluate system behavior and validate a measurement protocol, we conducted an exploratory within-subjects study (N=4) across four complex tasks, integrating 32-channel EEG, NASA-TLX workload, and behavioral metrics. Participants produced more structurally complete prompts with PAWNI (42% to 91% of assessed elements), rated LLM outputs higher across all quality dimensions, and reported lower workload (39.6 vs. 21.7 NASA-TLX). Every participant reached satisfactory output in a single turn, compared to 1-12 turns unaided. While effect sizes are unstable due to sample size, direction consistency supports the hypothesis that optimizing prompt formulation front-end is a critical lever for human-AI collaboration.

</details>


### [76] [Imprecise Belief Fusion Improves Multi-agent Social Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01367)
*Zixuan Liu,Jonathan Lawry,Michael Crosscombe*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文研究社会学习中交互过程中的不精确性是否能够提升集体学习效果。作者提出一个信念以命题逻辑公式表示的社会学习模型，通过参数化的融合算子控制交互的不精确程度，并综合运用差分方程模型和基于Agent的模拟进行验证。结果表明，当群体初始偏向错误信念时，一定程度的融合不精确性可以提高学习准确性，且该结论与差分方程模型不动点的稳定性分析一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会学习中个体不仅从直接证据学习，还通过与同伴互动学习。作者希望探究交互中的不精确性是否能够改善集体学习过程的效率，这是一个此前尚未被系统研究的问题。

Method: 提出一个社会学习模型，其中信念等价于命题语言中的公式，个体通过融合算子合并彼此的信念。融合算子被参数化以允许不同程度的不精确性——当两个信念存在差异时，更不精确的融合算子倾向于产生更不精确的融合信念。作者同时构建了差分方程模型和基于Agent的模拟，在不同条件下考察不同初始偏置的影响。

Result: 对于具有强烈初始错误信念偏置的群体，融合过程中一定程度的不可精确性可以在多种学习条件下提高学习准确性。此外，这种不精确性带来的益处与所提出的差分方程模型不动点的稳定性分析相一致。

Conclusion: 不精确性在社会学习的集体互动中可以是有益的，特别是当群体初始信念存在系统性偏差时，适当的不精确融合有助于提升整体学习效果。

Abstract: In social learning, agents learn not only from direct evidence but also through interactions with their peers. We investigate the role of imprecision in such interactions and ask whether it can improve the effectiveness of the collective learning process. To that end we propose a model of social learning where beliefs are equivalent to formulas in a propositional language, and where agents learn from each other by combining their beliefs according to a fusion operator. The latter is parametrised so as to allow for different levels of imprecision, where a more imprecise fusion operator tends to generates a more imprecise fused belief when the two combined beliefs differ. In this context we describe both difference equation models and agent-based simulations of social learning under a variety of conditions and with different initial biases. The results presented suggest that for populations with a strong initial bias towards incorrect beliefs some level of imprecision in fusion can improve learning accuracy across a range of learning conditions. Furthermore, such benefits of imprecision are consistent with a stability analysis of the fixed points of the proposed difference equation models.

</details>


### [77] [Training Small LLMs as Spatial Multi-Agent Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01425)
*Yi Mao,Andrew Perrault*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于符号选项（options）的多智能体强化学习框架，将冻结的小型LLM与符号规划器结合，通过机械合成的可行性守卫和每智能体LoRA适配器（PA-MAGRPO）训练，使智能体在空间协作游戏中从零奖励提升到熟练水平，并揭示奖励与合作解耦的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统训练日益流行，但现有工作主要关注奖励优化而忽视行为评估。在空间协作游戏中，使用低层动作提示的冻结小LLM完全失败（零奖励）。作者希望同时解决两个问题：如何让小型LLM通过多智能体强化学习学会协作，以及如何通过行为审计揭示奖励信号无法反映的真实协作情况。

Method: 基于选项/半MDP框架及其多智能体扩展（宏动作Dec-POMDP），为每个游戏构建符号选项库。选项由前沿编码模型根据游戏源码起草，可行性守卫通过随机策略滚动数据机械合成（仅当守卫能解释重复执行失败且不隐藏任何成功记录时才被采纳）。每个智能体的LLM作为选项上的策略，训练使用每智能体变体的多智能体GRPO（PA-MAGRPO），并配备私有LoRA适配器。

Result: 该方法在三个游戏和四种小型骨干模型上将冻结基础模型从零奖励提升到熟练水平。行为审计显示奖励与合作解耦：奖励上升可能仅仅意味着一个智能体学会了独立完成整个任务而同伴闲置，只有当任务本身需要合作时合作才会出现。

Conclusion: 奖励单独作为合作能力的衡量标准是不可靠的，行为评估必须与奖励指标并列使用。符号选项框架为小型冻结LLM提供了可学习的抽象行为空间，而行为审计则揭示了超出奖励信号之外的智能体交互质量。

Abstract: Training LLM-based multi-agent systems with multi-agent reinforcement learning is rapidly gaining traction, and a parallel line of work argues that such systems should be judged by their behavior, not only their reward. We take up both threads in spatial cooperative games, where small frozen LLMs prompted with low-level actions fail outright, earning zero reward. Guided by the options/semi-MDP framework---and, because option execution is asynchronous across agents, its multi-agent extension in macro-action Dec-POMDPs---we equip each game with a library of symbolic \emph{options}: typed, state-feasible, short-horizon behaviors executed by a symbolic planner. Each library is drafted by a frontier coding model from the game's source code; the feasibility guards that filter each menu are then synthesized mechanically from cheap random-policy burn-in rollouts---a guard is adopted only if it explains repeated execution failures while hiding no logged success---so no guard is authored, selected, or reward-tuned by hand. Each agent's LLM acts as its policy over options, with a private per-agent LoRA adapter trained by a per-agent variant of multi-agent GRPO (PA-MAGRPO); this lifts frozen bases from zero reward to competent play across three games and four small backbones. Behavioral audits then reveal that reward and cooperation decouple: a rising reward curve may simply mean that one agent has learned to run the entire task alone while its partner idles---cooperation emerges only when the task makes it necessary. Reward alone is thus an unreliable readout of cooperation; behavioral evaluation must sit alongside it.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [78] [Cross-Benchmark Generalization in Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00181)
*Sushant Mehta,Logan Ritchie,Liudas Panavas,Edwin Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对Qwen3.5-122B-A10B模型在363个MCP任务上进行两阶段SFT+RL后训练，在不使用任何外部基准数据的情况下，使模型在五个外部评估基准上均获得显著提升（Toolathlon +9.6pp、τ²-Bench +5.3pp、BFCL-V4 +3.5pp、SWE-Bench Pro +5.8pp、Terminal-Bench 2 +2.8pp），并通过成对轨迹分析识别出四种可跨域迁移的行为模式差异，为长程多工具后训练能提升跨基准通用能力提供了描述性证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中智能体可能通过利用环境特有的正则性（如工具模式、评分器解析、任务模板）而非习得可迁移技能来获得奖励，而分布内留出集无法区分这两者。作者认为判别性问题在于行为层面，即训练后的智能体如何行动，以及跨基准迁移是检验这一点的正确场所。

Method: 采用两阶段SFT-then-RL流水线，对开放权重混合专家模型Qwen3.5-122B-A10B在363个长视野MCP任务（覆盖27个类别）上进行后训练。训练过程中不使用任何外部基准任务或评分器，也不参考外部分数来调整奖励、超参数、检查点选择或停止时机。同时引入成对轨迹分析来识别行为差异。

Result: 在贪心pass@1评估下，训练后模型在五个外部基准上均优于基础模型：Toolathlon +9.6pp、τ²-Bench +5.3pp、BFCL-V4 +3.5pp、SWE-Bench Pro +5.8pp、Terminal-Bench 2 +2.8pp。值得注意的是，软件工程类基准的提升发生在训练数据完全不含软件工程任务的情况下。成对轨迹分析识别出四种反复出现的行为差异：更谨慎的局部目标形成、构建目标相关的工作状态、通过局部修复保持父目标稳定、以及验证完成情况。

Conclusion: 长视野多工具后训练能够改变智能体的工作方式，且这些行为模式能够迁移到训练域之外。四种行为差异在办公工作流和代码任务中以类似形式出现，为跨基准能力迁移提供了描述性证据。这一发现表明，自包含环境中的RL训练不仅能利用环境特定正则性，还能习得更具通用性的行为策略。

Abstract: For reinforcement learning (RL) in self-contained environments, a policy can get rewards by exploiting environment-specific regularities (tool schemas, grader parsing, task templates) rather than by acquiring transferable skill, and an in-distribution holdout shares those regularities. We argue that the discriminating question is behavioral, namely how a trained agent acts, and that cross-benchmark transfer is the right place to look for it. We post-train an open-weight mixture-of-experts model (Qwen3.5-122B-A10B) on 363 long-horizon Model Context Protocol (MCP) tasks across 27 categories, using a two-stage SFT-then-RL pipeline. Toolathlon performance informed the initial base-family and SFT-teacher choices, but no external-benchmark task or grader entered training and no external score informed the reward, training hyperparameters, trained-checkpoint selection, or stopping. At greedy pass@1, the trained model improves over the base on five reported external evaluations: Toolathlon (+9.6 pp), $τ^2$-Bench (+5.3 pp), BFCL-V4 (+3.5 pp), SWE-Bench Pro (+5.8 pp), and Terminal-Bench 2 (+2.8 pp). Both software-engineering benchmarks improve despite the training collection containing no software-engineering tasks. An exploratory paired-trajectory analysis identifies four recurring behavioral differences (more careful local-goal formation, building goal-relevant working state, keeping parent goals stable through local repairs, and verifying completion) that appear in analogous forms across office workflows and code. These results provide descriptive evidence that long-horizon multi-tool post-training can change ways of working that transfer beyond its training domain.

</details>


### [79] [Source Code Authorship Attribution Does Not Generalize from Competitions to Classrooms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00227)
*Serhii Yemets,Marek Horváth*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究使用CodeBERT在Google Code Jam（GCJ）和课程作业数据集上评估源代码作者归属任务。模型在GCJ数据上达到92.6% Top-1准确率（1000作者时70.7%），但在实际课程作业数据上仅达到0.2%和0.06%的Top-1准确率，远低于机会基线，表明GCJ基准高估了该任务在教育场景中的实际适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源代码作者归属技术在学术诚信和教育评估中具有潜在应用价值，但现有基准（如GCJ）可能无法反映真实教育场景中的表现，因此需要系统评估预训练模型在不同数据源上的泛化能力。

Method: 对预训练Transformer模型CodeBERT进行微调，使用三个数据源：Kaggle公开的GCJ提交、精选GCJ档案、以及一所技术大学的课程作业数据集。同时构建多模型基准，跨不同模型家族进行一致性验证，并分析数据集和任务属性以解释性能差距。

Result: GCJ数据上CodeBERT达到92.6%（10作者）和70.7%（1000作者）的Top-1准确率；课程作业数据上仅达到0.2%（690作者封闭式作业）和0.06%（812作者开放式作业）的Top-1准确率，低于或等于机会基线。多模型基准显示该差距跨模型家族持续存在。

Conclusion: GCJ基准会高估作者归属技术在教育场景中的实际应用价值，除非在目标课程作业上下文中进行验证。数据集和任务属性差异是造成性能差距的关键因素，研究者应避免仅依赖竞赛数据评估该任务在真实教育环境中的可行性。

Abstract: Source code authorship attribution aims to identify the author of a program fragment from its writing style. We fine-tune the pre-trained transformer CodeBERT on three sources of data: publicly available Google Code Jam (GCJ) submissions from an open Kaggle repository, a curated GCJ archive, and institutional coursework datasets collected at a technical university. On multi-round GCJ data, CodeBERT reaches 92.6% Top-1 accuracy for 10 authors and retains 70.7% Top-1 (88.2% Top-10) for 1000 authors. On the examined coursework datasets, the same pipeline performs at or below the corresponding chance baselines: 0.2% Top-1 on a closed-assignment dataset of 690 authors and 0.06% Top-1 on open-ended assignments evaluated over 812 authors. A companion multi-model benchmark provides consistent cross-model evidence across additional dataset configurations, indicating that the observed performance gap is not specific to CodeBERT and persists across the evaluated model families. We analyze dataset and task properties that plausibly explain this gap and argue that GCJ-based benchmarks overestimate the practical applicability of authorship attribution in educational settings unless they are validated on the target coursework context.

</details>


### [80] [Modelling Android applications through static analysis and systematic exploratory testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00228)
*Jordan Doyle,Thomas Laurent,Anthony Ventresque*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出DroidGraph框架，结合静态分析与高效的系统性探索测试，为Android应用生成全面的控制流模型，涵盖从底层方法语句到高层用户界面结构，以支持自动化测试生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android应用开发节奏快，需要自动化测试生成的支撑，但现有的静态分析难以应对Android事件驱动特性和多样的开发机制，而动态随机测试生成器效率低且缺乏应用知识。

Method: DroidGraph框架结合传统静态分析与高效系统性探索测试，生成Android应用的详细控制流模型，覆盖从低层方法语句到高层用户界面结构，并将动态分析结果与静态分析互补整合。

Result: 在19个多样化应用上的实验表明，该方法平均比常用随机探索多交互18%的应用内容，且少用345次交互；动态分析结果补充了静态分析，平均多发现51个组件和49%更多的界面回调链接。

Conclusion: DroidGraph通过静态与动态分析的有效融合，能够生成更全面可靠的Android应用模型，从而更好地支持自动化测试生成，提升测试覆盖率和效率。

Abstract: Mobile application development is a fast paced industry with frequent releases. While the development pace increases, so too does the need for automated test generation. Model-based test generation is one of the most common and successful approaches to support this need. Understanding and modelling the application under test is integral to producing comprehensive, dependable and effective tests. Unfortunately, mobile platforms such as Android, introduce a host of difficulties. Static analysis struggles with Android's event-based nature and the growing variety of mechanisms available for developers to implement different features. Additionally, dynamic analysis, implemented by popular random test generators, is slow, inefficient, and limited by a lack of application knowledge.
  This paper introduces DroidGraph, a framework to generate a comprehensive control flow model of Android applications using traditional static analysis and efficient systematic exploratory tests. DroidGraph provides a detailed model of an Android application, from low level method statements to high level user interface structures. This model can be used to support automated test generation. We apply DroidGraph to 19 diverse apps and show that our efficient exploratory tests, on average, interact with 18% more of the app than commonly used random exploration in 345 less interactions. Integrating the dynamic analysis results provided by these tests complements our static analysis, and uncovers on average 51 more components and 49% more interface callback links in the application code.

</details>


### [81] [PADRAIG: Precise Android Automated Input Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00230)
*Jordan Doyle,Thomas Laurent,Anthony Ventresque*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出PADRAIG，一个基于模型的Android自动化测试输入生成框架，利用详细的控制流模型生成测试，相比现有技术平均提高16%的代码覆盖率，并减少84%的测试生成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android自动化测试输入生成已研究十余年，但现有方法存在明显权衡：随机输入生成效率低，系统性和基于搜索的方法生成测试耗时长，传统基于模型的方法需要额外建模开销但生成速度较快。需要一个既能高效生成测试又能保持高覆盖率的方案。

Method: PADRAIG框架采用基于模型的方法，通过构建被测应用的详细控制流模型来指导测试输入生成，从而在保证高覆盖率的同时降低测试生成运行时间。作者将PADRAIG与3种使用不同测试输入生成技术的现有工具进行对比。

Result: 在从F-Droid应用商店随机选取的19个Android应用上进行评估，PADRAIG相比现有最先进工具平均多覆盖16%的代码，且测试生成运行时间平均减少84%。

Conclusion: PADRAIG通过详细的控制流模型驱动测试生成，在覆盖率和生成效率两个维度上都优于现有技术，证明了基于模型的方法在Android测试输入生成中的潜力。

Abstract: Android automated test input generation has been a highly researched topic for over a decade and has shown promising results with a variety of approaches. Random input generation is commonly used and the easiest to maintain, but ultimately inefficient. Systematic and search-based approaches produce effective tests but require a disproportionally large generation runtime. Model-based approaches have the additional overhead of modelling the application under test (AUT) but they result in a faster test generation.
  In this paper we present Precise AnDRoid Automated Input Generation (PADRAIG), a model-based test input generation framework that uses a detailed control flow model of the AUT to generate tests that can achieve higher line coverage, with a lower test generation runtime than the state of the art. We compare the line coverage achieved, and the generation runtime of PADRAIG against 3 state of the art tools, each of which uses a different test input generation technique. Our results, using 19 randomly selected Android apps from the F-Droid application store, show that PADRAIG achieves, on average, 16% more coverage of the AUT than the state of the art and it can generate tests with, on average, 84% less runtime.

</details>


### [82] [Improving Mobile User Interface Testing with Model Driven Monkey Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00236)
*Jordan Doyle,Takfarinas Saber,Paolo Arcaini,Anthony Ventresque*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种能够表示Android应用代码及其所有交互元素的控制流结构，通过将这种结构知识注入Exerciser Monkey工具（形成Monkey++），显著提升了自动化测试的覆盖率和效率。实验表明，基于该控制流结构的深度优先搜索测试套件比随机测试快一个数量级，并能实现用户交互元素的完全覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Android自动化测试工具（如Exerciser Monkey）缺乏对应用程序结构的了解，只能随机或按预定义方式生成事件，导致测试速度慢且难以发现缺陷。此外，人工设计测试场景是劳动密集型的任务，需要更智能的自动化测试方法。

Method: 提出一种新颖的控制流结构来表示Android应用的代码及所有交互元素；通过将测试环境知识注入Exerciser Monkey（形成Monkey++），并使用深度优先搜索（DFS）遍历该控制流结构来生成测试序列。

Result: 对比Exerciser Monkey和Monkey++的界面覆盖率，随机性质的Exerciser Monkey产生了速度慢且覆盖率低的测试套件，而基于DFS生成的测试套件速度快一个数量级，并实现了用户交互元素的完全覆盖。

Conclusion: 该研究能够使Exerciser Monkey更加高效和有效，同时为更精准的基于搜索的Android自动化测试技术奠定基础。

Abstract: Testing mobile applications often relies on tools, such as Exerciser Monkey for Android systems, that simulate user input. Exerciser Monkey, for example, generates random events (e.g., touches, gestures, navigational keys) that give developers a sense of what their application will do when deployed on real mobile phones with real users interacting with it. These tools, however, have no knowledge of the underlying applications' structures and only interact with them randomly or in a predefined manner (e.g., if developers designed scenarios, a labour-intensive task) -- making them slow and poor at finding bugs.
  In this paper, we propose a novel control flow structure able to represent the code of Android applications, including all the interactive elements. We show that our structure can increase the effectiveness (higher coverage) and efficiency (removing duplicate/redundant tests) of the Exerciser Monkey by giving it knowledge of the test environment. We compare the interface coverage achieved by the Exerciser Monkey with our new Monkey++ using a depth first search of our control flow structure and show that while the random nature of Exerciser Monkey creates slow test suites of poor coverage, the test suite created by a depth first search is one order of magnitude faster and achieves full coverage of the user interaction elements. We believe this research will lead to a more effective and efficient Exerciser Monkey, as well as better targeted search based techniques for automated Android testing.

</details>


### [83] [SMS Opt-In/Opt-Out Consent Record Architecture in Enterprise CRM Systems: Compliance Patterns for Multi-Tenant Managed Packages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00248)
*Devam Gupta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向企业消息系统的合规同意记录架构，解决了CRM平台原生不支持同意状态建模的问题，特别适用于多租户托管包环境。该架构涵盖数据模型设计、基于关键词的同意捕获、哈希去重策略以及发送时抑制执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TCPA等法规要求企业消息系统必须获取并记录明确的用户同意，且需立即响应退订请求。然而CRM平台虽提供丰富的联系人和线索数据模型，却不将同意状态作为一等记录类型原生建模。在多租户托管包场景下，包无法假设或修改安装组织的数据库结构，这一缺口在架构上变得尤为显著。

Method: 本文从CRM原生消息托管包中提炼并泛化了生产级同意记录架构。具体包括：1) 数据模型设计，在无法修改租户schema的约束下建模同意状态；2) 基于关键词的同意捕获机制；3) 采用基于哈希的唯一性策略进行去重；4) 在消息发送时执行抑制逻辑；5) 阐述了多租户约束如何影响上述设计决策。

Result: 论文基于已在医疗、金融服务和销售运营等多个独立企业组织中部署的CRM原生消息托管包，总结了生产环境下的实际实现模式与架构经验，验证了该同意记录架构在多租户环境中的可行性与有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了CRM平台在同意状态建模方面的架构空白，为在多租户托管包约束下实现合规的同意管理提供了可复用的架构参考，涵盖同意获取、去重、存储和发送时执行等完整环节。

Abstract: Regulatory frameworks such as the Telephone Consumer Protection Act (TCPA) impose strict consent requirements on enterprise messaging systems: organizations must obtain and record explicit opt-in consent before sending SMS communications, and must immediately honor opt-out requests. While CRM platforms provide rich contact and lead data models, they do not natively model consent state as a first-class record type. This gap becomes architecturally significant in multi-tenant managed packages distributed via enterprise application marketplaces, where the package cannot assume or modify the installing organization's schema. This paper presents a production consent record architecture, generalized from patterns implemented within a CRM-native messaging managed package serving independent enterprise organizations spanning healthcare, financial services, and sales operations. We describe the data model design, keyword-based consent capture, a hash-based uniqueness strategy for deduplication, suppression enforcement at message send time, and the multi-tenant constraints that shaped these design decisions.

</details>


### [84] [LoopsBench: From Harness Engineering to Loop Engineering in Benchmarking Coding Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00267)
*Han Li,Zhemin Fang,Rili Feng,Yingqi Zhao,Jiaheng Liu,Pengfei Gao,He Ye,Dayi Lin,Qingwei Lin,Saravan Rajmohan,Dongmei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LOOPSBENCH，一个用于编码智能体循环工程评估的长时程基准，包含112个来自真实来源的任务（覆盖8种编程语言和9个领域），每个任务以依赖DAG组织可独立测试的开发单元，并通过流式运行时发布就绪测试和保留回归义务。评估表明最强配置（Opus-4.7 + Claude Code + 外部续接）仅解决25%的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体基准多聚焦于局部任务或最终状态结果，缺乏对持续长时程执行的评估能力，无法为循环工程（loop engineering）提供有效度量。

Method: 构建112个任务，每个任务由依赖DAG（具有源证据表示的先决边）组织可独立测试的开发单元；采用流式感知运行时，沿就绪前沿（ready frontier）释放测试，并保留已完成节点作为回归义务。评估前沿编码智能体搭配多种常用循环实现。

Result: 最强配置Opus-4.7配合Claude Code和外部续接仅解决25.00%的任务；记录的规划只能部分恢复源代码中推导的先决依赖DAG，且在不同循环配置下回归事件仍然可见。

Conclusion: 现有编码智能体在长时程软件开发循环中的表现仍不理想，LOOPSBENCH为循环工程提供了标准化的评估基础，并开源了全部数据（超过5,300个开发单元和可执行测试）。

Abstract: Coding agent infrastructure is shifting from harness engineering toward loop engineering as coding agents are deployed for sustained long-horizon software development. Existing benchmarks often center on localized tasks or end-state outcomes, offering limited insight into sustained execution. We introduce LOOPSBENCH, a long-horizon benchmark for loop engineering in coding agent evaluation. Each task is a dependency DAG over separately testable development units with source-evidenced prerequisite edges. LOOPSBENCH comprises 112 tasks from authentic sources spanning 8 programming languages and 9 domains. Its flow-aware runtime releases tests along the ready frontier and retains completed nodes as regression obligations. We evaluate frontier coding agents paired with widely used loop implementations. The strongest configuration, Opus-4.7 with Claude Code and outer continuation, resolves 25.00% of tasks. Recorded plans recover only part of the source-recovered prerequisite DAG, and regression events remain visible across the evaluated loop profiles. We open source the benchmark data and code, including all tasks, more than 5,300 development units, and executable tests, at microsoft/Loopsbench.

</details>


### [85] [OrEdge: Efficient Multi-Modal Anomaly Detection in Distributed Software Systems via Orthogonal-Domain Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00309)
*Amr M. Zaki,Farhoud Jafari Kaleibar,Honggeun Ji,Komal Sarda,Marin Litoiu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: OrEdge是一个轻量级实时多模态异常检测框架，通过正交域时序表示替代昂贵的注意力/图结构，在保持竞争性检测性能的同时，将重建模型参数压缩至最多9.6K，并在边缘设备上实现亚秒级推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式系统异常检测方法依赖计算开销大的注意力机制和图结构架构，难以在资源受限的边缘设备上实现实时部署，需要更轻量高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出OrEdge框架，核心是OrEdgeCore轻量级正交域重建模块，通过正交时序表示联合分析日志、指标和轨迹等多模态数据，捕捉时序依赖并减少观测信号间的冗余，同时抑制瞬态变化。

Result: 在MSDS、SN、TT三个真实微服务数据集上取得竞争性检测性能；重建模型参数从现有方法的20K-143K降至最多9.6K；在Raspberry Pi上实现亚秒级推理，延迟降低超过一个数量级。

Conclusion: 正交域时序建模是计算密集型注意力/图结构架构的有效替代方案，在检测精度与计算效率之间取得良好平衡，适合边缘环境下的实时多模态异常检测。

Abstract: We introduce Orthogonal-Edge (OrEdge), a lightweight framework for real-time anomaly detection in multi-modal distributed software systems. Unlike existing approaches that rely on computationally expensive attention- and graph-based architectures, OrEdge leverages orthogonal-domain temporal representations to achieve accurate anomaly detection with substantially lower computational complexity and model size. It jointly analyzes heterogeneous monitoring data, including logs, metrics, and traces, to identify abnormal software behavior, capture temporal dependencies, and reduce redundancy across observability signals. At its core, OrEdge incorporates OrEdgeCore, a lightweight orthogonal-domain reconstruction module that captures recurring temporal patterns while suppressing transient variations. Evaluated on three real-world microservice datasets (MSDS, SN, and TT), OrEdge achieves competitive detection performance while reducing the reconstruction model size to at most 9.6K parameters, compared with 20K--143K parameters in existing methods. This compact design enables efficient deployment on resource-constrained edge devices: on Raspberry Pi platforms, OrEdge achieves sub-second inference and reduces inference latency by over an order of magnitude compared with existing approaches. Extensive ablation studies, sensitivity analyses, orthogonal basis evaluations, and qualitative case studies further validate the effectiveness of each design component. Overall, OrEdge demonstrates that orthogonal-domain temporal modeling provides an effective alternative to computationally intensive attention- and graph-based architectures, achieving a favorable balance between detection accuracy and computational efficiency for real-time multi-modal anomaly detection in edge environments. The code is available at https://github.com/theamrzaki/MicroService_Twin_Original.

</details>


### [86] [Artificial Intelligence and Modeling & Simulation: An Overview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00366)
*Niclas Feldkamp,Philippe J. Giabbanelli,Istvan David*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了人工智能（AI）与建模与仿真（M&S）之间的双向交叉关系，沿着M&S的各个阶段（从模型规范、输入建模到执行、实验、验证确认和输出分析）组织综述内容，并提供了概念路线图以帮助读者导航这一快速发展的生态系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI和M&S两个社区的研究需求日益趋同，加之生成式AI等技术的快速进步以及数据和计算资源的增长，需要对两者交叉领域进行结构化梳理，以帮助研究者理解这一快速变化的生态系统。

Method: 沿M&S的各阶段（模型规范、输入建模、执行、实验、验证与确认、输出分析）组织综述结构；按阶段选取代表性研究，展示如大语言模型（LLM）等技术如何重塑仿真实践，同时指出局限性和开放性挑战，并提供概念路线图。

Result: 揭示了AI与M&S之间双向互补的关系：AI可支持、增强甚至替代仿真研究中的部分组件，而仿真可作为AI的数据生成器、训练环境和评估平台；文中在每个阶段选取了代表性研究加以说明。

Conclusion: AI与M&S的交叉融合是双向且多层次的，该报告提供了结构化框架和概念路线图，有助于读者理解和导航这一快速演进的跨学科领域。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) and Modeling & Simulation (M&S) are increasingly intertwined, reflecting converging research needs across both communities, rapid technological advances such as the rise of generative AI, and the growing availability of data and computational resources. This report provides a structured overview of the intersections of AI and M&S. The relationship goes both ways: AI can support, augment, or even replace components of simulation studies, while simulations can serve as data generators, training environments, and evaluation platforms for AI. We organize this landscape along the stages of M&S from model specification and input modeling to execution, experimentation, verification and validation, and output analysis. Selected studies at each stage illustrates how techniques such as Large Language Models have reshaped simulation practices, while highlighting limitations and open challenges. This report also provides a conceptual roadmap that helps readers navigate a rapidly changing ecosystem.

</details>


### [87] [CASPER-Change-Aware Slice Prioritization for Efficient Regression Testing of LLM-based systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00378)
*Biruk Asmare Muse,Lionel Briand,Yiwei Lu,Keheliya Gallaba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CASPER，一个基于变更感知的切片优先级框架，用于LLM系统的回归测试。它通过进化式切片识别方法找到语义相关且性能一致的回归切片，并根据执行日志中的行为信息对这些切片进行优先级排序，从而在系统级和实例级之间提供中等粒度的回归分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM系统的回归测试面临两难：单个回归实例信息有限，无法有效诊断受影响行为；而仅依赖整体系统性能变化又过于粗粒度，无法定位具体受影响的模块。因此需要在中间层级（测试套件切片）上分析回归实例。

Method: CASPER包含两个核心步骤：一是利用进化式切片识别方法，找出包含语义相关实例且性能特征一致的回归切片；二是根据执行日志中提取的行为信息，对切片按其回归可能性进行优先级排序，以应对LLM应用中的prompt级和model级变更。

Result: 在软件问题解决领域进行实例化评估，切片识别方面优于基于聚类的基线方法，回归切片优先级排序方面优于随机排名基线。CASPER生成的切片一致性更好，同时保持相当或更优的语义连贯性，并在不同LLM系统变更和测试预算下改进了回归切片优先级排序效果。

Conclusion: CASPER在LLM系统回归测试中提供了有效的中间粒度分析方案，能够生成一致性更好的切片并提高回归切片优先级排序的准确性，为LLM系统的测试效率和诊断能力提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Regression testing for LLM-based systems poses unique challenges because individual regression instances provide limited information about system-level regressions. A failure in a single instance does not necessarily indicate a meaningful regression or provide sufficient information to diagnose affected behaviors. Conversely, detecting regressions based only on overall system performance changes is too coarse-grained, as it does not identify which behaviors are affected. This motivates analyzing regression instances at an intermediate level through test suite slices.
  To address this challenge, we propose CASPER, a change-aware slice prioritization framework for efficient regression testing of prompt-level and model-level changes in LLM-based systems. CASPER first identifies regression slices containing semantically related instances with consistent performance characteristics using an evolutionary slice identification approach. Given a change to an LLM-based application, CASPER prioritizes slices according to their likelihood of regression using behavioral information extracted from execution logs. We instantiate CASPER in the software issue resolution domain and evaluate slice identification against clustering-based baselines and regressed slice prioritization against a random ranking baseline. Results show that CASPER generates more consistent slices while maintaining comparable or better semantic coherence and improves regressed slice prioritization across different LLM-based system changes and testing budgets.

</details>


### [88] [Distilling Reasoning Traces into Advisory Prompts for Software Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00437)
*Faizan Faisal,Prem Devanbu,Toufique Ahmed*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需额外训练即可减少LLM编码错误的方法：通过让大型LLM分析小型LLM在"思考模式"下避免编码错误的案例，生成摘要解释并归纳为简短的"建议提示词"（advisory prompts），这些提示词可被注入到模型中以减少错误，且能跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在代码生成和处理中会犯错，而推理（thinking）模式虽能减少错误但资源开销大。受人类编程学习者通过反思错误并内化规则来避免犯错的教学过程启发，作者希望在不总是付出推理成本的前提下，实现更好的编码性能。

Method: （1）在低资源LLM中识别"思考模式"能避免错误的案例；（2）由更大的LLM检查这些错误及其被思考模式避免的过程，生成摘要性解释；（3）再由大型LLM将这些解释归纳为简短的建议提示词；（4）将这些提示词注入回原模型（或迁移到其他模型）以指导编码行为。

Result: 该方法在多种中等规模的模型上有效；在部分案例中，学到的"建议提示词"可以成功迁移到其他模型。论文还提供了对LLM编码错误本质的调查研究，以及对该方法适用条件的刻画。

Conclusion: 通过模拟人类的"反思-归纳-内化"学习过程，可以在不进行额外训练和不必每次使用推理模式的情况下，利用小型模型的思考结果提炼出可迁移的提示词来减少编码错误，为推理成本与性能之间的权衡提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Language models are widely used for generating and otherwise processing code (e.g., identifying code hallucinations, possible inputs, or predicting outputs); however, LLMs can make mistakes, which can be serious. One key issue is that models are trained on (still) largely human-written, and thus imperfect, code; it's not easy to find sufficiently large code corpora that are entirely free of bugs. Thus, other inference-time ways of reducing LLM errors, without additional training, are desirable. "Reasoning" or "thinking" modes, exposed as a togglable feature by hybrid reasoning models, do reduce errors; however, reasoning consumes additional resources. This paper asks if better performance can be achieved without always incurring the cost of reasoning. Human students of programming learn to avoid mistakes by (a) identifying them, (b) reflecting upon the cognitive lapses that led to them (essentially, "thinking through" the errors), (c) inferring general rules or lessons from these reflections, and (d) internalizing these lessons into rules. In tutorial sessions with an instructor, this is a common Socratic interaction. Examples of such internalizable rules might include the nugget "Before coding, restate the requirements to clarify them." Inspired by this process, this paper describes an approach where we first identify examples in which "thinking mode" in a (low-resource) LLM avoids errors. These errors, and their avoidance via "thinking" in the same LLM, are then examined by a bigger LLM to generate summary explanations; these are then summarized by a large LLM into brief advisory prompts. This approach works on many modest-sized models; in some cases, the "advisory prompts" thus learned can also be gainfully transferred to other models. We also present investigations into the nature of coding errors that language models make, and a characterization of when this approach can be helpful.

</details>


### [89] [AiFlow: Token-Native Reactive Orchestration with Bounded Backpressure for Streaming LLM Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00558)
*Qunhui Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AiFlow，一个token原生的反应式编排模型，将LLM提供商增量输出规范化为类型化的Context<T>事件流，通过有向流式图传播，每个节点由Node Guardian管理队列边界、并发、排序、溢出策略、取消传播和重试纪律，显著降低应用TTFPT并严格约束运行时队列深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM应用日益演变为流式工作流，但现有框架将生成过程视为粗粒度的请求-响应步骤，队列管理、worker分配、排序和背压等依赖临时回调代码，缺乏系统化的编排机制。

Method: 提出AiFlow流式编排模型：1) 将提供商增量规范化为类型化Context<T>事件；2) 通过有向流式图传播；3) 每个节点由Node Guardian管理本地队列边界、worker并发、排序、溢出策略、取消传播和重试纪律；4) 形式化有界内存属性；5) 从紧凑DSL和JSON图形式编译并提供静态验证。

Result: 微基准测试、DeepSeek轨迹回放（30次）、在线运行、LangGraph基线对比、流式RAG工作负载及Ollama本地后端验证表明：不改变提供商端Model TTFT，但应用TTFPT降低70.9-94.7%；运行时的队列深度严格在声明边界内（较无界策略MaxQ减少93.7-96.5%）。

Conclusion: AiFlow通过token原生反应式编排和Node Guardian机制，在不影响提供商端首token延迟的前提下，大幅降低应用端全流程完成时间，并确保运行时队列内存有界，为LLM流式工作流提供了系统化的编排方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) applications increasingly operate as streaming workflows combining retrieval, tool calls, safety filters, and multi-agent coordination. Although contemporary frameworks expose provider deltas, workflow nodes often treat generation as coarse request-response steps, leaving queue management, worker allocation, ordering, and backpressure to ad hoc callback code. This paper presents AiFlow, a token-native reactive orchestration model that normalizes provider deltas into typed Context<T> events propagated through a directed streaming graph. Each node is managed by a Node Guardian that declares and enforces local queue bounds, worker concurrency, ordering, overflow policy, cancellation propagation, and retry discipline. We formalize the bounded-memory property, present the compilation from a compact DSL and JSON graph form, and provide static validation for type safety, state concurrency, and injection compatibility. Controlled microbenchmarks, captured DeepSeek trace replay (30 runs), descriptive online runs, LangGraph baselines, a streaming RAG workload, and an Ollama local-backend check show that AiFlow does not alter provider-side Model TTFT but reduces Application TTFPT by 70.9-94.7\% versus aggregation and keeps runtime-owned queue depth within declared bounds (93.7-96.5\% MaxQ reduction versus unbounded policies). The supplementary artifact contains scripts, raw traces, machine-readable tables, checksums, and an API-free smoke test; the public implementation is available through the FIT Framework repository.

</details>


### [90] [Can Perplexity Serve as a Cognitive Signal for Code Understandability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00624)
*Xiaokai Rong,Mohammadali Sefidi Esfahani,Aashish Yadavally,Rigby Peter,Tien N. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过多个人类标注数据集进行了实证研究，发现尽管token级别的困惑度能与人类代码理解困惑对齐，但简单的片段级困惑度聚合（如平均值、中位数或峰值）并不能可靠地反映代码可理解性，并揭示了其背后的三个原因：token困惑度的极端偏斜分布、人类理解标注缺乏共识、以及不同模型和分词器间的巨大差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明大语言模型的token级困惑度可以对齐人类在代码理解过程中的局部困惑，这引发了一个自然的问题：困惑度能否也作为代码片段级别的可理解性信号？作者希望验证这一假设是否成立。

Method: 在多个基于人类标注的数据集上进行实证研究，包括方法级可理解性判断、输出预测任务和已接受的可理解性改进补丁；通过对比简单片段级聚合（平均、中位数、峰值困惑度）与人类理解标注的相关性来评估；并进一步分析token困惑度的分布特性、人类标签一致性以及跨模型和分词器的差异。

Result: 简单的片段级困惑度聚合与人类可理解性之间缺乏可靠的相关性。原因包括：(1) token困惑度呈高度偏斜和重尾分布，极端峰值不仅来自语义上有意义的构造，也来自标识符、字面量、类型、分隔符和分词伪影；(2) 人类可理解性标签缺乏共识，使整片段难度成为有噪声的目标；(3) 困惑度分布及其与人类难度的对齐在不同模型和分词器间差异显著。

Conclusion: 困惑度是一个有前景但微妙（delicate）的认知信号：它对局部代码困惑有用，但在支持可靠的代码可理解性度量之前，需要代码感知的聚合方法、共识感知的评估和模型敏感性分析。

Abstract: Recent work suggests that token-level perplexity from large language models can align with localized human confusion during code comprehension. This raises a natural question: can perplexity also serve as a snippet-level signal for code understandability? We conduct an empirical study of this question across multiple human-grounded datasets, including method-level understandability judgments, output-prediction tasks, and accepted understandability-improvement patches. Despite prior token-level evidence, we find that simple snippet-level aggregations of token perplexity, such as average, median, or peak perplexity, do not reliably correlate with human understandability. We then investigate why this happens. First, token perplexity is highly skewed and heavy-tailed across code structures; extreme spikes arise not only from semantically meaningful constructs, but also from identifiers, literals, types, separators, and tokenization artifacts. Second, human understandability labels often lack consensus, making whole-snippet difficulty a noisy target. Third, perplexity distributions and their alignment with human difficulty vary substantially across models and tokenizers.
