These findings explain why prior token-level perplexity--confusion alignment does not directly transfer to snippet-level understandability. Overall, our study positions perplexity as a promising but delicate cognitive signal: useful for localized code confusion, but requiring code-aware aggregation, consensus-aware evaluation, and model-sensitivity analysis before it can support reliable code-understandability measurement.

</details>


### [91] [Understanding Online Failure Prediction in Linux Through Complementary Multi-View Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00651)
*Diogo Dória,João R. Campos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了在Linux操作系统中构建可解释在线故障预测（OFP）管道的实践经验，结合共识特征选择、时间起始分析、子系统级因果分析和补充诊断机制。在严格跨工作负载条件下，该管道在未见工作负载上实现了91-94%的检测率且误报率低于1%，但故障模式诊断对工作负载变化更为敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅靠高精度的故障预测不足以实现实际部署，操作人员需要可解释的诊断信息来信任警报并决定如何响应。同时，高预测精度可能只是模型利用了工作负载特定的噪声和偶然关联，而非捕捉到真正可迁移的故障过程，需要可解释性机制来揭示预测信号的真实含义。

Method: 构建了一个可解释的OFP管道，采用以下技术组合：1）基于共识的特征选择用于故障检测；2）时间起始分析（temporal onset analysis）用于预警能力评估；3）子系统级因果分析用于故障归因；4）补充诊断机制支持故障解释。评估采用严格跨工作负载条件和冻结的训练模型，并使用Leave-One-Mode-Out（LOMO）评估诊断泛化能力。

Result: 在未见工作负载上实现91-94%检测率，误报率低于1%；故障模式诊断对工作负载变化更敏感；早期预警能力因故障模式而异（38-215秒）；在LOMO评估下，未见故障模式的诊断准确率为0%。

Conclusion: 研究揭示三个关键经验：1）检测比诊断更能跨工作负载泛化；2）早期预警能力强烈依赖具体故障模式；3）无法仅凭训练中的相关故障模式可靠地诊断未见故障。这些结果证明了互补可解释性机制在解读预测信号、区分可迁移故障结构与诊断泛化失效场景中的价值。

Abstract: Accurate Online Failure Prediction (OFP) has been shown to be feasible in Operating Systems (OSs) settings, but prediction alone is not sufficient for practical adoption. Without diagnostic insight, operators have limited basis to trust alerts or decide how to respond. Moreover, even when predictive accuracy is high, it is often unclear whether models are capturing meaningful failure processes or merely exploiting workload-specific noise and incidental correlations in telemetry. This paper reports a practical experience building and evaluating an explainable OFP pipeline for Linux OSs. We combine consensus-based feature selection for detection with temporal onset analysis, subsystemlevel causal analysis, and complementary diagnostic mechanisms to support failure interpretation. Evaluated under strict crossworkload conditions with frozen training artifacts, it achieved 91-94% detection on unseen workloads without retraining, while maintaining false alarm rates below 1%. However, failure mode diagnosis proved substantially more sensitive to workload shift, and several diagnostics mechanisms showed limited effectiveness for specific failure types. Our experience highlights three main lessons: i) detection generalizes more robustly than diagnosis across workload changes; ii) early-warning capability depends strongly on the failure mode, ranging from 38 to 215 seconds in our study; and iii) unseen failure modes are not reliably diagnosable from related training modes alone, providing 0% accuracy under Leave-One-Mode-Out (LOMO) evaluation. Taken together, these results show the value of complementary explainability mechanisms for interpreting accurate failure predictions, revealing when predictive signals reflect transferable failure structure and when diagnostic generalization breaks down under workload variation.

</details>


### [92] [Unreliable in Practice? A Comprehensive Study of Errors in LLM-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00661)
*Rodrigo Pato Nogueira,Marco Vieira,João R. Campos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文分析了由七个LLM在四种编译语言中生成的86,726个包含编译或运行时错误的代码样本，通过LLM分类并人工验证错误原因，比较了不同模型、语言和问题难度下的错误分布，发现即使最大的模型也常犯简单错误，且生成代码常缺乏输入验证和内存安全检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM被广泛用于编码并提升开发者生产力，但在复杂编码任务中仍存在困难。此前研究范围有限（单一语言、少量问题或有限模型），缺乏对LLM生成代码错误模式的全面理解，因此需要对错误进行系统分类和表征，以设定合理预期并设计缓解策略。

Method: 收集了86,726个由七个LLM在四种编译语言中生成的含编译或运行时错误的代码样本，使用LLM对错误按根本原因进行分类，人工验证分类结果，并进行比较分析，测量错误在不同模型、语言和问题难度下的流行程度，识别常见错误模式。

Result: 错误类型在不同语言和模型间差异显著，但即使最大的模型也频繁犯简单错误。生成代码常常遗漏基本的输入验证或内存安全检查，可能导致溢出、资源耗尽或其他可靠性/安全性问题。

Conclusion: LLM生成代码的错误模式具有多样性和模型/语言特异性，但同时也存在跨模型的普遍性问题（如缺乏输入验证和内存安全检查），这些发现为理解LLM代码质量、设定预期和设计改进策略提供了数据支撑。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are being widely used for coding, with reports indicating that AI now generates an increasing share of production code. Studies show that LLMs can significantly improve developer productivity, yet they still struggle with more complex coding tasks. Just as understanding error modes in human-written code has been central to improving software quality, identifying and characterizing the errors in LLM-generated code is critical for setting realistic expectations and designing mitigation strategies. Prior research has been limited in scope, often focusing on a single language, a small number of problems, or a limited selection of models. As a result, there is still no comprehensive understanding of which errors are common and which are specific to certain models or languages. To address these gaps and develop a deeper understanding of the quality of LLM-generated code, we analyzed a corpus of 86,726 code samples that contained compilation or runtime errors. These samples were generated by seven LLMs across four compiled languages. We classified errors by their underlying causes using an LLM, manually validated these classifications, and performed a comparative analysis. This labeled data is then used to measure error prevalence by model, language, and problem difficulty, to identify common error patterns. Results show that, although error types vary strongly across languages and models, even the largest models frequently make simple mistakes. We also observe that generated code often omits basic input validation or memory-safety checks, which can lead to overflows, resource exhaustion, or other reliability/security issues.

</details>


### [93] [Vul4Py: Benchmarking Automated Vulnerability Repair in Python with Paired Exploit and Functional Oracles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00692)
*Tan Bui,Ting Zhang,Ferdian Thung,Yunpeng Xiong,Penghao Jiang,Xin Zhou,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了Vul4Py，一个包含100个真实Python漏洞的AVR基准测试，每个漏洞都配有成对的测试预言（exploit oracle和pytest功能oracle），并对比了六种方法，发现软件工程智能体（OpenHands修复41个漏洞）远优于直接提示的LLM（4个）和专用修复工具（2个）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Python漏洞修复基准测试存在缺陷：要么仅凭概念验证漏洞利用来验证补丁，要么只对部分项目应用功能测试，两者都会漏掉补丁引入的功能回归问题。缺乏一个可验证的、头对头的Python AVR方法对比。

Method: 构建Vul4Py基准测试，包含100个来自60个开源项目的真实漏洞，横跨60个CWE和2017至2025年。每个条目配有成对预言：漏洞利用测试（在漏洞版本失败、修复版本通过）和项目自带的pytest功能测试（在两个版本都必须通过）。每个实例打包了可复现的固定环境。在此基础上对比六种方法：专用漏洞修复工具、直接提示的LLM、软件工程智能体。

Result: 软件工程智能体表现最佳：OpenHands修复41/100个漏洞，而最强的直接提示LLM仅修复4个，专用工具仅修复2个（三者共享相同的底座模型）。成对预言拒绝了119个补丁中15个仅凭漏洞利用预言会接受的补丁，104个被接受的补丁中有98个经人工确认与开发者的补丁语义等价。

Conclusion: Vul4Py通过成对的预言设计确保了补丁验证的可信度，实证表明软件工程智能体在Python漏洞修复方面显著优于直接提示的LLM和专用工具，同时揭示了仅依赖漏洞利用验证的基准测试会严重高估修复质量。

Abstract: Automated Vulnerability Repair (AVR) has advanced rapidly across program analysis, machine learning, and Large Language Models (LLMs), but a verifiable, head-to-head comparison of AVR approaches on Python is still missing. Python underpins critical web, data, and machine-learning infrastructure, yet existing Python benchmarks accept a patch on the strength of a proof-of-concept exploit alone, or apply a functional test only on the subset of entries whose upstream project happens to ship one. Both therefore miss functional regressions, in which a patch defeats the exploit but breaks unrelated behavior. We present Vul4Py, a Python AVR benchmark in which every entry carries a paired oracle: an exploit oracle that must fail on the vulnerable revision and pass on the fixed one, together with a project-native pytest functional oracle that must pass on both. Vul4Py comprises 100 real vulnerabilities from 60 open-source projects, spanning 60 distinct CWEs and the years 2017 to 2025, each packaged with a pinned, reproducible per-instance environment. Using Vul4Py, we compare six approaches in three categories: a specialized vulnerability repair tool, directly prompted LLMs, and software engineering agents. The agents dominate: OpenHands repairs 41 of 100 vulnerabilities, against 4 for the strongest directly prompted LLM and 2 for the specialized tool, despite all three sharing the same backbone model. The paired oracle is what makes these counts trustworthy: it rejects 15 of the 119 patches that an exploit-only oracle would accept, and 98 of the 104 patches it admits are manually confirmed to be semantically equivalent to the developer's patches

</details>


### [94] [Deep Agentic Search for Repository-Level Code Question Answering: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01507)
*Amirkia Rafiei Oskooei,Bora Ilci,Alperen Kayim,Mehmet Egemen Uzun,Berat Can,Kaan Emre Kara,Ozan Orhan,Mehmet S. Aktas*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文比较了代码智能体的两种代码定位策略：语义搜索（预先构建向量索引检索代码块）和深度智能体搜索（规划代理委派子代理在隔离上下文窗口中探索）。在SWE-QA基准上，语义搜索正确率65.2%，远高于深度智能体搜索的46.2%，且成本不到后者一半。失败模式分析显示，深度智能体搜索41.8%的失败发生在规划者与子代理之间的交接环节，且多为静默失败——产出流利但错误的答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度智能体搜索（即grep-search by subagent）已被Claude Code、Codex、Antigravity等主流代码智能体广泛采用，被视为良好的上下文工程实践，但缺乏关于其是否真正产生更好答案的证据。作者希望实证检验这种设计是否值得其额外成本。

Method: 在SWE-QA基准（仓库级代码问答）上对比两种方法：语义搜索（预构建向量索引检索代码）与深度智能体搜索（规划代理委派子代理在隔离上下文窗口探索）。对每次失败运行进行编码，建立失败模式分类体系。

Result: 语义搜索以65.2%的正确率显著优于深度智能体搜索的46.2%，且每次正确答案的成本不到后者一半。深度智能体搜索并未消除失败，反而引入了新的失败类别：41.8%的失败发生在规划者与子代理的交接环节，且通常是静默失败，产出流利但错误的答案。

Conclusion: 深度智能体搜索虽然解决了上下文污染这一真实问题，但其保护并非没有代价。对于可索引仓库的只读问题，语义搜索是更强且更便宜的选择。这一发现对当前代码智能体设计的主流趋势提出了重要质疑。

Abstract: Code agents spend much of their effort simply locating the right code inside a repository. Two approaches dominate current practice. In Semantic Search, the agent retrieves code blocks from a vector index built from the repository in advance. In Deep Agentic Search (also known as grep-search by subagent), a planning agent delegates the exploration to a separate subagent that works in an isolated context window and returns only a condensed result. The second design, which is considered good context engineering practice, exists to protect the main agent from context pollution (also known as context rot), the loss of accuracy that occurs as unrelated material accumulates in the context window. Recent code agents (such as Claude Code, Codex, Antigravity, etc) have adopted it quickly, but there is little evidence on whether it produces better answers. We compare the two approaches on SWE-QA, a benchmark for repository-level code question answering. Semantic search answered 65.2% of questions correctly against 46.2% for deep agentic search, and it produced each correct answer at less than half the cost. To explain the gap, we then coded every failed run into a taxonomy of failure modes. The taxonomy shows that deep agentic search did not remove failures but introduced a new class of them: the single largest share of its failures, 41.8%, occurred at the hand-off between the planner and its sub-agent, and these were usually silent, ending in a fluent and confident answer that was wrong. Deep agentic search addresses a real problem and is now the preferred design in many code agents. However, our results show that the protection it offers may not be free, and that for read-only questions over a repository that can be indexed, retrieval was the stronger and cheaper option.

</details>


### [95] [EduPluginBench: Executable Assurance for AI-Generated Educational Plugins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00739)
*Nizam Kadir*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EduPluginBench，一个可执行基准和分阶段准入方法，用于评估受管治理软件生态系统中生成插件的合规性（最小权限、遥测同意、来源、特权写入权限、生命周期约束和故障有界性）。通过1,440个激活检查的一阶突变体验证，P0-P4相比P0-P2将发布阻断缺陷召回率提升74.7个百分点，且120个干净引用零误拒。然而，两个当前编码模型生成的600个未修改样本中，只有300个通过解析，无一通过P0或P0-P4一致性检测，独立标注的Moodle研究也显示生成检测器未能识别任何漏洞修订，表明负迁移结果使其难以直接等同于真实缺陷有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码生成模型虽能产生可执行组件，但传统编译和功能测试无法验证其是否符合治理生态系统的合规性要求（如最小权限、遥测同意、来源、特权写入权限、生命周期约束和故障有界性），现有评估方法存在盲区。

Method: 构建EduPluginBench可执行基准，采用分阶段准入方法（P0-P4），通过1,440个激活检查的一阶突变体和120个干净引用进行验证；同时开展固定转移研究（600个未修改生成样本）、独立标注的Moodle研究和540个生成的诊断实验（后验有界修复）。

Result: P0-P4比P0-P2召回率提升74.7个百分点（规范聚类95% CI 73.4-75.8），120个干净引用零误拒（95% Wilson上界3.1%）；但转移研究中600个生成样本仅300个解析成功且无一通过P0，Moodle研究中冻结检测器未发现任何漏洞修订；早期诊断中112个P0通过全部不合规。

Conclusion: EduPluginBench在域内验证中表现出色，但负迁移结果严重限制了其作为真实缺陷有效性的直接证据；作者认为无法将受控契约一致性结果解读为独立于真实缺陷的有效性，强调了在治理生态系统中评估生成代码合规性的挑战和谨慎态度。

Abstract: Code-generation models can produce executable components, but compilation and functional tests do not establish compliance with least privilege, telemetry consent, provenance, privileged-write authority, lifecycle constraints, or bounded failure. We introduce EduPluginBench, an executable benchmark and staged admission method for generated plugins in governed software ecosystems. Across 1,440 activation-checked first-order mutants from 30 specifications, P0-P4 increased release-blocking-defect recall by 74.7 percentage points (specification-clustered 95% CI 73.4-75.8) over P0-P2, with no observed rejection among 120 clean references (95% Wilson upper bound 3.1%). A frozen transfer study of 600 unmodified generations from two current coding models found that 300/600 parsed, but none passed P0 or achieved P0-P4 conformance (95% upper bound 0.64%); downstream assurance estimands were undefined. An independently labelled Moodle study retained 16 vulnerable/fixed pairs; the frozen generic PHP detector found no vulnerable revisions. These negative transfer results prevent controlled contract consistency from being read as independent real-defect effectiveness. An earlier 540-generation diagnostic found that post-hoc bounded repair yielded 112 P0 passes, all nonconforming, with recall increasing from 13.4% to 100%. The artifact retains protocols, public-source provenance, raw generations, row-level decisions, audits, analysis code, and reproduction instructions.

</details>


### [96] [Less Is More: Tuning Configurable Systems with Imperfect Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00759)
*Yulong Ye,Miqing Li,Tao Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出MFTune调优器，利用"少即是多"的理念，通过在低成本的不完美保真度环境中进行部分调优，生成高质量种子后在完美保真度环境中深度调优，显著提升系统配置调优的预算利用率。实验表明MFTune在83.33%的情况下优于10个现有调优器，最高提升19.34%，并节省数小时调优时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高度可配置系统的配置调优对于优化性能至关重要，但由于可选参数多且配置测量成本高昂，调优过程极具挑战性。现有方法在完美保真度环境下直接调优，预算利用率低，亟需更高效的调优策略。

Method: 提出"不完美保真度"概念框架，即在一个相似但测量成本更低的环境中进行部分调优。在此基础上设计MFTune调优器，主动搜索超过10^4种可能的不完美保真度设置，找到近似最优的调优环境，生成高质量种子用于完美保真度环境下的深度调优，实现"广度"与"深度"的平衡。

Result: 在19个月全天候运行的真实系统实验中，与10个最先进的调优器对比，MFTune在83.33%的情况下表现显著更优，性能最多提升19.34%，并普遍节省数小时的调优预算。

Conclusion: "少即是多"的调优范式是有效的：在低成本的不完美保真度环境中进行部分调优，可以大幅提升调优效率和预算利用率，为高成本配置调优问题提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Configuration tuning is essential for optimizing the performance of highly configurable systems, e.g., throughput or runtime, under a given environment. Yet, this is a challenging process as there can be many options to tune, and configuration measurement is often highly expensive. In this paper, we demonstrate the phenomenon of ``less can be more'': system configuration tuning can be greatly improved with much superior budget utilization by partially tuning under the imperfect-fidelity---an environment that is similar, but cheaper to measure, compared with the concerned perfect-fidelity of environment under which the system should be tuned. We codify a conceptual framework of fidelity for configurable systems, drawing on which allows us to propose MFTune, a tuner that proactively explores in the space of $>10^4$ possible imperfect-fidelity settings to approximate a useful one, which strikes for the wideness of tuning. This creates high-quality seeds for the perfect-fidelity, which in turn ensures the tuning depth. Experiment results against $10$ state-of-the-art tuners, obtained from running diverse real-world systems for $19$ months $24 \times 7$, show that MFTune performs considerably better on $83.33$\% cases with up to $19.34\%$ improvement while achieving hours of budget saving in general.

</details>


### [97] [Turning Interaction History into Execution State: A Runtime Layer for Long-Horizon Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00808)
*Zehao Wang,Yisen Xu,Chenglin Li,Chao Peng,Bram Adams,Ahmed E. Hassan,Tse-Hsun,Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Ledger，一个确定性运行时层，通过维护显式的执行账本（记录观察、修改和尝试状态），在长程编码智能体的每一步操作前提供运行时状态视图，并在命令执行前检查其与账本的一致性，从而避免基于过时信息操作和冗余重复执行。在SWE-bench Verified上，Ledger将GPT-5 mini的Pass@1从56.2%提升至64.2%，将MiniMax M2.5从75.8%提升至81.0%，同时分别降低28.9%和31.8%的成本，附接到OpenAI Codex上额外提升3.4个百分点且成本降低24.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程编码智能体在长时间轨迹中累积大量动作和观察，但记录中没有任何信息能表明哪些观察仍然反映仓库的当前状态。模型必须隐式地从原始历史推断执行状态，当推断不足时，智能体会基于过时的文件内容行动或重新执行结果仍然有效的操作，导致效率低下和错误。

Method: Ledger是确定性运行时层，将智能体的已完成交互提炼为显式执行状态（已观察、已修改、已尝试），并在线维护执行账本。它在每一步的两个边界生效：行动前的inform路径将紧凑的运行时状态视图附加到提示中；命令执行前的govern路径对账本检查命令，返回仍然有效的先前结果以替代重新执行，并标记可能冗余的重复。该层不增加语言模型调用，直接包裹未修改的智能体。

Result: 在全部500个SWE-bench Verified实例上，Ledger将GPT-5 mini的Pass@1从56.2%提升至64.2%，MiniMax M2.5从75.8%提升至81.0%，总成本分别降低28.9%和31.8%。附接到OpenAI Codex时，Pass@1提升3.4个百分点，成本降低24.4%。消融实验显示govern路径贡献了大部分解析度提升，inform路径贡献了大部分效率提升，二者组合效果最佳。

Conclusion: 长程智能体所缺乏的不是更短的历史视图，而是对自身执行状态的显式记录。通过在线维护显式的执行账本并在模型行动前和命令执行前两个边界应用该状态，可以显著提升长程编码任务的解析成功率和成本效率，且无需修改智能体本身或增加任何语言模型调用。

Abstract: Long-horizon coding agents accumulate hundreds of actions and observations in their trajectories, yet nothing in this record indicates which observations still describe the repository as it currently stands. Before every decision, the model must implicitly infer the execution status from raw history, and when this inference falls short, the agent acts on outdated file contents or re-executes work whose results are still valid. We propose Ledger, a deterministic runtime layer that distills an agent's completed interactions into an explicit execution state: what has been observed, what has been modified, and what has been attempted. Ledger keeps this state in an online execution ledger and applies it at two boundaries of every step. Before the model acts, an inform path appends a compact runtime state view to the prompt; before a proposed command runs, a govern path checks it against the ledger, returning still-valid earlier results in place of re-execution and flagging likely-redundant repetition. The layer adds no language-model calls and wraps an otherwise unmodified agent. Across all 500 SWE-bench Verified instances, Ledger raises Pass@1 from 56.2% to 64.2% with GPT-5 mini and from 75.8% to 81.0% with MiniMax M2.5, while cutting total cost by 28.9% and 31.8%. Attached to OpenAI Codex, it adds 3.4 percentage points of Pass@1 at 24.4% lower cost. Ablations attribute most of the resolution gain to govern and most of the efficiency gain to inform, with their combination performing best. What long-horizon agents lack, we conclude, is not a shorter view of their history but an explicit account of their own execution state.

</details>


### [98] [Structure-Aware Semantic Chunking with Title-Chain Prefixes: A 1600-Query Evaluation and the Measurement Trap in Text-Transform Ablations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00824)
*Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种零额外LLM调用的三段式纯分块侧语义分块流水线（标题拆分、语义合并、标题链前缀），在1600查询的生产级Markdown知识库评估中，MRR@5从0.374提升至0.463（+23.8%）；核心方法论发现是：在同池前缀开关消融中，双标注者一致性从kappa 0.45骤降至0.04，揭示了现有分块研究评估实践中的测量陷阱，并推荐检索时逐候选前缀评估方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分块是RAG中最首要且影响最深远的步骤，每个下游检索决策都继承了分块边界，因此需要一个高效且有效的分块策略来提升检索质量。

Method: 提出三阶段纯分块侧语义分块流水线：(1) 标题拆分；(2) 语义合并；(3) 标题链前缀——复用文档自身的标题层级而非生成摘要，从而零额外LLM调用。

Result: 在1600查询分层评估中，全量集MRR@5从0.374提升至0.463（+23.8%），可回答子集从0.828提升至0.925（+11.7%），双标注者Cohen's kappa为0.45。同时报告了失败尝试：三个查询侧或架构级方案为死胡同、一个测量失败（前缀权重衰减）、以及一个协议级失败（同池消融中kappa从0.45崩塌至0.04）。

Conclusion: 标题链前缀提供了标注者达成相关性共识所需的关键消歧信号；现有分块研究中的常见评估实践存在测量陷阱，应转向检索时逐候选前缀评估。

Abstract: Chunking is the first and most consequential step in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG): every downstream retrieval decision inherits the chunk boundaries. We present a three-stage, chunk-side-only semantic chunking pipeline---header-split, semantic merge, and title-chain prefixing---that costs zero additional LLM calls: the title chain reuses the document's own header hierarchy instead of a generated summary. On a 1600-query stratified evaluation over a production Markdown knowledge base, the pipeline improves MRR@5 from 0.374 to 0.463 (+23.8%) on the full set and from 0.828 to 0.925 (+11.7%) on the answerable subset (n=563), with dual-annotator Cohen's kappa 0.45 (unweighted, 16,000 score pairs). We then report what we tried and what failed: three query-side or architecture-level follow-ups are design dead ends (unevaluated---no comparable run artifacts), one measured failure (prefix weight decay), and one protocol-level failure that is the paper's central methodological finding. In a same-pool prefix on/off ablation, dual-annotator agreement collapsed from kappa 0.45 to 0.04 under identical prompts---stripping the title-chain context strips the disambiguation signal annotators need to agree on relevance. This measurement trap invalidates a common evaluation practice in chunking research and motivates retrieval-time per-candidate prefix evaluation, the direction we recommend from all our evidence.

</details>


### [99] [Documentation vs. Code Patterns: What Drives LLM-Based Exception Oracle Generation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00884)
*Soneya Binta Hossain,Matthew B. Dwyer,Tasfia Tasnim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模干预实验研究了基于LLM的测试预言生成（TOG）系统在异常预言生成中的预测依据。研究发现移除Javadoc @throws子句对准确率影响甚微（最大下降不到1个百分点），表明结构化异常文档并非主要预测驱动因素。通过归因引导的替换消融实验发现，模型的预测依赖于捷径信号——有些模型对少量结构标记高度敏感，有些则分散依赖大量词汇线索。结论是强异常预言准确率不一定反映对异常语义的稳健理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based测试预言生成方法在异常预言生成上报告了高准确率，但缺乏对这些预测背后证据来源的理解——不清楚模型是利用显式异常行为文档（如Javadoc @throws子句），还是依赖测试、代码和文档中的重复模式。作者希望揭示模型预测的真正驱动力。

Method: 采用基于干预的大规模实验研究：1）对三种TOG系统（涵盖分类器和生成式架构，模型规模从约110M到7B参数）进行测试；2）使用三个真实世界基准（两个生成测试数据集和一个新构建的开发人员编写测试基准）；3）先移除Javadoc @throws子句观察准确率变化；4）应用归因引导的替换消融实验识别模型预测依赖的信号。

Result: 1）移除Javadoc @throws子句后准确率仅发生边际变化，最大下降不到1个百分点，说明结构化异常文档不是预测的主要驱动力；2）归因引导的替换消融实验显示高质量预测可由捷径信号驱动：部分模型对少量结构标记高度敏感，另一些模型则分散依赖大量词汇线索；3）这些发现挑战了强异常预言准确率反映稳健异常语义使用这一假设。

Conclusion: 未来TOG系统的评估不应仅看预测是否正确，还应检查预测是否基于有意义的异常触发证据。研究为TOG领域的评估标准提出了新的维度，强调可解释性和证据groundedness的重要性。

Abstract: LLM-based test oracle generation (TOG) methods report high accuracy on exception oracle generation, but it remains unclear what evidence drives these predictions. In particular, do models use explicit exceptional-behavior documentation such as Javadoc @throws clauses, or do they rely on recurring patterns in tests, code, and documentation?
  We investigate this question through a large-scale intervention-based study of three TOG systems spanning classifier-based and generative architectures and model sizes from roughly 110M to 7B parameters, evaluated on three real-world benchmarks comprising two generated-test datasets and a new benchmark of developer-written tests. We first remove Javadoc @throws clauses and find that accuracy changes only marginally, with the largest drop below one percentage point. This indicates that structured exception documentation is not the primary driver of exception-oracle prediction. We then apply attribution-guided substitution ablations to identify the signals that predictions depend on. The results show that high accuracy can be driven by shortcut signals: some models are highly sensitive to a small number of structural tokens, while others distribute reliance across many lexical cues. These findings challenge the assumption that strong exception-oracle accuracy reflects robust use of exception semantics. Future TOG systems should therefore be evaluated not only by whether they predict the correct oracle type, but also by whether their predictions are grounded in meaningful exception-triggering evidence.

</details>


### [100] [Joint Optimization of Human Headcount and Stochastic AI Resource Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00886)
*Marco Montes de Oca*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了在固定预算下，组织如何在人类员工与生成式AI token容量之间进行最优分配。作者将认知摩擦调整的生产函数与机会约束随机预算前沿相结合，利用中心极限定理和Cornish-Fisher偏度修正将机会约束转化为确定性等价形式，最终得到闭式最优人力配置和token强度的Lagrangian解。核心发现是：token使用波动性上升会提高人均token强度但缩减人力规模（尽管两者在生产上是互补品，但在随机预算约束下成为边际替代品）；使用偏度则是一种纯无谓损失税，缩小预算但不改变替代动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具虽能提升生产力，但其不可预测且往往高昂的成本给组织带来严重财务压力。管理者必须在固定预算下分配人力与AI token容量，而标准的确定性规划忽略了token消耗的重尾波动性和审计AI输出的认知成本，因此需要一种能够同时处理这两类不确定性的联合分配模型。

Method: 将认知摩擦调整的生产函数与机会约束随机预算前沿相交；每位工程师的token使用量设为i.i.d.、非负、右偏，仅需均值、方差和偏度即可（不假设参数族）；通过中心极限定理结合Cornish-Fisher偏度校正将机会约束简化为确定性等价；求解Lagrangian得到闭式最优人力配置、人均token强度的超越方程条件及bordered-Hessian二阶条件；通过比较静态分析探讨波动性、偏度和离散度缩放机制对最优解的影响。

Result: 得到闭式最优人力数量、人均token强度的超越条件，以及在合理波动-人力比下成立的二阶条件。比较静态显示：个人token波动率上升会提高最优人均token强度并缩减人力（替代效应）；个人使用偏度是纯无谓损失税，仅缩小预算不改变替代动态。若离散度随均值等比例缩放，则替代关系反转，两种机制可通过实证检验区分。

Conclusion: 该模型为组织在生成式AI时代的人力与算力联合配置提供了理论框架，揭示了随机预算约束下生产互补品在边际上成为替代品的关键洞见，并为经验研究提出了一个可检验的判别条件来区分两种不同的随机性机制。

Abstract: Generative AI tools are rapidly becoming standard for coding and other business operations. However, while these tools can drive productivity, their unpredictable, and often significant, costs are putting severe financial strain on organizations. The leadership of such organizations must split a fixed budget between human headcount and generative-AI token capacity, yet standard deterministic planning ignores both the heavy-tailed volatility of token consumption and the cognitive cost of auditing AI-generated output. We model the joint allocation by intersecting a cognitive-friction-adjusted production function with a chance-constrained stochastic budget frontier. Per-engineer token usage is treated as i.i.d., non-negative, and right-skewed; no parametric family is assumed, as only its mean, variance, and skewness enter the analysis. The chance constraint is reduced to a deterministic equivalent via the Central Limit Theorem with a Cornish--Fisher skewness correction. Solving the resulting Lagrangian yields a closed-form optimal headcount, a transcendental condition for optimal per-capita token intensity, and a bordered-Hessian second-order condition that holds for any reasonable volatility-to-headcount ratio. Comparative statics show that rising individual token volatility raises optimal per-capita token intensity while contracting headcount: although human labor and tokens are complements in production, the stochastic budget makes them substitutes at the margin. Individual usage skewness acts as a pure deadweight tax that shrinks the budget without altering the substitution dynamics. Both conclusions presuppose that usage dispersion is invariant to the planned mean allocation. In the case where dispersion instead scales proportionally with the mean, the substitution reverses, so which regime applies is a sharp, empirically testable question.

</details>


### [101] [RefactorAssist: Agentic Refinement for Reliable Code Refactoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00924)
*Jonathan Cordeiro,Shayan Noei,Ying Zou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了RefactorAssist智能体，用于修复LLM生成的代码重构中的功能错误。通过分析10个开源Java项目中重构失败的根因，设计了一个结合单元测试日志、错误解释、项目上下文检索和代码差异的智能体修复方法，在最佳配置下实现了70.8%的剩余失败修复率和94.2%的累计通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的代码重构经常引入细微错误，导致功能行为改变和单元测试失败，限制了其在实际开发中的采用。需要系统性地分析失败根因并开发自动化修复方法。

Method: 使用10个开源Java项目及其原生测试套件，手动分析LLM生成重构失败的原因；设计RefactorAssist智能体，采用分层策略：先进行无需LLM的静态修复（缺失导入、括号不匹配、编译错误），再结合错误日志、错误解释、项目上下文检索和代码差异进行迭代式智能体修复。

Result: 识别出8类主要的重构失败原因（如上下文误解/幻觉占24.3%、重命名错误占15.3%等）；RefactorAssist在最佳配置下对剩余失败实现70.8%的修复率，累计通过率达94.2%。

Conclusion: 静态检查与测试引导、上下文感知的智能体修复相结合，能够显著提高LLM生成重构的可靠性，使其更接近实际开发者工作流程中的实用集成。

Abstract: Code refactoring aims to enhance the internal structure of source code without affecting its functional behavior. The recent advancements of Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated potential for automating software engineering tasks, such as code refactoring. However, the refactorings produced by LLMs often introduce subtle errors, leading to functional behavior changes and failed unit tests, which limit their practical adoption. To address the limitations of LLM-generated refactorings, we analyze the root causes of their failures and develop the RefactorAssist agent to improve the functional correctness of LLM-generated refactorings. To this end, we use 10 open-source Java projects with their native test suites and manually evaluate why LLM-generated refactorings fail unit tests. We then design an agentic approach that leverages unit-test logs, error explanations, project context retrieval, and code diffs to guide the iterative refactoring. Our findings show that the main reasons for failure are context misunderstanding/hallucination (24.3%), incorrect or inconsistent renaming (15.3%), adding new functionality or variables (13.7%), code incompleteness (11.3%), syntax and structural errors (9.7%), edge cases not handled (9%), improper type handling (8.7%), and variables outside scope (8%). To make our approach cost-effective, RefactorAssist first applies a static repair step for missing imports, unbalanced brackets, and compilation errors without LLMs. For remaining failures, RefactorAssist incorporates error logs and code diffs, achieving up to a 70.8% repair rate on the remaining failures and a 94.2% cumulative pass rate under the best-performing configuration. These results indicate that static checks and test-guided, context-aware agentic repair can increase the reliability of LLM-generated refactorings, bringing them closer to practical integration within developer workflows.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Revisiting Classic Thought Experiments to Measure Consciousness for Artificial Intelligence Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00001)
*Peter David Fagan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过守恒一致编码（CCE）框架重新审视莱布尼茨的磨坊、图灵的模仿游戏和塞尔的汉语房间，提出用任务表现（$W_{causal,T}$）度量行为成功，用操作意识（$κ_T$）度量内部结构保真效率，并证明非压缩查找系统与紧凑生成系统可在行为上等价但在$κ_T$上显著分化，从而将外在表现与支撑表现的内在组织分离开来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的思想实验（莱布尼茨磨坊、图灵测试、塞尔中文房间）长期争论何为"理解"，但缺乏形式化的区分框架。作者试图通过CCE框架分离外在行为表现与内部组织效率，以澄清这些争论并为AI安全分析提供理论基础。

Method: 构建一个玩具符号设置，在CCE框架下定义两个核心度量：任务表现$W_{causal,T}$衡量行为层面的成功程度，操作意识$κ_T$衡量保留的内部结构支持该行为时的效率。在此框架中对比两类系统：非压缩查找系统（依赖不断扩大的未复用映射存储）与紧凑生成系统（复用紧凑内部结构）。

Result: 在原则上，非压缩查找系统和紧凑生成系统可以达到可比较的行为表现（$W_{causal,T}$相近），但两者在$κ_T$上产生显著差异：前者依赖不断扩张的未复用映射存储，后者通过复用紧凑内部结构获得更高的效率。这证明了行为等价性与内部组织效率可以相互独立。

Conclusion: 该框架将关于"理解"的经典争论重新表述为外在表现与支撑该表现的内在组织之间的区分，并指出这种区分对后续AI安全分析具有重要意义。

Abstract: This research note revisits Leibniz's mill, Turing's imitation game, and Searle's Chinese Room through the Conservation-Congruent Encoding (CCE) framework. It formalises a toy symbolic setting in which successful behaviour is measured by task performance ($W_{causal,T}$), while the efficiency with which preserved internal structure supports that behaviour is measured by operational consciousness ($κ_T$). Within this setup, an uncompressed lookup system and a compact generative system can in principle achieve comparable behavioural success, yet diverge sharply in $κ_T$: the former relies on an expanding standing store of unreused mappings, whereas the latter reuses compact internal structure. The note therefore reframes classic disputes about understanding by separating outward performance from the organisation that sustains it, and motivates why this distinction may matter for later AI-safety analysis.

</details>


### [103] [AutoFOAM: The Self-Refining Autonomous OpenFOAM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00003)
*Arun Govind Neelan,A Seshaditya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoFOAM是一个基于大语言模型的自进化智能体，能够仅通过自然语言指令自动创建、评估、运行和演化OpenFOAM仿真，显著降低CFD使用门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源CFD求解器（如OpenFOAM）需要大量专业知识和耗时的手动配置文件设置，阻碍了广泛使用。

Method: 基于Qwen-coder 2.5-14B模型，在252个文本提示上微调（覆盖7个OpenFOAM求解器、13个参数化网格模板及y+感知数值策略），采用7阶段进化循环，并通过三种反坍缩流（RAG增强重试、字典级补丁、提示多样化改写）防止模型退化。

Result: AutoFOAM能够自动完成从自然语言指令到OpenFOAM仿真全流程，加速快速原型设计并降低CFD使用门槛。

Conclusion: AutoFOAM成功将生成式人工智能与严格的流体仿真相结合，实现了高级CFD工作流的大众化。

Abstract: Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) plays an important role in modern engineering, but using open-source solvers such as OpenFOAM requires considerable knowledge and skills, as well as time-consuming configuration file setup. To reduce this burden, we propose AutoFOAM - a self-evolving large language model (LLM) agent that creates, evaluates, runs, and evolves its own OpenFOAM simulations based solely on natural-language instructions. Our model is pre-trained on the Qwen-coder 2.5-14B, which is then fine-tuned on 252 text prompts targeting 7 OpenFOAM solvers, 13 parametrized mesh templates, and a y plus-aware numerical policy. The crucial element of the algorithm is a sophisticated evolution loop composed of 7 stages. To prevent model degeneration under repeated self-training, the agent employs three complementary anti-collapse streams: RAG-augmented retry context, surgical dictionary-level patching, and prompt-diversity paraphrasing. By bridging generative artificial intelligence with rigorous fluid simulations, AutoFOAM accelerates rapid prototyping and democratizes advanced CFD workflows.

</details>


### [104] [Enhancing LLMs with Context-Specific Knowledge for Mitigating Misinformation in SMEs: A RAG-based Modeling and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00006)
*Md. Samiul Islam,Iqbal H. Sarker,Chadni Islam,Ahmad Mohsin,Ahmed Ibrahim,Helge Janicke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了VectorRAG和GraphRAG两种检索增强生成方法，用于减少中小企业在使用大语言模型时的幻觉和错误信息风险。研究在LLaMA、Mistral和Qwen等多个LLM上进行了实验评估，结果显示RAG增强的LLM能显著提高响应质量，减少幻觉，支持更可靠和可信的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在中小企业中被广泛采用以增强问答和决策支持能力，但其生成输出中的幻觉现象可能成为错误信息的来源，降低用户对LLM可靠性和可信度的信心。因此需要有效的方法来缓解这一问题。

Method: 提出VectorRAG和GraphRAG两种检索增强生成建模方法，通过将外部知识源纳入建模过程来缓解幻觉和错误信息风险，并在多种先进LLM（LLaMA、Mistral、Qwen）上进行实验评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，RAG增强的LLM能够显著提升有用响应的生成质量，降低幻觉风险，提高上下文相关性，并改善人工可解释性，从而在中小企业环境中支持更可靠、更值得信赖的决策。

Conclusion: RAG方法（包括向量检索和知识图谱检索）是缓解LLM幻觉问题的有效途径，能够显著提升中小企业环境中AI驱动的问答和决策支持系统的可靠性与可信度。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs), a part of artificial intelligence (AI), are increasingly being adopted by Small and Medium Enterprises (SMEs) to enhance question-answering capabilities and support business decision-making processes. However, hallucinations in LLM-generated outputs can serve as a source of misinformation, reducing user confidence in their reliability and trustworthiness within SMEs. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a promising approach to address this challenge by incorporating external knowledge sources into the modeling process. In this paper, we present VectorRAG and GraphRAG modeling approaches to mitigate hallucinations and misinformation risks and evaluate their effectiveness in SME environments. Our experimental evaluation is conducted on multiple state-of-the-art LLMs, including LLaMA, Mistral, and Qwen, to assess performance in terms of useful response generation, risk of hallucination, contextual relevance, as well as human-interpretation. The results demonstrate that RAG-enhanced LLMs can significantly improve response quality by reducing hallucinations and misinformation, thereby supporting more reliable, trustworthy, and context-aware decision-making in SME environments.

</details>


### [105] [Energy Efficiency of Locally Deployed LLMs: A Preliminary Quantitative GPU Power Benchmark on Consumer Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00008)
*Philipp M. Zähl,Anika Hennig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可复现的硬件级能源基准测试，在RTX 4060Ti GPU上通过Ollama推理引擎评估了9个开源LLM（1B到7B参数量）的能耗表现，发现模型架构和量化策略比参数数量更能驱动能源效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着本地部署LLM的需求增长（出于隐私和本地推理考量），但消费者硬件上的能耗成本尚未得到充分刻画，现有基准大多只关注准确性而忽略能源效率。

Method: 在单个消费者GPU（RTX 4060Ti 16GB）上，使用Ollama推理引擎对9个开源LLM（1B至7B参数）执行固定提示集；通过nvidia-smi以2Hz频率采样GPU功耗，评估平均/峰值功耗、每提示总能量（J/prompt）、每输出token能量（J/token）和吞吐量（tok/s）。

Result: gemma3:1b和llama3.2:1b实现了最低能耗成本（0.56 J/token和0.65 J/token）和最高吞吐量（>170 tok/s）；7B-Mistral模型每token能耗比最高效模型高4.4倍；qwen3.5:2b因扩展内部推理导致每提示能耗异常偏高。

Conclusion: 模型架构和量化策略等参数数量以外的因素对能源效率起关键驱动作用；效率指标中需要区分不同的token生成模式（如推理/生成），以更准确评估模型能耗表现。

Abstract: The local deployment of large language models (LLMs) is gaining traction due to privacy concerns and the desire for on-premise inference. However, the energy costs on consumer hardware remain poorly characterized, as most benchmarks focus solely on accuracy. This paper presents a reproducible, hardware-level energy benchmark of nine open-source LLMs (1B to 7B parameters) executed on a single consumer GPU (RTX 4060Ti 16GB). Using the Ollama inference engine, GPU power draw was sampled at 2Hz via nvidia-smi across a fixed prompt set. We evaluate mean/peak power, total energy per prompt (J/prompt), energy per output token (J/token), and throughput (tok/s). Our findings suggest that factors beyond raw parameter count, including model architecture and quantization strategy, drive energy efficiency. Specifically, gemma3:1b and llama3.2:1b achieve the lowest energy cost (0.56 J/token and 0.65 J/token) and the highest throughput (>170 tok/s). In contrast, the 7B-Mistral model consumes up to 4.4x more energy per token than the most efficient model. Notably, qwen3.5:2b exhibits anomalously high per-prompt energy due to extended internal reasoning, highlighting the need to distinguish between token generation modes in efficiency metrics.

</details>


### [106] [CoT-Core: Accelerating LLM Evaluation via CoT-Aware Coreset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00014)
*Qihua Pan,Zhenheng Tang,Peijie Dong,Xiang Liu,Huacan Wang,Bo Li,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CoT-Core框架，利用零样本思维链推理轨迹将问题投影到潜在空间，按内在逻辑等价性而非表面文本相似度进行聚类，实现免训练的核问题选择，大幅降低LLM评估成本同时保持高精度分数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评估在持续开发中计算开销巨大，现有核心集选择方法存在两个缺陷：基于IRT等传统方法需要大量历史日志，存在严重的"冷启动"瓶颈；基于表面词汇相似度的方法忽略了任务背后的推理流形。

Method: CoT-Core包含三步：(1) 提示LLM生成零样本思维链(CoT)推理轨迹；(2) 将这些轨迹投影到潜在空间；(3) 基于逻辑等价性对问题聚类，选择代表性核心问题集合。该方法无需训练，不依赖历史评估日志。

Result: 在GSM8K、MMLU、MMLU-Pro和GPQA数据集上的实验表明，CoT-Core大幅降低评估成本同时保持高保真度的分数估计，同时揭示了推理感知剪枝的边界条件——其有效性受任务复杂度内在门槛限制。

Conclusion: CoT-Core通过引入推理轨迹来捕捉问题间的逻辑等价性，有效解决了冷启动和表面词汇偏差问题，但需要注意其效果随任务复杂度变化，并非在所有场景下均适用。

Abstract: Evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) incurs prohibitive computational overhead during continuous development processes. While coreset selection accelerates evaluation, existing methods either suffer from a severe ``cold start'' bottleneck requiring massive historical logs (e.g., Item Response Theory) or exhibit a surface lexical bias that misses the underlying reasoning manifold of tasks. We propose CoT-Core, a novel training-free core question selection framework. Recognizing that lexically disparate questions can share equivalent underlying logic, CoT-Core prompts LLMs to unroll zero-shot Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning trajectories. Projecting these paths into a latent space effectively clusters questions by intrinsic logical equivalence rather than superficial text similarity. Extensive experiments on GSM8K, MMLU, MMLU-Pro, and GPQA demonstrate that CoT-Core drastically reduces evaluation costs while maintaining high-fidelity score estimation, and delineate the boundary conditions of reasoning-aware pruning, revealing that its efficacy is intrinsically gated by task complexity.

</details>


### [107] [Optimization and Constraint Modeling using LLMs with a Retrieval Augmented Generation Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00015)
*Prateek Roy,Akash Singirikonda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于检索增强生成（RAG）的流水线，通过构建包含500个合成优化问题的向量数据库，为大语言模型提供语义相似的上下文示例，从而显著提升优化建模的准确性和结构完整性。在Qwen 3 30B Instruct模型下，NL4OPT准确率从40%提升至72%，MAMO Easy从40%提升至56%，MAMO Complex从32%提升至56%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化建模和约束建模需要深厚的领域专业知识和建模语言熟练度，而现有LLM在组合优化场景下频繁产生结构不一致或不完整的优化公式。作者希望探索一种无需昂贵模型微调的替代方案，以提升LLM在优化建模任务上的表现。

Method: 首先基于Text2Zinc数据集的种子描述和LLM生成的专业人员角色，合成了500个优化问题（以JSON格式指定并配有经过验证的Python求解脚本），然后编码存入Chroma向量数据库。推理时，通过语义相似度检索相关问题的示例作为上下文指导，交由LangChain LLM智能体完成建模。使用三个基准测试集（NL4OPT、MAMO Easy、MAMO Complex）在Qwen 3 30B Instruct模型下进行评测。

Result: NL4OPT准确率从40%提升到72%，MAMO Easy从40%提升到56%，MAMO Complex从32%提升到56%，均取得显著提升。语义验证的合成示例同时改善了方案的求解准确性和结构完整性。

Conclusion: 合成数据集生成与检索增强相结合的方案是模型微调的有效替代方案，领域特定的合成语料库配以检索增强可以作为在真实决策支持场景中部署LLM优化工具的实际可行路径，无需昂贵的模型重训练。

Abstract: Both optimization modeling and constraint modeling are non-trivial problems requiring deep domain expertise and proficiency in modeling formalism languages. Despite their importance across logistics, healthcare, and supply chain management, current large language models regularly produce structurally inconsistent or incomplete optimization formulations, particularly in combinatorial settings. This paper evaluates whether a Retrieval-Augmented Generation pipeline built on a curated synthetic dataset can meaningfully improve LLM optimization modeling performance. A total of 500 optimization problems were synthesized using seed descriptions from the Text2Zinc dataset and professional personas created using an LLM, specified in JSON and associated with validated Python solver scripts. These problems were encoded in a Chroma vector database. For each inference problem, semantically similar problems were retrieved and used as contextual guidance for a LangChain LLM agent. Three benchmark testbeds were used to evaluate the proposed pipeline under the Qwen 3 30B Instruct model. Accuracy rose from 40% to 72% on NL4OPT, 40% to 56% on MAMO Easy, and 32% to 56% on MAMO Complex. The use of semantically validated synthetic examples greatly improves both solution accuracy and structure. The combination of synthetic dataset generation with retrieval augmentation provides an effective alternative to fine-tuning, suggesting that domain-specific synthetic corpora paired with retrieval augmentation can serve as a practical pathway for deploying LLM-based optimization tools in real-world decision-support contexts without costly model retraining.

</details>


### [108] [Memory Reward Inflation in Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00017)
*Mohammad Asadolahi,Amir Amini,Samira Talebi,Amirfarhad Farhadi,Azadeh Zamanifar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于外部记忆的自我改进型LLM智能体中的一个系统性缺陷——"回声鸿沟"（Echo Gap）：当存储的奖励分数由LLM自评而非真实标签产生时，错误样本会获得虚高奖励并被优先复用，导致错误在记忆中复合而非抵消。作者形式化了纠错所需的"误差独立性假设"（EIA），证明其为必要条件的闭式解，并提出了无答案去膨胀算法LUCID，在BIRD文本转SQL基准上将执行准确率提升至56.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我改进型LLM智能体通过外部记忆存储经验、评分和检索来改进行为，无需更新权重。然而在部署场景中缺乏真实标签，存储的奖励分数只能来自LLM自评，这种替代可能产生系统性偏差。作者想揭示并解决这个根本性的失败模式。

Method: 作者首先识别并形式化"回声鸿沟"（Echo Gap）问题，指出错误记忆被高估导致智能体优先复用最有信心的错误。然后形式化"误差独立性假设"（EIA），证明它是纠正奖励膨胀的必要条件，并推导出可恢复收益的闭式解。最后提出无答案的去膨胀算法LUCID，用于校正存储分数。

Result: 在BIRD文本转SQL基准上，LUCID算法将执行准确率提升至56.9%，高于Memento式自评智能体的54.0%（平均提升+2.9个百分点），也高于无记忆的同等架构智能体的52.4%。

Conclusion: 记忆偏差导致的奖励膨胀是自我改进型LLM智能体的根本性缺陷，其并非通过简单平均化消失，而是通过记忆复合放大。纠正它必须同时要求信号追踪真实性和误差去相关（EIA），LUCID算法证明了在无需真实答案的情况下可以有效地降低这种偏差并提升性能。

Abstract: Self-improving LLM agents increasingly learn from experience without updating any weights. Each episode is stored in an external memory, scored, and retrieved for similar future tasks to shape later behavior. Viewed through a reward lens, the stored score is a proxy reward for an implicit, non-parametric policy. Each retrieved episode then becomes a policy-improvement step whose reliability hinges on how that score is produced. In deployment, ground-truth labels are unavailable, so the stored reward is at best an LLM assessment. This substitution creates a failure mode, the *Echo Gap*, across the memory-based self-improving agents and model families studied. Incorrect episodes receive inflated rewards; thus, the agent preferentially reuses the very mistakes it has most confident in. Because the error compounds through memory rather than averaging out and the confirming judge's errors remain correlated with the original self-grading bias, so it cannot identify which memories are overvalued. The missing property is formalized as the *Error-Independence Assumption* (EIA), which we prove is a *necessary* condition for correcting the inflation, not merely a description of a good verifier: a usable signal must track truth *and* decorrelate its error from the memory bias, and the recoverable payoff is a closed-form function of exactly those two quantities. We further show the inflation compounds not only when retrieval ranks by the stored score but also under plain similarity retrieval which is the regime the deployed agent uses. Finally, the answer-free de-inflation algorithm LUCID delivers a consistent end-to-end gain on the BIRD text-to-SQL benchmark. It raises execution accuracy to $56.9\%$, above both a Memento-style self-graded agent ($54.0\%$, a $+2.9$-point mean gain across seeds) and a memory-less agent of identical architecture ($52.4\%$).

</details>


### [109] [Can LLM Agents Price Competitively? A Dynamic Multi-Attribute Auction Benchmark for Agentic Commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00102)
*Shimaa Ahmed,Yiwei Cai,Mohsen Minaei,Rahul Rachuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Bazaar，一个动态密封投标基准，用于系统测试LLM驱动的智能体在多属性拍卖环境中的定价能力。研究发现，前沿LLM在客户获取和利润方面表现不一致，且面对需求冲击时的适应能力存在显著差异，但即便是最强模型也仅能达到事后最优利润的三分之一以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体商务正从概念走向实际部署，支付网络、零售商和AI平台都在为智能体代表商家和消费者进行交易铺路。然而，LLM能否在真实市场环境（客户偏好隐藏、竞争对手实时适应、需求意外波动）中胜任定价任务，尚未被系统性地测试。

Method: 作者设计了Bazaar，一个动态密封投标基准，用于测试多属性拍卖环境下的定价能力。基准虽然具有动态特性，但基于闭式客户效用函数构建，从而支持精确评估。研究覆盖了来自四个提供商的11个前沿LLM。

Result: 研究发现：客户获取领先的智能体（如Gemini 3.1 Pro）往往不是利润领先者（如Opus 4.6）；需求冲击下排名再次变化，冲击前学习最快的智能体通常在冲击后修订信念最慢，而Gemini 3.1 Pro恢复最快；但即使是最强智能体也仅捕获不到事后最优利润的三分之一。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在智能体商务领域已有进展，但距离实现接近最优的定价决策仍有相当大的提升空间。这一基准为评估和推动LLM在真实市场条件下的商务能力提供了系统化的测试框架。

Abstract: Agentic commerce is moving from concept to deployed infrastructure: payment networks, retailers, and AI platforms are setting the stage for agents to transact on behalf of merchants and consumers. Yet whether the LLMs behind these agents can price competently in real markets, where customer preferences are hidden, competitors adapt in real time, and demand can shift without warning, has not been systematically tested. We introduce Bazaar, a dynamic sealed-bid benchmark for multi-attribute auction under these conditions. Despite its dynamics, the benchmark is grounded in closed-form customer utilities, enabling exact evaluation. Across 11 frontier LLMs from four providers, the leading agents on customer acquisition (e.g. Gemini 3.1 Pro) are often not the leading agents on profit (e.g. Opus 4.6). The ranking shifts again under demand shocks: agents that learned fastest pre-shock are typically the slowest to revise their beliefs afterwards, while Gemini 3.1 Pro recovers fastest despite not leading on profit. However, even the strongest agent captures less than a third of hindsight-optimal profit, suggesting current LLMs are progressing in agentic commerce but leave substantial headroom.

</details>


### [110] [Request-Level Energy Attribution for Batched LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00026)
*Qi Luo,Kunlin Li,Ziwen Wang,Dongsheng Wang,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JouleShare，一个LLM批量推理服务的请求级能耗归因框架，包含离线实测Shapley能耗真值基准和在线校准模型JCalib，发现token按比例归因与精确Shapley能耗存在显著偏差，而JCalib可将误差大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大批量LLM服务虽提升吞吐但能耗记账复杂：GPU功耗是聚合的，而可持续性报告、费用分摊和工作负载分析需要请求级能耗。现有基准只提供模型/阶段/token级能耗，缺乏实测的请求级真值，实践中使用的记账规则与公平分配的偏差程度一直未知。

Method: JouleShare包含两个组件：(1) 离线基准测试框架，在vLLM下重放请求子集，整合GPU功耗遥测并计算每个请求的精确Shapley能耗作为真值；(2) JCalib轻量校准模型，从廉价请求特征学习预测Shapley分摊值，用于在线服务时。两种能耗计量方法（token按比例归因和连续/静态批处理）被系统性比较。实验覆盖16个模型/工作负载组合和3种数据中心GPU。

Result: 在静态批处理下，token按比例归因与精确Shapley的平均归一化L1偏差为0.440，连续批处理下为0.458，且在3种GPU上均复现。JCalib将误差降至静态批处理0.116和连续批处理0.177，优于不可用的在线独立测量基线，同时保持精确的批次能耗效率。采样Shapley可扩展测量参考至更大规模，偏差持续存在，单次离线校准是最准确的可用规则。

Conclusion: token按比例归因不能可靠地代表批量执行下的边际能耗，实测的Shapley真值可以校准低成本的请求特征，实现更公平的能耗归因。

Abstract: Batched LLM serving improves throughput but complicates energy accounting. GPU power telemetry is aggregate, whereas sustainability reporting, chargeback, and workload analysis often require request-level energy charges. Existing inference-energy benchmarks report model-, phase-, or token-level energy, and recent carbon-accounting work motivates Shapley fairness conceptually. Neither provides measured request-level ground truth, so how far the accounting rules used in practice deviate from a fair allocation has remained unknown. We present JouleShare, an attribution framework with two components. An offline harness establishes this ground truth by replaying request subsets under vLLM with a reproducible protocol, integrating GPU power telemetry, and computing exact Shapley energy for each request. A lightweight calibration model, JCalib, then learns to predict Shapley shares from cheap request features for use at serving time. Across 16 model/workload runs, token-proportional attribution differs from exact Shapley by 0.440 normalized L1 on average under static batching and by 0.458 under continuous batching, a gap that reproduces across three data-center GPUs. JCalib reduces this error to 0.116 under static batching and 0.177 under continuous batching, below even a standalone-measurement baseline that is unavailable online, while preserving exact batch-energy efficiency. Sampled Shapley extends the measured reference to larger group sizes, where the gap persists and a single offline calibration remains the most accurate deployable rule. The results show that token attribution is not a reliable proxy for marginal energy under batched execution, and that measured Shapley ground truth can calibrate low-cost request features toward fairer attribution.

</details>


### [111] [Motif-Mamba: network motif improved mamba for long-range sequence modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00027)
*Chonghe Hao,Yue Sun,Jian Zhang,Yansong Wang,Wangzi Yao,Yunjie Yao,Tielin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Motif-Mamba是一种基于Mamba的结构化状态空间模型，通过引入"motif约束的低秩循环通路"，在保持线性时间复杂度的同时增强了状态维度间的显式交互，在长序列外推、语言建模和脑机接口解码任务上均优于Mamba基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba虽然通过选择性状态空间递归实现了线性时间的长序列建模，但其状态转移矩阵以对角形式为主，限制了状态维度之间的显式交互，这成为其在长序列建模中的结构瓶颈。

Method: 受三节点网络模体（motif）动力学启发，Motif-Mamba在Mamba基础上增加一个motif约束的低秩循环通路：先将隐藏状态投影到紧凑的动力学子空间，施加模体引导的维度间交互，再将动力学结果映射回原始状态空间。该设计在保留Mamba线性时间递归结构的同时，增强了跨维度通信能力。

Result: 在长序列外推、语言建模基准测试以及脑机接口解码三个任务上，Motif-Mamba均取得了相对Mamba后端的持续改进，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: Motif引导的低秩动力学提供了一种有效的结构先验，可用于长序列建模，在不牺牲计算效率的前提下改善了Mamba的状态维度交互能力。

Abstract: Efficient long-sequence modeling remains a central challenge for large language models, as self-attention scales quadratically with sequence length. Mamba offers a linear-time alternative through selective state space recurrence, but its predominantly diagonal state transitions restrict explicit interactions among state dimensions. We propose Motif-Mamba, a structured state space model that augments Mamba with a motif-constrained low-rank recurrent pathway. Inspired by the dynamics of three-node network motifs, the proposed pathway projects hidden states into a compact dynamical subspace, imposes motif-guided interactions, and maps the resulting dynamics back to the original state space. This design enhances cross-dimensional communication while preserving the linear-time recurrent structure of Mamba. Experiments on long-sequence extrapolation, language modeling benchmarks, and brain--computer interface decoding show consistent improvements over Mamba backbones, suggesting that motif-guided low-rank dynamics provide an effective structural prior for long-range sequence modeling.

</details>


### [112] [HetGPS: Scalable Graph Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning with Physics-Anchored Adaptive Safety for EV Charging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00679)
*Xiangwei Wang,Nanduni Nimalsiri,Yu Xia,Peng Wang,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HetGPS，一个混合图控制框架，通过将干预幅度与矫正方向分离，结合学习图风险和物理锚定矫正，在保障电网共享约束的同时最小化对任务导向策略的干扰。在EV充电场景中，该方法将电压违规率从3.93-7.74%降至0.52-3.44%，同时保持99%以上的出发成功率，模型规模与车队规模无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模网络耦合智能体的安全干预需要在保护共享约束的同时，避免过度覆盖任务导向的策略决策。现有方法难以在保障安全性与保留策略自主性之间取得良好平衡。

Method: HetGPS采用混合图控制框架，分离干预幅度与矫正方向：动作条件图残差模型根据状态调度干预权限，物理模型确定矫正方向。在EV充电场景中，将该滤波器与参数共享的异质图软演员-评论家（SAC）策略耦合，实现拓扑感知协调，且模型大小与车队规模无关。

Result: 在5个嵌套配电网（200-3218辆EV）和100个评估日上，自适应权限将电压违规率降为0.52-3.44%，出发成功率达99.06-100%；相比固定权限的物理投射，5个网络的平均奖励均提升，4个网络的平均安全分数降低。模型仅383,702个参数，而3218辆EV时匹配的集中式SAC策略约大170倍；训练的8变压器系统策略可零样本迁移到16和32变压器系统。

Conclusion: 学习到的图风险能够在规模上有效分配干预权限，同时馈线物理模型锚定矫正行动，验证了物理-学习混合方法在大规模网络耦合智能体安全干预中的可行性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Safety interventions for large populations of network-coupled agents must protect shared constraints without unnecessarily overriding task-oriented policy decisions. We present HetGPS, a hybrid graph-control framework synergizing learned graph risk with physics-anchored correction by separating intervention magnitude from corrective direction. An action-conditioned graph residual model schedules state-dependent intervention authority, while a physics model determines its direction. For electric vehicle (EV) charging, we couple this filter with a parameter-shared heterogeneous graph soft actor-critic policy, enabling topology-aware coordination with a learned model size independent of fleet size. Across five nested distribution networks with 200--3,218 EVs and 100 evaluation days, Adaptive Authority reduces bus--step voltage violations from 3.93--7.74\% without filtering to 0.52--3.44\%, while maintaining 99.06--100\% departure success. Relative to the same physics-directed projection with fixed authority, it improves mean reward on all five networks and lowers the mean safety score on four. The deployed policy-and-risk model contains 383,702 learned parameters at every scale; at 3,218 EVs, a matched centralized SAC actor is about $170\times$ larger. A policy trained on the eight-transformer system transfers zero-shot to the 16- and 32-transformer systems, attaining 0.57--0.75\% violation rates and at least 99.99\% departure success. These results show that learned graph risk can allocate intervention authority at scale while feeder physics anchors corrective action.

</details>


### [113] [Nova: An End-to-End MLIR Compiler for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00029)
*Adwaid Suresh,Aparna A,Harshini V M,Jona Delcy C A,Killi Uma Maheswara Rao,Ram Charan Golla,Surendra Vendra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Nova，一个自动化端到端即时编译器，通过捕获急切执行、统一前向和反向传播为单一值语义方言，实现跨操作融合、内存层次优化和寄存器级调优，在RTX 3060上匹配或超越cuBLAS和XLA性能，模型吞吐量最高提升10.6%，内存占用降低29%，并能训练144百万参数模型而PyTorch在同一GPU上遭遇OOM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能深度学习模型在规模化部署时，高层张量框架的急切执行模式缺乏全图可见性和对硬件/内存的细粒度控制，无法原生最大化物理硬件利用率，需要一种能够实现绝对硬件映射控制的编译器来弥合这一差距。

Method: Nova采用三步方案：(1) 捕获急切执行并将前向和反向传播统一为单一值语义方言，实现全图优化；(2) 利用解析配置器(Analytic Configurator)基于算术强度确定性推导最优执行调度，将搜索时间降至零；(3) 通过结构化哈希运行时直接根据计算结构合成细粒度内核。

Result: 在RTX 3060上，Nova在大多数形状的TF32矩阵乘法上匹配或略超cuBLAS和XLA（相对误差<5e-4）；模型层面比PyTorch提升10.6%吞吐量、比XLA提升4.4%；内存占用较PyTorch降低29%，能以17,900 tokens/s训练144百万参数模型，而PyTorch在同一12GB GPU上遭遇OOM。

Conclusion: Nova通过将急切执行转化为可全图优化的统一方言，并结合解析式调度推导和结构化内核合成，在无需搜索的情况下实现了接近或超越现有主流框架的硬件利用效率，同时显著降低内存足迹，使大型模型能够在消费级GPU上训练。

Abstract: The performance of deep learning models at scale relies heavily on how effectively high-level mathematical operations are mapped to underlying physical hardware. While high-level tensor frameworks provide flexible abstractions for model design, their eager execution models inherently lack the whole-graph visibility and granular control over hardware and memory required to maximize physical hardware utilization natively. To bridge this gap, we designed Nova, an automated end-to-end JIT compiler whose defining purpose is to achieve absolute control over this hardware mapping: fusing operations across operation boundaries, optimizing complex memory hierarchies, and tuning execution down to the register level. By capturing eager executions and unifying forward and backward passes into a single value-semantic dialect, Nova unlocks aggressive whole-graph optimizations. It then utilizes an Analytic Configurator to deterministically derive optimal execution schedules based on arithmetic intensity, dropping search time to zero. Backed by a structural hashing runtime, Nova synthesizes fine-grained kernels directly from the computation's structure. In our evaluations on an RTX 3060, Nova matches or modestly exceeds cuBLAS and XLA on TF32 matmuls on most shapes, maintaining a stringent < 5e-4 relative error. At the model level, Nova achieves up to 10.6% greater throughput than PyTorch and 4.4% greater than XLA on a 42-million parameter model, without compromising on numerical fidelity. Crucially, by reducing the memory footprint by up to 29% relative to PyTorch, Nova successfully trains a 144-million parameter model at 17,900 tokens/s where PyTorch encounters Out-Of-Memory (OOM) failures on the same 12 GB consumer GPU.

</details>


### [114] [SIRIN: A Unified Toolkit for Detecting Contextual Hallucinations in Retrieval-Augmented and Memory-Grounded LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00033)
*Julia Belikova,Rauf Parchiev,Mikhail Filimonov,Konstantin Polev,Andrey Savchenko,Maksim Makarenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SIRIN是一个统一的工具包和交互式Web界面，用于检测检索增强、智能体驱动和记忆接地LLM系统中的上下文幻觉（流畅但不受证据支持的响应）。它统一了三种检测器范式（表示探测、不确定性估计和法官式验证），并支持生成前查询可回答性任务，提供响应级和跨度级的检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强和智能体LLM系统中存在上下文幻觉问题（生成的响应流畅但缺乏证据支持），现有检测工具分散且缺乏统一接口，难以进行系统化的比较和评估。

Method: SIRIN通过统一三个检测器范式（表示探测、不确定性估计、法官式验证）以及生成前查询可回答性任务，提供统一的配置系统和评估管道。支持白盒和黑盒设置下的响应级与跨度级检查，Web UI支持实时分析用户提供的上下文-查询-答案三元组，并提供幻觉评分、不支持跨度高亮和并排检测器比较。

Result: 在幻觉检测、查询可回答性以及长期记忆系统中的忠实度门控任务上进行了演示验证。源代码已公开在GitHub上。

Conclusion: SIRIN提供了一个统一、可扩展且交互式的幻觉检测工具包，便于研究者比较不同检测范式，并支持在多种LLM系统场景中作为忠实度门控使用。

Abstract: SIRIN (Semantic Inconsistency Recognition and Inspection Nexus) is a unified toolkit and interactive web UI for detecting contextual hallucinations (fluent, plausible responses unsupported by the provided evidence) in retrieval-augmented, agentic, and memory-grounded LLM systems. SIRIN unifies three detector paradigms (representation probing, uncertainty estimation, and judge-style verification) and the complementary task of pre-generation query answerability under one interface, configuration system, and evaluation pipeline, supporting response- and span-level inspection in both white-box and black-box settings. The web UI enables live analysis of user-supplied context-query-answer triples through hallucination scores, unsupported-span highlighting, and side-by-side detector comparison, with a lightweight plug-in design for adding new detectors. We demonstrate SIRIN on hallucination detection, query answerability, and as a faithfulness gate within long-term memory systems.
  The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/sb-ai-lab/SIRIN.

</details>


### [115] [Linguistic Context Recodes Visual Representations in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00035)
*Brian Song,Michael A. Lepori,Ellie Pavlick*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过实验证明了视觉语言模型（VLMs）中语言提示会主动"重编码"视觉表征，具体表现为两种机制：抽象参考表征（识别目标相关对象）和属性调制（放大目标相关属性），并通过因果干预验证了这些机制对模型预测的直接影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究将VLM中的视觉表征视为静态信息仓库，由语言表征进行操作，但语言目标导向处理是否会导致视觉表征本身被主动重编码这一问题尚未被充分刻画。

Method: 1) 提取对比转向向量（contrastive steering vectors）识别抽象参考表征，并通过因果干预验证其对预测的影响；2) 分析后续层对视觉表征中目标相关属性的选择性放大（属性调制）；3) 通过因果干预证明属性调制中介了VLM的响应分布。

Result: 发现两种语言诱导的视觉重编码现象：抽象参考表征在不同对象、任务上下文乃至合成到自然图像之间泛化；属性调制在多种提示下均出现，且因果干预证明其调控模型响应。

Conclusion: VLM中的视觉令牌并非静态信息仓库，而是被动态调制以支持语言表达的任务查询，这为跨模态处理提供了更动态的解释框架。

Abstract: Goal-directed visual processing is a hallmark of human visual intelligence, resulting in representations that support downstream tasks such as categorization or search. Though vision-language models (VLMs) are often faced with these same tasks, their ability to recode visual representations when presented with goal-directed language remains poorly characterized. Indeed, prior work largely treats visual representations in VLMs as static repositories of visual information that are manipulated by language representations. In the present work, we provide evidence for two concrete instances of language-induced recoding of visual representations. First, we identify an abstract reference representation that denotes which objects are goal-relevant under a natural language prompt. We extract contrastive steering vectors corresponding to this reference representation and demonstrate that they are causally implicated in model predictions. These reference representations are abstract in that they generalize to different objects, different task contexts, and even from synthetic to naturalistic images. Second, we demonstrate language-induced attribute modulation: later layers selectively amplify goal-relevant attributes in visual representations of objects. We demonstrate this phenomenon across a range of different prompts. Finally, we provide a causal intervention that demonstrates that attribute modulation mediates a VLM's response distribution. Together, our results support a more dynamic account of cross-modality processing in VLMs -- rather than vision tokens serving as static repositories of information, they are modulated to support queries articulated in language.

</details>


### [116] [Co-evolution of social reward and punishment under institutional interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01183)
*Van An Nguyen,Vuong Khang Huynh,Hoai Thuong Nguyen,Duc Tin Duong,An Nguyen Gia,Tat Kien Nguyen,Huu Loi Bui,My Nguyen Tra,Ho Nam Duong,Ba Thanh Phan,Thanh Vo,Dinh Anh Trung Hoang,Adeela Bashir,Zhao Song,Manh Hong Duong,Le Hong Trang,The Anh Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了同伴激励与制度激励如何共同影响社会困境中合作、社会福利和执法效率的演化，在四种策略的囚徒困境中同时允许去中心化同伴激励和中心化制度激励，发现同伴惩罚最促进合作、同伴奖励更利于社会福利，制度性奖励同伴激励策略能显著提升合作与福利，而制度惩罚中只有直接惩罚背叛者始终有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会困境中合作行为的维持是社会科学的核心问题，但以往研究多单独考察同伴激励或制度激励，缺乏对二者联合作用的系统分析，需要理解制度如何调控去中心化激励以实现合作与社会福利的平衡。

Method: 构建包含无条件合作者(C)、背叛者(D)、社会惩罚者(SP)和社会奖励者(SR)四种策略的囚徒困境模型；在无限混合均匀群体中采用四策略复制动力学分析，在结构化群体中利用方格上的基于智能体模拟研究空间效应和网络互惠，通过均衡态和演化流评估无限群体，通过合作水平和社会福利（扣除制度成本的总体收益）评估结构化群体。

Result: 同伴惩罚最有效促进合作，同伴奖励对社会福利更有利；制度性奖励同伴激励策略可显著改善合作与福利，补贴无条件合作者影响甚微；在制度惩罚下，直接惩罚背叛者是唯一始终有效的政策，惩罚同伴激励策略会破坏去中心化激励、降低合作并损害社会福利，表明最大化合作不一定优化整体社会效益。

Conclusion: 研究为机构在促进合作与最大化社会福利之间寻求平衡提供了设计原则，揭示了同伴激励与制度激励的协同机制及不同干预策略的差异化效果。

Abstract: We investigate how peer and institutional incentives jointly shape the evolution of cooperation, social welfare, and enforcement efficiency in social dilemmas. In a Prisoners Dilemma with four strategies, unconditional cooperators (C), defectors (D), social punishers (SP), and social rewarders (SR), we allow decentralised peer incentives and centralised institutional incentives to act simultaneously, with the institution able to reward or punish any subset of strategies. In infinite well-mixed populations, we analyse the resulting four-strategy replicator dynamics, and in structured populations we use agent-based simulations on square lattices to study spatial effects and network reciprocity. Intervention schemes are evaluated by equilibrium states and evolutionary flow for infinite well-mixed populations, by cooperation levels and social welfare for structured populations, defined as aggregate population payoff net of institutional cost. We find that peer punishment most strongly promotes cooperation, whereas peer reward is more beneficial for social welfare. Institutionally rewarding peer incentive strategies substantially improves both cooperation and welfare, while subsidising unconditional cooperators has little impact. Under institutional punishment, directly penalising defectors is the only consistently effective policy; punishing peer incentive strategies dismantles decentralised incentives, reduces cooperation, and harms social welfare, showing that maximising cooperation does not necessarily optimise overall societal benefit. Our findings provide design principles for institutions seeking to balance cooperation promotion with welfare maximisation.

</details>


### [117] [RAG-TESTER: Automated End-to-End Testing of Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00054)
*Ange Maiztegi,Jon Ayerdi,Miren Illarramendi,Aitor Arrieta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RagTester，一种面向RAG系统的自动化端到端测试方法，通过生成检索文档、测试输入和预期输出，并使用LLM作为裁判评估回答，在8个LLM和6个嵌入模型组成的24种配置下进行了72,000次测试执行，检测出21,633个故障，比基线方法多6.6%，在20/24配置中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统的可靠性取决于生成模型、嵌入模型、检索机制和提示构造策略之间的交互，但目前缺乏自动化端到端测试方法来系统性地评估这些组件的协同效果，特别是检索与生成之间的交互故障。

Method: RagTester的方法包括：（1）生成检索文档、测试输入和预期输出；（2）执行测试；（3）使用LLM作为裁判评估答案。其测试生成策略针对复杂段落、不支持查询和文档覆盖率三个标准。

Result: 在8个LLM和6个嵌入模型组成的24种配置、72,000次测试执行中，RagTester检测到21,633个故障，比基线方法多6.6%，并在20/24配置中优于基线。检测到的故障类型包括检索不准确、不支持的回答、未完整使用检索上下文以及解释复杂段落的困难。

Conclusion: 覆盖率导向的测试生成能有效暴露由检索与生成组件交互引起的故障，可支持RAG配置在部署前的评估与选择。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enables Large Language Models (LLMs) to use external and domain-specific knowledge, but its reliability depends on the interaction between the generative model, embedding model, retrieval mechanism, and prompt construction strategy. We present RagTester, an automated end-to-end testing approach for RAG systems. RagTester generates retrieval documents, test inputs, and expected outputs; executes the tests; and evaluates the resulting answers using an LLM as a judge. Its test-generation strategy targets complex passages, unsupported queries, and document-coverage criteria. We evaluate RagTester using eight LLMs and six embedding models, yielding 24 compatible configurations, and compare it with a baseline test-input generator. Across 72,000 test executions, RagTester detected 21,633 failures, 6.6% more than the baseline, and outperformed it in 20 of the 24 configurations. The detected failures include inaccurate retrieval, unsupported answers, incomplete use of retrieved context, and difficulties interpreting complex passages. These results show that coverage-oriented test generation can effectively expose failures caused by the interaction between retrieval and generation components and support the assessment of RAG configurations before deployment.

</details>


### [118] [Humans Are More Diverse: Frontier LLMs Show Extreme Policies in Idealised AI Development Races](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01193)
*Phu Hoa Pham,Duy Minh Dao Sy,Trung Kiet Huynh,Phu Quy Nguyen Lam,Chi Nguyen Tran,Minh Trung Le,Phong Hao Le,Dinh Nam Nguyen,Thien Ky Nguyen Dong,Elias Fernandez Domingos,Le Hong Trang,The Anh Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究AI开发竞赛中的多智能体安全困境，通过在2-5人竞赛中测试LLM代理的策略安全行为。作者在行为解释前设置了审计关卡，验证游戏引擎、规则记忆、状态追踪、收益计算等，发现强大规则记忆可伴随弱状态追踪与收益计算，聚合率掩盖了不同模型间的大差异，结果提示多智能体AI竞赛模拟需要有效性检查和轨迹级分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI开发竞赛情境下，公司可选择缓慢安全开发或冒险快速开发，形成多智能体安全困境。研究旨在探索LLM代理在这种重复博弈中的策略安全行为，但需先验证代理是否真正理解游戏而非仅产生有效动作。

Method: 设置审计关卡：先验证游戏引擎，再测试规则记忆、状态追踪、收益计算及在不同但等效任务描述下的稳定性；然后将LLM动作序列与进化博弈论基准和已发表的人类数据比较，并探索不同模型、风险条件、人设及2-5人竞赛下的差异。

Result: 审计显示强规则记忆可与弱状态追踪和期望收益计算共存；提供已验证的算术和改变响应表示方式可在规则不变的情况下改变后续动作。七个模型端点的聚合率掩盖了动作序列、对对手响应和竞赛位置响应上的巨大差异；3-5人竞赛的模式也是模型特异的，而非单纯增加竞争者带来的单一效应。

Conclusion: 多智能体AI竞赛模拟在将其输出描述为战略性、类人或安全感知之前，需要进行有效性检查和轨迹级分析。研究发现是探索性的，仅适用于所测试的模型、提示和采样设置。

Abstract: An AI development race creates a multi-agent safety dilemma. Each company can develop slowly and safely, or move faster while taking a risk that may remove its final reward. We use this repeated game to study strategic safety behaviour among large language model (LLM) agents in races with two to five players. However, a valid action does not show that an agent understands the game. We therefore place an audit gate before behavioural interpretation. We first verify the game engine, then test rule recall, state tracking, payoff calculation, and stability under different but equivalent task descriptions. We then compare LLM action sequences with an evolutionary game-theory benchmark and published human data, and explore differences across models, risk conditions, personas, and two- to five-player races. The audit shows that strong rule recall can coexist with weak state tracking and expected-payoff calculation. Providing verified arithmetic and changing the response representation can also change later actions, even when the game rules stay fixed. Across seven tested model endpoints, aggregate rates hide large differences in action sequences, responses to opponents, and responses to race position. Patterns across the tested three- to five-player races are also model-specific rather than a single effect of adding competitors. These results show why multi-agent AI-race simulations need validity checks and trajectory-level analysis before their outputs are described as strategic, human-like, or safety-aware. Our findings are exploratory and apply only to the tested models, prompts, and decoding settings.

</details>


### [119] [H+ Embedding: Harmonizing Global and Token-Level Retrieval with Context-Dependent Phrases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00065)
*Shusen Zhang,Junyi Hu,Ye Feng,Ziteng Wang,Zhaoyuan Pan,Guosheng Dong,Xiaojun Yuan,Jiangshou Hong,Xiangzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出H+ Embedding，一种统一的多粒度检索器，通过预测变长短语分区并在全局向量与token级交互之间建立中间检索单元，在16个科学、医学和双语任务上，短语检索分支比全局检索分支提升6.91 macro nDCG@10，同时比Token方法少用13.7%的文档向量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 术语密集型检索（尤其在医学领域）需要保留多词实体、缩写、数字约束和组合概念，但现有表征处于两个极端：单向量检索器过度压缩局部相关性信号，而token级延迟交互因保留所有子词导致高昂的索引、存储和评分成本。作者希望探索上下文相关短语是否能作为介于全局向量与token之间的有用检索单元。

Method: 提出H+ Embedding，一个统一的多粒度检索器，核心包括：(1) 预测变长短语分区；(2) 将未覆盖的token保留为单例（singleton）；(3) 采用重要性引导的单元选择（importance-guided unit selection）与加权MaxSim交互。

Result: 在16个科学、医学和双语任务上，短语检索分支比全局检索分支提升6.91 macro nDCG@10；几乎匹配Token方法的性能，同时减少13.7%的文档向量；在中等的向量预算下，优于基于内容无关的分组规则。

Conclusion: 上下文相关的短语交互为实际检索系统提供了介于全局压缩与token级交互之间的质量-成本折中点。

Abstract: Terminology-intensive retrieval, especially in medical settings, depends on preserving multi-word entities, abbreviations, numerical constraints, and compositional concepts. However, existing representations lie at two extremes: single-vector retrievers often over-compress local relevance signals, while token-level late interaction retains every tokenizer subword at substantial indexing, storage, and scoring cost. This mismatch raises a natural question: can context-dependent phrases provide a useful retrieval unit between global vectors and tokens? We introduce H+ Embedding, a unified multi-granularity retriever that predicts variable-length phrase partitions, preserves uncovered tokens as singletons, and applies importance-guided unit selection with weighted MaxSim interaction. Across 16 scientific, medical, and bilingual tasks, its phrase retrieval branch exceeds the global retrieval branch by 6.91 macro nDCG@10. It also nearly matches Token while using 13.7% fewer document vectors and outperforms content-independent grouping rules under moderate vector budgets. Context-dependent phrase interaction therefore provides an intermediate quality-cost point between global compression and token-level interaction for practical retrieval systems.

</details>


### [120] [CRAFTS: Collaborative Role-Adaptive Fine-Tuning of LLM Agents for Chemical Process Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01369)
*Ziyun Zhang,Yuxin Lin,Eldin Wee Chuan Lim,Xinghao Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CRAFTS是一个将化学过程模拟构建任务分解为七个有界角色子任务的多智能体框架，通过确定性IDAES/Pyomo门控机制连接各阶段，在450例IDEAES数据集上实现了91.5%的验证和执行合同完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建可执行的化学过程模型目前仍高度依赖人工，化学工程师需要处理单元操作、热力学、流股、规格、自由度、初始化、求解器修复和优化等多重耦合决策，一个错误就可能导致整个模型失效。现有方法缺乏系统化的自动化手段来模拟工程师的分阶段工作流。

Method: CRAFTS将仿真构建分解为七个有界角色子任务：输入理解、意图识别、视觉、拓扑、规格、调试和优化。其中视觉、拓扑和规格三个关键角色使用微调模型，其余角色使用未微调的Qwen。各阶段之间通过确定性的IDAES/Pyomo门控机制进行验证。整个过程产生了VisualGraphIR、TopologyIR、SpecIR、BuildPlan和SolveReport等中间表示，并在工程门控通过后才附加构造器、属性包和运行器。同时引入了OpenIDAES-450数据集用于训练和评估。

Result: 在冻结的82例测试集上，CRAFTS完成了91.5%案例的验证和执行合同，单元、流股和有向连接F1分数分别为0.815、0.791和0.782。

Conclusion: 角色专业化、类型化中间表示和确定性工程门控机制的结合，能够有效实现可靠的自动化过程模型构建，为化学过程仿真领域提供了一个系统化的多智能体解决方案。

Abstract: Constructing an executable chemical-process model remains manually intensive. Chemical engineers translate underspecified requests into coupled decisions about unit operations, thermodynamics, streams, specifications, degrees of freedom (DoF), initialization, solver repair, and optimization; one error can invalidate the model. CRAFTS mirrors the staged workflow of chemical engineers by decomposing simulation building into bounded subtasks assigned to seven bounded roles, with deterministic IDAES/Pyomo gates between stages. Given a natural-language request, process flowsheet diagram (PFD) evidence, and curated chemical-engineering knowledge, Input Understanding and Intent recover requirements, constraints, and process semantics; visual, topology, and specification specialists translate them into typed simulator contracts; and Debug and Optimization support bounded repair and eligible optimization. Fine-tuning is applied to the three schema-critical visual, topology, and specification roles, while the remaining roles use untuned Qwen. The resulting VisualGraphIR, TopologyIR, SpecIR, BuildPlan, and SolveReport expose unit, port, thermodynamic, numerical, and execution decisions. Compatible constructors, property packages, and runners are attached only after semantic artifacts pass engineering gates. We introduce OpenIDAES-450, a 450-case IDAES process- simulation dataset, and evaluate the complete seven-role LangChain/LangGraph workflow through solve and eligible optimization on its frozen 82-case held-out split. CRAFTS completes the prescribed validation and execution contract for for 91.5% of cases and achieves unit, stream, and directed-connection F1 scores of 0.815, 0.791, and 0.782. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of role specialization, typed intermediate representations, and deterministic engineering gates for reliable automated process-model construction.

</details>


### [121] [Agentic Coding in the Wild: Characterizing GitHub Copilot Traces at Production Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00101)
*Banruo Liu,Haoran Qiu,Íñigo Goiri,Rodrigo Fonseca,Ricardo Bianchini,Esha Choukse*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对AI编码智能体（如GitHub Copilot）的生产级工作负载进行大规模特征分析，基于2026年6月采样的32亿用户、1300万会话、7610亿次LLM调用和95万亿token的数据，揭示了智能体编码会话的独特工作负载特性，并提出了一种轻量级空闲时间预测器来优化资源调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码智能体的工作负载与聊天机器人截然不同，但此前缺乏大规模生产环境的实证分析。理解这种新型工作负载的特性对设计高效、面向智能体的基础设施至关重要。

Method: 对GitHub Copilot的生产级trace数据进行大规模统计分析，涵盖用户数、会话数、LLM调用、token消耗等多个维度。特别关注KV缓存命中率、会话结构、空闲时间模式等关键指标，并设计了一个轻量级的空闲时间预测器。

Result: 发现智能体编码会话由稀疏的用户交互回合组成，每个回合展开为自主智能体循环，通常伴随工具执行。回合内KV缓存命中率平均高达90%，但跨回合边界降至55%，模型切换或上下文压缩会导致缓存大幅失效。工作负载呈长尾分布，智能体响应时间快但用户在回合边界有数分钟级空闲。设计的空闲时间预测器能捕获86-90%的总空闲时间。

Conclusion: 这些发现挑战了现有LLM服务系统的基本假设，为面向智能体的新一代基础设施提供了实证基础，特别是通过空闲时间预测实现主动资源编排。

Abstract: AI coding agents like GitHub Copilot, Claude Code, and Codex interleave multi-step LLM inference with tool execution, creating a workload different from chatbots. We present the first production-scale characterization of this workload using sampled GitHub Copilot traces from June 2026, comprising 3.2M users, 13M sessions, 761M LLM calls, and 95T tokens.
  Our analysis reveals distinctive workload properties with important systems implications. For example, agentic coding sessions consist of sparse user-initiated turns, each unfolding into an autonomous agent loop of LLM calls almost always coupled with tool execution. This structure yields KV cache hit rates averaging 90% within a turn, but falling to 55\% across turn boundaries and drastically invalidated after events like model switches or context compaction. Diverse workflows and user behaviors are observed with variable and long-tailed token consumption, time span, and tool calls. We highlight the difference between quick agentic turnaround times and the minutes-long user idle periods at turn boundaries, and design a lightweight idle-time predictor that captures 86-90\% of total idle time, enabling proactive decisions for efficient resource orchestration. These findings challenge assumptions underlying current LLM-serving systems and provide an empirical foundation for agent-native infrastructure.

</details>


### [122] [Where Reasoning Diverges: Localized Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01463)
*Weijun Gao,Xiang Ding,Tiancheng Xing,Haoyang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出局部多智能体辩论（LMAD），通过将智能体推理迹表示为类型化节点并定位最早冲突点，将辩论限制在局部片段，从而减少冗余推理并提升多跳问答的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多智能体辩论在意见分歧仅涉及少数中间声明时，仍交换完整的推理迹，造成计算浪费和噪声干扰，需要更精准的辩论机制。

Method: 提出LMAD协议：将智能体推理迹表示为类型化节点，定位最早冲突节点，仅针对对应局部片段进行辩论；同时引入受控扩展（Guarded Resolution）维护共享已提交状态，处理后续冲突时无需重新打开已接受的步骤。

Result: 在四个多跳问答基准上使用十个骨干模型（来自四个模型家族）评估，LMAD在宏平均裁判准确率上优于所有基线，相比最强常规基线最高提升7.20个百分点。

Conclusion: LMAD通过局部化冲突定位与受控状态扩展，显著提升了多智能体辩论的效率和准确性，且单一配置即可在多种模型上稳定取得最优表现。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate commonly exchanges complete reasoning traces even when disagreements concern only a few intermediate claims. We introduce Localized Multi-Agent Debate (LMAD), an inference-time protocol that represents agent traces as typed nodes, locates their earliest conflict, and restricts debate to the corresponding local segments. Guarded resolution extends a shared committed state so that later conflicts can be addressed without reopening accepted steps. We evaluate a single fixed LMAD configuration on four multi-hop question-answering benchmarks using ten backbones from four model families. Our method achieves the highest macro-averaged judge accuracy across all ten backbones, outperforming the strongest conventional baseline by up to 7.20 percentage points.

</details>


### [123] [Shared Organizational Memory for Enterprise Coding Agents: System Design and Deployment Snapshot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00122)
*Harsh Rao Dhanyamraju,Leonidas Raghav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个生产环境中部署的组织共享记忆系统，将知识捕获作为编码工作的平台级组成部分，自动收集任务相关经验、策展为可复用的问答记忆，并门控安全与隐私风险，供未来智能体检索使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业编码智能体依赖工具和检索，但企业知识（如内部DSL、专有平台、本地约定、近期修复、隐性工作流）往往不在公共训练数据或正式文档中。现有知识接口虽能暴露存储资源，但仍依赖智能体主动识别和显式记录值得复用的经验，导致捕获与编码工作流脱节，开发经验被反复重新发现。

Method: 构建了一个平台级组织共享记忆系统：在编码过程中收集与任务相关的经验（需贡献者批准），将其策展为可复用的问答型记忆，通过安全与隐私风险门控机制过滤，并供未来智能体检索使用。论文描述了该系统的部署生命周期和运行快照。

Result: 论文报告了生产环境的部署情况和操作快照，但对检索和编码任务的实际效果仍在评估中，尚未给出最终效果数据。

Conclusion: 该系统将知识捕获从显式记录转变为平台内生的编码工作流环节，有望减少企业开发中经验的重复发现成本；但有效性仍需进一步评估验证。

Abstract: Enterprise coding agents rely on tools and retrieval, yet enterprise knowledge often remains outside public training data and formal documentation: internal DSLs, proprietary platforms, local conventions, recent fixes, and tacit workflows. Existing knowledge interfaces expose stored resources but still depend on agents recognizing and explicitly recording lessons worth reusing, disconnecting capture from the coding workflow and leaving development experience repeatedly rediscovered. We report an ongoing production deployment of a shared organizational memory system that makes capture a platform-level part of coding work: it collects task-adjacent experience with contributor approval, curates it into reusable question-answer memories, gates obvious security and privacy risks, and retrieves memories for future agents. This short paper describes the deployed lifecycle and an operational snapshot. Effects on retrieval and coding tasks remain under evaluation.

</details>


### [124] [Securing Agentic AI: From Per-Action Checks to Trajectory Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01558)
*Alireza Lotfi,Subangkar Karmaker Shanto,Imtiaz Karim,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了大语言模型驱动的自主智能体在安全领域面临的跨层级挑战，涵盖单智能体、多智能体、行为约束以及供应链等层面，并提出安全应成为架构、协议和运行时的可验证属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体自主性增强并跨组织边界委派任务，其安全性不再取决于单次动作的正确性，而在于整体行为是否符合系统规则与不变量。现有安全方法多为引导性附加层，缺乏系统性的安全框架，亟需全面梳理各层级安全挑战并形成统一原则。

Method: 本文采用系统性分析的方法，从智能体技术栈的多个层级（单智能体层、多智能体层、执行控制层、行为约束层、供应链层）逐一识别安全攻击面与潜在漏洞，并归纳出统一的安全原则：安全必须是架构、协议和运行时中可验证的属性。

Result: 论文识别出各层级的具体安全挑战：单智能体层面包括提示注入、记忆污染、检索知识篡改和工具接口滥用；多智能体层面涉及身份、信任、能力控制和决策透明度问题；行为约束层面存在"单项允许但整体违规"的组合性风险；供应链层面则面临完整性、溯源、问责和端到端可观测性等开放问题。

Conclusion: 安全应内嵌为智能体架构、协议和运行时的可验证属性而非可选附加层，该框架为可信自主智能体部署提供了系统性路线图。
